## 论文列表

### 1. Will the User Ever Know? Covert Indirect Prompt Injection on Tool-Using LLM Agents

**内容概述：** 针对传统 ASR 只统计注入是否成功、却忽略用户能否从最终答复察觉攻击的问题，论文把 indirect prompt injection 成功拆为 covert 与 overt 两类并提出 ICoA，通过攻击后把 Agent 拉回原用户任务来掩盖痕迹，在四个模型上将 CSR 相对最强基线提高 3.79–12.01 个百分点。

- 收录理由：论文给出了 tool-using Agent 中用户不可见 IPI 的独立 threat model、指标分解和可复现攻击机制，直接补足只看 ASR 会低估隐蔽危害的评测缺口。
- 论文/arXiv：[Will the User Ever Know? Covert Indirect Prompt Injection on Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30362)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30362`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- 作者及单位：
  - Yunseok Lee — 单位未核实
  - Yunji Kim — 单位未核实
  - Woojin Lee — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：As LLM agents take real-world actions through tools, indirect prompt injection (IPI) has emerged as a serious threat. The standard metric, Attack Success Rate (ASR), counts whether an injection succeeds but ignores what the user notices in the agent's final response. Looking at successful injection traces, we find two distinct outcomes: the agent executes the injection while returning an otherwise normal response, or reports the injected action in its final response, giving the user a chance to notice. We call these covert and overt successes. From the user's perspective, we decompose ASR into the Covert Success Rate (CSR), counting successes leaving no trace in the final response, and the Overt Success Rate (OSR), counting successes the user can detect. To understand what drives the gap, we analyze successful trajectories and find that the agent's behavior after the injection separates covert from overt: covert traces hand control back to the user task before ending, while overt traces end at the attack itself. This split follows from the ReAct format, where the final response summarizes the most recent action. Building on this observation, we propose ICoA (Induced Covert Attack), an IPI attack designed to induce covert outcomes by steering the agent back to the user task after executing the injection. Across four target models on AgentDojo, ICoA achieves the highest CSR, with gains of 3.79-12.01 percentage points over the strongest baseline.
- 中文摘要：随着 LLM Agent 通过工具执行现实世界动作，indirect prompt injection（IPI）已成为严重威胁。标准指标 Attack Success Rate（ASR）统计注入是否成功，却忽略用户在 Agent 最终响应中能看到什么。通过考察成功注入的轨迹，我们发现两种不同结果：Agent 执行注入后返回一个在其他方面正常的响应，或者在最终响应中报告被注入的动作，从而让用户有机会察觉。我们把它们称为 covert success 和 overt success。从用户视角出发，我们把 ASR 分解为 Covert Success Rate（CSR）和 Overt Success Rate（OSR）：前者统计在最终响应中不留痕迹的成功，后者统计用户能够发现的成功。为了理解这种差异由何而来，我们分析成功轨迹，发现注入后的 Agent 行为区分了 covert 与 overt：covert 轨迹会在结束前把控制权交回用户任务，而 overt 轨迹则在攻击本身结束。这种分化源自 ReAct 格式，因为最终响应会概括最近一次动作。基于这一观察，我们提出 ICoA（Induced Covert Attack），这是一种通过在执行注入后引导 Agent 返回用户任务来诱导 covert 结果的 IPI 攻击。在 AgentDojo 上针对四个目标模型的实验中，ICoA 取得最高 CSR，相比最强基线提高 3.79–12.01 个百分点。

### 2. Reachability-Based Capability Confinement for LLM Agents under Indirect Prompt Injection

**内容概述：** 针对不可信 skill 输出进入状态后现有防御仍不改变 Agent 后续权限的问题，论文提出 harness-level enforcement 层 SkillGuard，以 Skill Impact Graph、steerability signature 和 inline reference monitor 计算污染后的 capability restriction，在 AgentDojo 上对三个套件消除 Tool Knowledge 攻击且不增加模型调用或 token 开销。

- 收录理由：论文把 skill 注入后的风险从内容分类提升为未来 authority 的运行时强制收缩，并以组合式攻击和 benign utility 验证 reference monitor，直接对应 skill security、IPI 与 capability confinement。
- 论文/arXiv：[Reachability-Based Capability Confinement for LLM Agents under Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30041)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30041`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供

  - Wujie Xiong — 单位未核实
  - Rabimba Karanjai — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：Large language model agents place outputs from external skills into their execution context, allowing attacker-controlled data to influence later privileged actions. Existing defenses mainly classify untrusted content or authorize proposed operations. They do not directly address how an agent's future authority should change once untrusted data enters its state. We present SkillGuard, a harness-level enforcement layer that treats this event as contamination and restricts future capabilities to disconnect the resulting state from deployer-defined forbidden states. Given sound skill summaries and policies, SkillGuard represents security-relevant transitions with a Skill Impact Graph, specifies admissible control over skill parameters via steerability signatures, and mediates invocations with an inline reference monitor. Following contamination, it computes weighted capability restrictions using binary, fractional, or fractional-flow strategies without auxiliary language-model inference. We evaluate SkillGuard on four AgentDojo suites with two backend LLMs, Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama3.3-70B, against an LLM-only No Defense baseline and three defenses at different system layers: Spotlighting, CaMeL, and AttriGuard. We construct a compositional attack benchmark in which each attack combines observations individually insufficient to induce target violation and evaluate the same baselines on it. Under AgentDojo's Tool Knowledge attacks, SkillGuard eliminates attack success on three of four suites for both backends and reduces it to 4.8% and 14.3% on Slack. Against compositional attacks, it outperforms every baseline on Llama and matches the strongest baseline on Gemini at higher benign utility. Fractional-flow restriction preserves substantially more capabilities than binary restriction at the same attack success rate. Across both settings, SkillGuard adds no model calls or token overhead.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型 Agent 会把外部 skill 的输出放入执行上下文，使攻击者控制的数据能够影响后续特权动作。现有防御主要对不可信内容进行分类，或对已经提出的操作作授权判断；它们没有直接处理这样一个问题：一旦不可信数据进入 Agent 状态，其未来权限应如何改变。我们提出 SkillGuard，一个 harness-level enforcement 层，它把这一事件视为污染，并通过限制未来 capability，使由此产生的状态与部署者定义的 forbidden state 断开。给定可靠的 skill summary 和 policy，SkillGuard 使用 Skill Impact Graph 表示安全相关的状态转换，通过 steerability signature 指定对 skill 参数可接受的控制，并用 inline reference monitor 仲裁调用。污染发生后，它无需辅助语言模型推理，即可使用 binary、fractional 或 fractional-flow 策略计算加权 capability restriction。我们在四个 AgentDojo 套件上，以 Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Llama3.3-70B 两个后端 LLM 评估 SkillGuard，并与仅使用 LLM 的 No Defense 基线以及位于不同系统层的 Spotlighting、CaMeL 和 AttriGuard 三种防御比较。我们还构建组合式攻击 benchmark，其中每次攻击组合多个单独不足以诱发目标违规的 observation，并在其上评估相同基线。在 AgentDojo 的 Tool Knowledge 攻击下，SkillGuard 对两个后端都在四个套件中的三个上消除了攻击成功，并在 Slack 上把攻击成功率降至 4.8% 和 14.3%。面对组合式攻击，它在 Llama 上优于全部基线，并在具有更高 benign utility 的情况下于 Gemini 上追平最强基线。在相同攻击成功率下，fractional-flow restriction 比 binary restriction 保留了多得多的 capability。在两种设置中，SkillGuard 都不增加模型调用或 token 开销。

### 3. SIR: Self-improving Red-teaming for Compute Use Agents

**内容概述：** 针对固定手写注入会低估 adaptive attacker 对 computer-use Agent 的风险，论文提出黑盒攻击 SIR，用可复用原则组合隐蔽注入并从失败轨迹持续提炼新 bypass strategy，以确定性系统状态 oracle 评测后把 Claude Opus 4.8 和 Gemini 3.5 Flash 的 ASR 分别由 4% 提至 24%、由 0% 提至 28%。

- 收录理由：论文面向拥有鼠标、键盘和终端权限的 CUA，建立自改进 IPI red-teaming 闭环并验证跨模型迁移，安全后果与评测证据均落在真实操作系统状态而非 LLM judge。
- 论文/arXiv：[SIR: Self-improving Red-teaming for Compute Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30207)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30207`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Chen Xiong — 单位未核实
  - Zhiyuan He — 单位未核实
  - Pin-Yu Chen — 单位未核实
  - Stjepan Picek — 单位未核实
  - Tsung-Yi Ho — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Computer use agents (CUAs) are vision-language models that perceive a screen and act on a real operating system through mouse, keyboard, and terminal, and they are increasingly deployed to automate everyday digital tasks. Because they can be exposed to untrusted content while operating, they are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which an adversary plants instructions in content the agent will read and redirects it toward actions that violate the user's intent. Existing CUA safety benchmarks evaluate fixed injections written by hand, which may underestimate the risk posed by an adaptive adversary. We present SIR, a black box IPI attack that (i) composes stealthy injections from a small library of reusable principles stated in plain language and (ii) wraps composition in an iterative feedback loop that diagnoses the victim's failed trajectories and distills the bypasses into new, named strategies that are reapplied across tasks. Unlike prior red teaming of web agents, we target CUAs at the operating system level and score attacks with a fully deterministic oracle, using checks on filesystem, service, and permission state rather than an LLM judge. On experiment, we evaluate three frontier CUAs. Composing principles with feedback raises the attack success rate over a baseline written by hand, for example from 4% to 24% on Claude Opus 4.8 and from 0% to 28% on Gemini 3.5 Flash, while the benign task still completes. Principles discovered against one model further transfer to a different architecture with no additional feedback.
- 中文摘要：Computer use Agent（CUA）是能够感知屏幕并通过鼠标、键盘和终端在真实操作系统上行动的视觉语言模型，正越来越多地被部署来自动完成日常数字任务。由于它们在操作时可能接触不可信内容，因此容易受到 indirect prompt injection（IPI）攻击：攻击者把指令植入 Agent 将要读取的内容，并将其重定向至违反用户意图的动作。现有 CUA 安全 benchmark 评估人工编写的固定注入，可能低估 adaptive adversary 带来的风险。我们提出 SIR，一种黑盒 IPI 攻击：（i）从以自然语言陈述的少量可复用原则库中组合隐蔽注入；（ii）在组合过程外加入迭代反馈循环，诊断 victim 的失败轨迹，把 bypass 提炼成新的具名策略，并跨任务再次应用。不同于此前针对 Web Agent 的 red teaming，我们以操作系统层面的 CUA 为目标，并使用完全确定性的 oracle 评分；该 oracle 检查文件系统、服务和权限状态，而不是使用 LLM judge。在实验中，我们评估了三个 frontier CUA。结合原则组合与反馈后，攻击成功率高于手写基线，例如在 Claude Opus 4.8 上从 4% 升至 24%，在 Gemini 3.5 Flash 上从 0% 升至 28%，同时 benign task 仍然完成。针对一个模型发现的原则还可在没有额外反馈的情况下迁移至不同架构。

### 4. Lazy Grounding: Attacking Search Agents with Factual Evidence

**内容概述：** 针对 search Agent 只防虚假或恶意文档、却可能错误套用真实证据的问题，论文提出 lazy grounding 攻击，用相邻改写问题的事实性证据干扰原问题，在 12 个模型—benchmark 组合上令准确率平均下降 5.9 个百分点、最高下降 17.3 个百分点。

- 收录理由：论文把 RAG／search poisoning 的 threat model 扩展到“证据真实但不回答当前问题”，并量化证据位置和 answer-shaped 形式对误用的影响，属于 evidence integrity 的新型攻击面。
- 论文/arXiv：[Lazy Grounding: Attacking Search Agents with Factual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30303)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30303`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/frankyzha/lazy-grounding)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference). Code: https://github.com/frankyzha/lazy-grounding
- 作者及单位：
  - Yulin Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Yukun Huang — 单位未核实
  - Sanxing Chen — 单位未核实
  - Tianyi Lin — 单位未核实
  - Ziang Yang — 单位未核实
  - Xunjian Yin — 单位未核实
  - Bhuwan Dhingra — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Search agents reduce hallucination by grounding answers in retrieved web evidence. Yet reliance on retrieval also creates an attack surface: poisoned corpora with false or malicious documents can cause agents to reproduce misinformation. We show that falsehood is not necessary -- a search agent can be misled by factual evidence for a nearby question, adopting that nearby answer even when it does not answer the current question. We call this failure lazy grounding. We expose lazy grounding using nearby evidence from answer-changing rewrites of benchmark questions. Each document truthfully supports a neighboring rewritten question, but is surfaced for the original question. Across 12 model-benchmark pairs, nearby evidence reduces accuracy by 5.9 points on average and by up to 17.3 points, while inducing nearby-answer adoption in every setting. The effect is stronger when nearby evidence appears later or is more answer-shaped. Our results show that robust search agents must defend against not only misinformation but also the misapplication of factual evidence. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/frankyzha/lazy-grounding.
- 中文摘要：Search Agent 通过把回答建立在检索到的 Web 证据上来减少 hallucination。然而，对检索的依赖也形成了攻击面：含有虚假或恶意文档的 poisoned corpus 会使 Agent 复述错误信息。我们表明，攻击不需要虚假内容：针对相近问题的事实证据也能误导 search Agent，使其采纳相近问题的答案，即使该答案没有回答当前问题。我们把这种失效称为 lazy grounding。我们利用 benchmark 问题经答案改变式改写后得到的 nearby evidence 来暴露 lazy grounding。每份文档都真实支持一个相邻的改写问题，却被呈现给原始问题。在 12 个模型—benchmark 组合中，nearby evidence 使准确率平均下降 5.9 个百分点，最高下降 17.3 个百分点，并在所有设置中诱发对 nearby answer 的采纳。当 nearby evidence 出现得更晚或更像一个直接答案时，影响更强。结果表明，稳健的 search Agent 不仅要防御 misinformation，还要防御对事实证据的错误套用。代码已公开于 https://github.com/frankyzha/lazy-grounding。

### 5. Extracting Knowledge from Tools in LLM Agents

**内容概述：** 针对 Agent 为合法响应访问知识型工具时可被逐步反推出底层文件、数据库或索引的问题，论文提出 query-only 攻击 ToolSiphon，以 Tool Contrastive Analysis 稳定选择目标工具、用 Evidence Chained Feedback 扩大抽取覆盖，平均恢复 74.3% 的源记录且能绕过代表性防御。

- 收录理由：论文明确刻画 tool-selection uncertainty 与 tool-argument compression 两个抽取障碍，并在三类工具、六个数据集和三个真实 Agent 平台上验证知识源重建，直接涉及 tool-mediated data extraction。
- 论文/arXiv：[Extracting Knowledge from Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30288)

- 英文摘要：LLM agents commonly use knowledge-based tools and access their underlying files, databases, and search indexes through tool invocation. This integration improves agents' ability to provide domain-specific services but also introduces the risk of tool-mediated knowledge extraction: source content exposed to an agent for legitimate responses may be progressively recovered from its outputs, enabling reconstruction of the knowledge source behind a target tool. This paper systematically investigates this risk and identifies two challenges introduced by tool invocation: tool-selection uncertainty, where an agent may invoke a competing tool instead of the target tool, and tool-argument compression, where fine-grained query information may be lost when the agent generates tool arguments. To tackle these challenges, we propose ToolSiphon, a query-only extraction attack that introduces two complementary signals: a target-discriminative signal, implemented through Tool Contrastive Analysis, to steer queries toward the target tool; and a response-grounded factual signal, implemented through Evidence Chained Feedback, to mitigate argument compression and progressively expand extraction coverage. Across three types of knowledge-based tools and six domain-specific datasets, ToolSiphon recovers 74.3% of source records on average when coarse-grained information about non-target tools is available, with 83.2% textual recovery and 90.2% semantic similarity. Even without such information, it recovers 66.3% of source records. ToolSiphon also remains effective against representative defenses and on three real-world agent platforms.
- 中文摘要：LLM Agent 通常会调用知识型工具，并通过工具调用访问其底层文件、数据库和搜索索引。这种集成增强了 Agent 提供领域特定服务的能力，但也带来 tool-mediated knowledge extraction 风险：为生成合法响应而暴露给 Agent 的源内容，可能从其输出中被逐步恢复，从而重建目标工具背后的知识源。本文系统研究这一风险，并识别出工具调用带来的两个挑战：tool-selection uncertainty，即 Agent 可能调用竞争工具而非目标工具；以及 tool-argument compression，即 Agent 生成工具参数时可能丢失细粒度查询信息。为解决这些挑战，我们提出 ToolSiphon，一种 query-only extraction attack，它引入两个互补信号：一是通过 Tool Contrastive Analysis 实现的 target-discriminative signal，用于把查询引向目标工具；二是通过 Evidence Chained Feedback 实现的 response-grounded factual signal，用于缓解参数压缩并逐步扩大抽取覆盖。在三类知识型工具和六个领域特定数据集上，当可获得关于非目标工具的粗粒度信息时，ToolSiphon 平均恢复 74.3% 的源记录，文本恢复率为 83.2%，语义相似度为 90.2%。即使没有此类信息，它仍能恢复 66.3% 的源记录。ToolSiphon 对代表性防御和三个真实 Agent 平台也保持有效。

### 6. Understanding Stage-Wise Utility-Risk Trade-offs in LLM Agent Memory

**内容概述：** 针对长期 Agent memory 的 utility 与 poisoning risk 通常被分开、且只在固定配置下评估的问题，论文提出 MemGauge，分别控制写入准入、管理策略和检索暴露，在 11 个 LLM 上发现写入阶段存在阈值式风险转变、管理阶段可局部解耦、检索阶段效用与风险耦合增长。

- 收录理由：论文直接评测 memory poisoning，并把污染风险定位到 write、management、retrieval 三个可干预阶段，为 memory pipeline 的分阶段准入和暴露控制提供证据。
- 论文/arXiv：[Understanding Stage-Wise Utility-Risk Trade-offs in LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30177)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30177`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 英文摘要：Long-term memory is becoming a core capability of LLM agents, enabling personalization and long-horizon interaction. However, memory mechanisms that retain, transform, or expose more information can affect both benign utility and susceptibility to memory poisoning. Existing evaluations typically measure memory utility or attack risk in isolation under fixed configurations, providing limited insight into how stage-specific design choices reshape their trade-off. We present \textsc{MemGauge}, a controllable framework that separately varies writing admission, management policy, and retrieval exposure under matched clean and poisoned conditions. Across 11 LLMs and two long-term memory benchmarks, controlled evaluations reveal three distinct profiles: a threshold-like risk transition during writing, policy-dependent local decoupling during management, and coupled growth of utility and risk during retrieval. We further apply analogous stage-level measurements to four existing memory systems and observe diagnostic associations qualitatively consistent with these profiles. These results show that targeted poisoning risk varies across memory operations and motivate stage-aware evaluation and control of LLM-agent memory.
- 中文摘要：长期记忆正成为 LLM Agent 的核心能力，使其能够进行个性化和长时交互。然而，保留、转换或暴露更多信息的记忆机制，既会影响 benign utility，也会影响系统对 memory poisoning 的易感性。现有评估通常在固定配置下孤立测量 memory utility 或 attack risk，因而难以理解特定阶段的设计选择如何重塑二者权衡。我们提出 \textsc{MemGauge}，一个可控框架，在匹配的 clean 和 poisoned 条件下分别改变 writing admission、management policy 和 retrieval exposure。对 11 个 LLM 和两个长期记忆 benchmark 的受控评估揭示出三种不同画像：写入期间出现阈值式风险转变，管理期间出现依赖策略的局部解耦，检索期间则出现 utility 与 risk 的耦合增长。我们进一步对四个现有 memory system 采用类似的阶段级测量，观察到与这些画像在定性上一致的诊断关联。结果表明，定向 poisoning risk 会随 memory operation 而变化，并说明需要对 LLM Agent memory 进行 stage-aware evaluation 与控制。

### 7. Safe to Resume? Breaking Execution Continuity of Agent Execution via Rollback

**内容概述：** 针对 Agent checkpoint 正确恢复内部状态却可能与外部 effect 形成从未真实共存的执行历史，论文建立 C/R 安全模型、归纳五类恢复失效并在 Hermes、Cline、LangGraph 上实现恶意软件验证绕过、未授权邮件转发和重复支付，且五个框架均复现这一系统性缺口。

- 收录理由：论文首次把 persistent Agent 的 rollback 从可靠性机制转化为可利用的 execution-continuity threat model，并用真实外部 effect 验证 integrity 与 authorization 后果。
- 论文/arXiv：[Safe to Resume? Breaking Execution Continuity of Agent Execution via Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29381)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29381`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`

- 作者及单位：
  - Guanlong Wu — 单位未核实
  - Dahui Li — 单位未核实
  - Ke Jiang — 单位未核实
  - Jianyu Niu — 单位未核实
  - Cong Wang — 单位未核实
  - Yinqian Zhang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：AI agents are moving toward persistent, stateful execution across various applications, accumulating execution state and external effects that are costly to reconstruct after failures. Checkpoint and rollback (C/R) are becoming essential for recovery, yet their security implications remain largely unexplored. Correct rollback does not imply secure recovery: a faithfully restored checkpoint may resume an execution whose states, assumptions, and external effects never coexisted in any valid history. In this paper, we present the first systematic security study of checkpoint and rollback in existing agent systems. By examining representative agent C/R systems, we characterize the design space of existing C/R mechanisms and develop a general execution model that captures their recovery boundaries and state dependencies. From this model, we identify five fundamental failure modes spanning incomplete or inconsistent internal state, stale external dependencies, nondeterministic replay, and unrecorded external effects. We further demonstrate their security impact through three end-to-end attacks on Hermes, Cline, and LangGraph, enabling malware-verification bypass, unauthorized mail forwarding, and double payment. To systematically study these failures in practice, we develop a multi-agent analysis pipeline that reconstructs execution semantics, identifies violations of the five failure conditions, and validates them through actual rollback. Across five representative frameworks, our evaluation shows that these failures recur across heterogeneous C/R designs and stem from a common gap between the state restored by a checkpoint and the dependencies required for secure continuation.
- 中文摘要：AI Agent 正走向跨多种应用的持久、有状态执行，并积累在失败后重建成本很高的执行状态和外部 effect。Checkpoint and rollback（C/R）因而成为恢复的关键，但其安全影响仍基本未被探索。正确 rollback 并不意味着安全恢复：一个被忠实还原的 checkpoint 可能恢复某次执行，而其中的状态、假设和外部 effect 从未在任何有效历史中同时存在。本文首次对现有 Agent 系统中的 checkpoint and rollback 开展系统性安全研究。通过考察代表性 Agent C/R 系统，我们刻画现有 C/R 机制的设计空间，并建立一个捕获其恢复边界和状态依赖的一般执行模型。由此模型，我们识别出五种基础失效模式，覆盖不完整或不一致的内部状态、过时的外部依赖、非确定性 replay 和未记录的外部 effect。我们进一步在 Hermes、Cline 和 LangGraph 上通过三种端到端攻击展示其安全影响，使恶意软件验证绕过、未授权邮件转发和重复支付成为可能。为系统研究这些实际失效，我们开发了一个 multi-agent 分析 pipeline，用于重建执行语义、识别对五种失效条件的违反，并通过实际 rollback 加以验证。对五个代表性框架的评估显示，这些失效在不同 C/R 设计中反复出现，源于 checkpoint 所恢复的状态与安全继续执行所需依赖之间的共同缺口。

### 8. Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs

**内容概述：** 针对 multi-agent handoff 压缩会保留操作事实却削弱用途边界的问题，论文定义 summary collapse 并测量 boundary marker 存活率，发现 25 词预算将其由约 0.80 降至约 0.57、模糊边界导致最高 73% 泄漏，而显式 audience allowlist 几乎消除泄漏。

- 收录理由：论文揭示多 Agent 状态交接中的 privacy boundary 丢失不是简单的信息损耗，并比较 prompt、redaction 与结构化 audience control，为跨 Agent 信息流 enforcement 提供直接证据。
- 论文/arXiv：[Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29028)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29028`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Yian Wang — 单位未核实
  - Agam Goyal — 单位未核实
  - Eshwar Chandrasekharan — 单位未核实
  - Hari Sundaram — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Multi-agent LLM systems often coordinate by compressing an upstream interaction into a handoff artifact that downstream agents treat as shared state. We show that this handoff step is a structural source of privacy leakage: summaries preferentially preserve operational facts while weakening the boundary metadata that governs how those facts may be used---a failure mode we call \emph{summary collapse}. On a controlled multi-agent coordination testbed we measure marker survival with a human-validated judge ($κ= 0.74$), where $σ_b = 1$ means every boundary marker survives verbatim and $σ_b = 0$ means all are lost. Boundary-marker and operational-fact survival are nearly uncorrelated at the handoff level on both GPT-5-mini and DeepSeek-R1-32B (Pearson $r$ near zero): uncompressed free-text handoffs preserve boundaries at $σ_b \approx 0.80$, whereas a $25$-word budget drops $σ_b$ to ${\approx}0.57$ while operational-fact survival stays near ceiling. Controlled downstream tests reveal that protection depends on \emph{boundary explicitness}: vague languages leak in $73\%$ of GPT and $50\%$ of DeepSeek cases, while explicit constraints reduce leakage to under $15\%$ across all three tested models. A no-handoff single-agent control further shows the failure is not reducible to multi-agent topology as direct full-marker access still leaks more often than the operationalized handoff. Prompt-only mitigation and exact-string redaction only partially address the problem, while a gold-derived audience allowlist nearly eliminates leakage across models, showing that correctly identifying audience boundaries is the key factor.
- 中文摘要：Multi-agent LLM 系统通常把上游交互压缩成交接 artifact，并由下游 Agent 将其视为共享状态。我们表明，这一 handoff 步骤是隐私泄漏的结构性来源：摘要会优先保留操作事实，却削弱规定这些事实可如何使用的 boundary metadata；我们把这种失效称为 summary collapse。在一个受控的 multi-agent 协作 testbed 上，我们使用经人工验证的 judge（$κ=0.74$）测量 marker survival，其中 $σ_b=1$ 表示每个 boundary marker 都逐字保留，$σ_b=0$ 表示全部丢失。在 GPT-5-mini 和 DeepSeek-R1-32B 上，boundary marker 与 operational fact 在 handoff 层面的存活率几乎不相关（Pearson $r$ 接近零）：未压缩的自由文本 handoff 以 $σ_b \approx 0.80$ 保留边界，而 25 词预算使 $σ_b$ 降至约 0.57，同时 operational fact 的存活率仍接近上限。受控下游测试表明，保护效果取决于 boundary explicitness：模糊语言在 GPT 和 DeepSeek 中分别造成 73% 和 50% 的泄漏，而显式约束使三个测试模型的泄漏率均降至 15% 以下。无 handoff 的单 Agent control 进一步说明，该失效不能归结为 multi-agent topology，因为直接访问完整 marker 时仍比经过 operationalized handoff 更容易泄漏。仅依靠 prompt 的缓解和精确字符串 redaction 都只能部分解决问题，而由 gold 标注派生的 audience allowlist 几乎在所有模型上消除泄漏，说明正确识别 audience boundary 是关键因素。

### 9. When Errors Become Memories: Causal Pathway Tracing in Multi-Turn Memory-Augmented LLMs

**内容概述：** 针对 memory-augmented LLM 的局部错误如何跨响应、记忆状态和未来交互持久传播尚不清楚，论文以结构因果模型分离 memory update 与 question feedback 两条路径并构造反事实轨迹，发现前者影响更持久，Joint Repair 可将残余传播降低 98.3%。

- 收录理由：论文虽不以恶意 poisoning 为唯一来源，但直接分析错误写入 memory 后的持久完整性风险、潜伏状态和因果修复位置，与 Agent memory 污染机制高度相关。
- 论文/arXiv：[When Errors Become Memories: Causal Pathway Tracing in Multi-Turn Memory-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30198)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30198`

- 英文摘要：Long-term memory enables large language models (LLMs) to preserve and reuse information across interactions, but it can also turn localized errors into persistent risks. Existing work mainly evaluates whether memory systems store and retrieve information correctly, leaving limited understanding of how errors propagate across responses, memory states, and future interactions. We propose a structural causal model (SCM)-based framework for cross-turn error propagation in memory-augmented LLMs. We model user questions, model responses, and memory states as a dynamic causal process, and identify two entry pathways: internal memory updating and external question feedback. By intervening on these pathways, we construct four counterfactual trajectories and quantify their downstream effects and interaction. Error influence is evaluated at four levels: memory retention, natural responses, targeted diagnostic probing, and probability-level error preference. Experiments show that error influence generally decays with interaction distance, while the memory-update pathway contributes more persistent effects than question feedback; latent errors may remain even after disappearing from natural responses. Propagation patterns also vary across memory categories and memory mechanisms. Pathway-guided restoration further validates this decomposition: Question Repair reduces residual error by 27.5%, Memory Repair by 70.2%, and Joint Repair by 98.3%, nearly eliminating residual propagation.
- 中文摘要：长期记忆使大型语言模型（LLM）能够跨交互保存并复用信息，但也可能把局部错误转化为持久风险。现有工作主要评估 memory system 是否正确存储和检索信息，对于错误如何跨响应、记忆状态和未来交互传播，理解仍然有限。我们提出一个基于 structural causal model（SCM）的框架，用于研究 memory-augmented LLM 中的跨轮错误传播。我们把用户问题、模型响应和记忆状态建模为动态因果过程，并识别两条进入路径：内部 memory updating 与外部 question feedback。通过干预这些路径，我们构造四条反事实轨迹，并量化其下游效应和交互。错误影响在四个层面评估：memory retention、自然响应、定向诊断 probing 和概率层面的错误偏好。实验表明，错误影响通常会随交互距离衰减，但 memory-update 路径比 question feedback 产生更持久的作用；潜在错误即使从自然响应中消失，也可能继续存在。传播模式还会随 memory category 和 memory mechanism 而变化。Pathway-guided restoration 进一步验证了这一分解：Question Repair 将残余错误降低 27.5%，Memory Repair 降低 70.2%，Joint Repair 降低 98.3%，几乎消除残余传播。

### 10. Influence Is Not Authority: When Causal Guardrail Signals Make Legitimate Tool Use Look Like an Attack in Tool-Using LLM Agents

**内容概述：** 针对 influence-based guardrail 把“外部信息影响动作”等同于“动作未获授权”的问题，论文进行 96 条件 authorization-equivalence audit，发现仅把合法值来源从用户移到工具就会在全部 24 个配对案例中把因果信号推向攻击区域，且该偏移大于真正改变授权产生的平均偏移。

- 收录理由：论文直接检验 Agent guardrail 的 authorization validity，说明 causal influence signal 不能替代 prompt authority，并量化错误路由如何同时伤害 benign utility 和攻击阻断。
- 论文/arXiv：[Influence Is Not Authority: When Causal Guardrail Signals Make Legitimate Tool Use Look Like an Attack in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29942)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29942`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`

  - Tanzim Ahad — 单位未核实
  - Ismail Hossain — 单位未核实
  - Md Jahangir Alam — 单位未核实
  - Sai Puppala — 单位未核实
  - Syed Bahauddin Alam — 单位未核实
  - Sajedul Talukder — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：The key limitation of current state-of-the-art influence-based guardrails is that they do not reliably distinguish a legitimate, user-authorized action from a malicious, unauthorized action when both rely on external tool information. This ambiguity can cause benign actions to trigger unnecessary verification and intervention, reducing utility and adding latency. We expose this limitation through an authorization-equivalence audit of 96 conditions derived from 24 base cases. Within matched source comparisons, we hold authorization, the exact committed action, and its intended effect fixed, changing only whether a required value comes from the user or a legitimate tool result. Although the action remains unchanged, this harmless relocation shifts the causal signal toward the attack region in all 24 cases under both Llama and Gemma scorers. Matched unauthorized controls show that the signal remains attack-sensitive, yet the benign relocation produces a larger average score shift than the actual change in authorization. Architecture-level evaluation shows how this mismatch propagates through guardrail designs. With a semantic monitor, attack success is 0% and utility is 28%, compared with 16% and 60% without it. A shadow-based guardrail allows every tested harmless run, yet does not reject matched unauthorized actions more often overall: 57.5% of unauthorized runs pass automatically before reaching the later security check, compared with 29.2% of authorized runs. These results show that the studied causal signal reveals what shaped an action without reliably encoding whether the action was authorized, and that reference construction and routing are integral to the effective security decision.
- 中文摘要：当前最先进的 influence-based guardrail 的关键局限在于：当合法、用户授权的动作与恶意、未授权的动作都依赖外部工具信息时，它们无法可靠地区分二者。这种歧义会使 benign action 触发不必要的验证和干预，从而降低 utility 并增加延迟。我们通过一项 authorization-equivalence audit 揭示这一局限；该审计包含从 24 个基础案例派生的 96 个条件。在匹配的来源比较中，我们固定 authorization、准确的已提交动作及其预期效果，只改变一个必要值是来自用户还是合法工具结果。尽管动作保持不变，这种无害的来源移动在使用 Llama 和 Gemma scorer 时都使全部 24 个案例中的 causal signal 向攻击区域偏移。匹配的未授权 control 表明，该信号仍对攻击敏感，但 benign relocation 造成的平均分数偏移大于 authorization 真正改变造成的偏移。架构层评估展示这种不匹配如何沿 guardrail 设计传播。使用 semantic monitor 时，攻击成功率为 0%、utility 为 28%；不使用时则分别为 16% 和 60%。Shadow-based guardrail 允许所有测试过的无害运行通过，却没有在总体上更频繁地拒绝匹配的未授权动作：57.5% 的未授权运行在到达后续安全检查前自动通过，而授权运行的比例为 29.2%。结果表明，所研究的 causal signal 能揭示是什么塑造了一个动作，却不能可靠编码该动作是否获得授权；reference construction 和 routing 是实际安全决策不可分割的一部分。

### 11. CAST: Critique-Aware Supervision for Training Reliable Long-Horizon Tool-Calling Agents

**内容概述：** 针对长轨迹 Agent 的错误动作可能不可逆、而优化方法缺少细粒度验证 rationale 监督的问题，论文提出 CAST，把稀疏任务结果转为 action-level critique supervision，并在动态 tool-calling benchmark 上令 Qwen3 系模型在 Retail 的 pass^4 超过 GPT-OSS-120B 逾 10%、在域外 Telehealth 再提高 9%。

- 收录理由：论文围绕执行前拦截错误 refund 等外部 effect，将 trajectory critique 转成 policy training 信号，直接服务于长时 Agent 的 step-level verification 与运行时安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[CAST: Critique-Aware Supervision for Training Reliable Long-Horizon Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30147)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30147`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 作者及单位：
  - Amir Saeidi — 单位未核实
  - Zehua Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Rishitosh Singh — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in long-horizon, interactive, and stateful environments. In these settings, a single wrong action, such as refunding the wrong purchase, can cause irreversible task failure and must be intercepted before execution. Such failures may not appear in every single run, but can emerge across repeated trials, making reliability across steps and trials critical. However, ensuring agentic reliability is challenging: even frontier LLMs struggle to explain why an action may be wrong, especially in long, intertwined trajectories governed by domain-specific policies. Much recent work relies on prompt-based critique agents, while optimization-based methods lack a systematic way to produce rich verification rationales for training. We address this gap with CAST, a critique-aware training framework that converts sparse task outcomes into action-level supervision for critique learning and policy optimization. CAST analyzes agent trajectories to synthesize structured rationales explaining action validity under partial observability. The resulting critique model is used to construct critique-aware training data for optimizing the policy model. Fine-tuning Qwen3-family models on dynamic tool-calling benchmarks, CAST improves reliability across domains, outperforming GPT-OSS-120B by over 10% pass^4 on Retail tasks and yielding an additional 9% improvement on Telehealth in an out-of-domain setting. These results demonstrate that critique-aware training improves the robustness of LLM agents in realistic dynamic environments.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）Agent 正越来越多地部署在长时、交互式且有状态的环境中。在这些环境中，一个错误动作，例如对错误的购买项目退款，就可能导致不可逆的任务失败，因此必须在执行前拦截。这类失败不一定在每次运行中都出现，却可能在重复试验中浮现，使跨步骤和跨试验的可靠性至关重要。然而，保证 Agent 可靠性很困难：即使 frontier LLM 也很难解释一个动作为什么可能错误，尤其是在受领域特定 policy 约束、相互交织的长轨迹中。近期许多工作依赖基于 prompt 的 critique Agent，而基于优化的方法缺少系统生成丰富 verification rationale 以供训练的方式。我们用 CAST 填补这一缺口；CAST 是一个 critique-aware training 框架，把稀疏任务结果转化为用于 critique learning 和 policy optimization 的 action-level supervision。CAST 分析 Agent 轨迹，合成结构化 rationale，解释 partial observability 下动作的有效性。所得 critique model 用于构建 critique-aware training data，再优化 policy model。在动态 tool-calling benchmark 上微调 Qwen3 系列模型后，CAST 提高了跨领域可靠性：Retail 任务上的 pass^4 比 GPT-OSS-120B 高出 10% 以上，并在域外 Telehealth 设置中额外提高 9%。结果表明，critique-aware training 能提升 LLM Agent 在真实动态环境中的稳健性。

### 12. Source-Dependent Deference in Medical Imaging Agents Under Falsified Findings: A Pilot Audit

**内容概述：** 针对医疗影像 Agent 在工具返回伪造 finding 后会否放弃原本正确判断缺乏测量，论文比较自调用工具 JSON 与归因给放射科医生的 prose 两种投递方式，最强模型层级在后者下改错 10/13、前者仅 1/13，但实验未分离 source label 与 delivery channel。

- 收录理由：论文以 falsified evidence 为攻击输入，直接审计高风险 tool-using Agent 的 source-dependent deference，并明确披露小样本与混杂边界。

- 英文摘要：Tool-using agents are being proposed for medical imaging, and their behaviour when a tool returns a false finding is largely unmeasured. We audit whether a ReAct-style tool-calling agent abandons an answer it has already given correctly once a falsified finding arrives, and whether that depends on how the finding is presented. On 20 VQA-RAD closed questions across four vendor-designated model tiers, the agent commits to an answer from the image alone; a negated finding is then delivered either as JSON from an analyze_image tool the agent invokes itself, or as quoted prose attributed to a radiologist. Our outcome is the commission-error rate over cases answered correctly without any tool. Deference is much higher under the prose-attributed claim: at the strongest tier the agent revised its correct answer in 10 of 13 cases against 1 of 13 under the tool (exact McNemar p=0.0039, Holm-adjusted 0.012). We do not claim this isolates the source label. Attribution travels with the delivery channel in our design, and exposure differs because the tool claim reaches the agent only when it calls the tool. The finding is a joint source-and-delivery asymmetry from a small-scale pilot whose pre-specified stopping rule was not met.
- 中文摘要：Tool-using Agent 正被建议用于医疗影像，但当工具返回虚假 finding 时，它们的行为几乎没有得到测量。我们审计这样一个问题：当伪造 finding 到达后，ReAct 风格的 tool-calling Agent 是否会放弃此前已经正确给出的答案，以及这是否取决于 finding 的呈现方式。在 20 个 VQA-RAD 封闭式问题和供应商指定的四个模型层级上，Agent 先仅依据图像承诺一个答案；随后，一个否定该答案的 finding 以两种形式之一送达：Agent 自己调用的 analyze_image 工具所返回的 JSON，或归因于放射科医生的引述 prose。我们的结果指标是在不使用任何工具时回答正确的案例中所出现的 commission-error rate。对归因给医生的 prose claim，Agent 的服从程度高得多：在最强层级，Agent 对 13 个案例中的 10 个修改了正确答案，而工具条件下为 1/13（精确 McNemar $p=0.0039$，Holm 校正后为 0.012）。我们不声称这单独分离了 source label。在我们的设计中，归因信息与 delivery channel 一同变化，而且 exposure 也不同，因为只有 Agent 调用工具时，工具 claim 才会送达。该发现是一个小规模 pilot 中 source 与 delivery 的联合不对称性，且该 pilot 没有达到预先规定的停止规则。

### 13. Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records

**内容概述：** 针对医疗文档抽取的似真 LLM 输出若写入长期健康记录会累积完整性风险，论文提出由确定性 monitor 执行的 evidence gate，要求唯一支持引文、同一化验行和完整 provenance；22 项测试全部通过，97 个数值候选中仅 72 个获准复用、25 个保留人工复核。

- 收录理由：论文把生成内容与授权其下游持久使用的权限分离，用不可自批的 deterministic gate 阻断无证据数据进入 longitudinal record，属于高风险 evidence integrity enforcement。
- 论文/arXiv：[Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29965)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29965`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 作者及单位：
  - Nora Girda — 单位未核实
  - Adrian Groza — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models can convert medical documents into structured data, but plausible output may still be unsupported by the source. Persisting such output in a longitudinal health record, a record that accumulates patient information over time, therefore creates an integrity risk: unverified data may influence later summaries, trends, or preventive-care computations. We introduce an evidence-gated trust-promotion model that keeps generated data provisional until a deterministic monitor verifies it against the source document. The monitor admits a candidate for a specified downstream use only when the source contains a unique supporting quotation, the relevant fields occur within the same laboratory row, and the required provenance is preserved. The generator cannot approve its own output, missing or ambiguous evidence causes refusal, and refused candidates remain available for human review rather than being silently discarded. We implement the model in Medical DataCloud, a personal health-record application, and evaluate it through automated tests and a replay of saved extraction outputs. All 22 conformance and mutation tests pass. The replay covers nine historical laboratory PDF reports containing 102 manually labelled rows. The reports produce 97 numeric candidates: schema validation accepts all 97, an earlier packet-level evidence check accepts 94, and the hardened quotation- and row-level policy admits 72 while retaining 25 for review. The study evaluates system integrity rather than clinical correctness or clinical safety. The results demonstrate the technical feasibility of an enforceable boundary that prevents generated claims from authorizing their own reuse in a longitudinal health record.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型可以把医疗文档转换为结构化数据，但看似可信的输出仍可能没有源文档支持。因此，把这类输出持久写入不断累积患者信息的纵向健康记录会产生完整性风险：未经验证的数据可能影响后续摘要、趋势或预防保健计算。我们提出一种 evidence-gated trust-promotion model，使生成数据保持 provisional 状态，直到 deterministic monitor 根据源文档完成验证。只有当源文档包含唯一的支持性引文、相关字段位于同一化验行且所需 provenance 得以保留时，monitor 才允许候选用于指定的下游用途。Generator 不能批准自己的输出；证据缺失或有歧义会触发拒绝，而被拒候选会保留供人工复核，不会被静默丢弃。我们在个人健康记录应用 Medical DataCloud 中实现该模型，并通过自动化测试和已保存抽取输出的 replay 进行评估。全部 22 项 conformance 与 mutation 测试均通过。Replay 覆盖 9 份历史化验 PDF 报告，其中包含 102 个手工标注行。这些报告产生 97 个数值候选：schema validation 接受全部 97 个，早期 packet-level evidence check 接受 94 个，而强化后的 quotation-level 和 row-level policy 只准入 72 个，并保留 25 个供复核。该研究评估系统完整性，而不是临床正确性或临床安全性。结果证明，建立一条可强制执行的边界在技术上可行，可防止生成 claim 自行授权其在纵向健康记录中的复用。

### 14. Agent Zero Memory: Provenance-Aware Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents

**内容概述：** 针对单一 fact store、vector index 或 knowledge graph 会继承各自 blind spot，论文提出 Agent Zero Memory，以三套并行记忆、intent gate、source router 和 citation lock 强制每条学习内容携带来源与证据指针，在 LongMemEval／LoCoMo 上分别达到 95.60%／93.60%，但其安全声明主要是结构设计而非对抗评测。

- 收录理由：论文把 provenance、timestamp、evidence pointer 和“只引用实际打开证据”的 citation lock 纳入长期 Agent memory 读取纪律，为防止记忆来源混淆和无证据持久化提供系统级设计。
- 论文/arXiv：[Agent Zero Memory: Provenance-Aware Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29606)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29606`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：10 pages, 3 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Ming Wu — 单位未核实
  - Pengyuan Zhu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents need durable, faithful memory of everything a user or organization has said and stored, yet most memory systems commit to a single organizing structure (a fact store, a vector index, or a knowledge graph) and inherit its blind spots. We present Agent Zero Memory, a provenance-aware long-term memory system that distils a user's conversations, files, and connected sources into three parallel memory systems, each capturing a different facet of the same history: an episodic Memory Events timeline that makes when and what changed first-class, an associative entity-event knowledge graph that links people and projects across sessions, and a semantic, curated, citation-locked Hierarchical Documentary Memory (HDM) of durable facts. A retrieval turn runs an intent gate (so self-contained turns add no latency), a source router, and three concurrent agentic searches, one per system, each a tool-using loop over hybrid (embedding + lexical) search under agent-controlled filters; their grounded, cited answers are integrated into one answer with a single confidence. We formalize the reading discipline: every learned item is a provenanced item carrying its origin, timestamp, and evidence pointer, and every answer is read under a citation lock, so it may cite only evidence its reader actually opened; fabrication is structurally excluded and the system abstains rather than guesses. On two public benchmarks the system sets a new state of the art: 95.60% on LongMemEval and 93.60% on LoCoMo, improving over the strongest prior systems by +0.73 and +1.10 points. A controlled study across eight backbone LLMs characterizes the accuracy-cost-latency frontier: accuracy varies by only 3.4 points while per-query cost varies by ~30x, with near-state-of-the-art quality at up to 20x lower cost per query, the signature of memory-driven, rather than model-driven, quality.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）Agent 需要对用户或组织曾经说过和存储过的一切形成持久、忠实的记忆，但多数 memory system 只采用一种组织结构（fact store、vector index 或 knowledge graph），因而继承其 blind spot。我们提出 Agent Zero Memory，一个 provenance-aware long-term memory system，它把用户的对话、文件和连接来源蒸馏进三套并行记忆系统，每套捕获同一历史的不同侧面：把“何时发生何种变化”作为一等信息的 episodic Memory Events 时间线；跨会话连接人物与项目的 associative entity-event knowledge graph；以及对持久事实进行语义化精选并锁定引用的 Hierarchical Documentary Memory（HDM）。一次检索轮次会运行 intent gate（使自包含轮次不增加延迟）、source router 和三项并发 agentic search，每套系统各一项；每项搜索都是在 Agent 控制 filter 下对 hybrid（embedding + lexical）search 进行的 tool-using loop，最后把有 grounding 和 citation 的回答整合为一个带统一 confidence 的答案。我们形式化了读取纪律：每个 learned item 都是带有来源、时间戳和证据指针的 provenanced item；每个答案都在 citation lock 下读取，因此只能引用其 reader 实际打开的证据；系统在结构上排除 fabrication，并会 abstain 而不是猜测。在两个公开 benchmark 上，该系统取得新 SOTA：LongMemEval 为 95.60%，LoCoMo 为 93.60%，分别比此前最强系统提高 0.73 和 1.10 个百分点。一项覆盖八个 backbone LLM 的受控研究刻画了 accuracy-cost-latency frontier：准确率只变化 3.4 个百分点，而每查询成本相差约 30 倍；在每查询成本最高降低 20 倍时仍可取得接近 SOTA 的质量，这体现出质量由 memory 而非 model 驱动。

### 15. Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Skills: Architecture, Lifecycle, and Security

**内容概述：** 针对 agentic skill 缺少统一系统抽象的问题，论文把 skill 定义为连接高层规划与确定性执行环境的外置程序知识，提出覆盖发现、编写、存储、路由、编排、执行修复、终身适应、评测和安全治理的九阶段生命周期，并梳理 marketplace／registry 攻击面与 runtime verification。

- 收录理由：这是本批次直接讨论 Agent skill architecture、lifecycle、registry／marketplace threat vector 和防御机制的系统性工作，可作为后续 skill poisoning 与 supply-chain 研究的范围框架。

  - Sanket Badhe — 单位未核实
  - Deep Shah — 单位未核实
  - Priyanka Tiwari — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face reliability, context consumption, and execution stability bottlenecks when deployed on complex, long-horizon tasks. While monolithic prompt engineering and stateless tool-calling paradigms struggle to scale, the field is rapidly converging toward \emph{agentic skills}: modular procedural abstractions that externalize execution knowledge into reusable, executable, and portable artifacts. This paper establishes a unified systems foundation and reference architecture for the agentic skills ecosystem. We formalize skills as externalized procedural knowledge bridging high-level cognitive planning with deterministic execution environments, and systematically delineate the architecture across a nine-stage lifecycle: autonomous discovery, authoring and representation formats, memory storage, dynamic retrieval and routing, composition and orchestration, execution and repair, lifelong adaptation, empirical evaluation, and security governance. We further examine marketplace dynamics, public registries, and emerging adversarial threat vectors, alongside runtime verification and defense mechanisms. Finally, we categorize system implementations across software engineering, operating system navigation, embodied robotics, and scientific discovery, while highlighting critical open challenges in continual learning and benchmark realism. This work establishes agentic skills as a foundational paradigm for building scalable, robust, and verifiable autonomous language agents.
- 中文摘要：自主大型语言模型（LLM）Agent 部署在复杂长时任务上时，越来越多地面临可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性瓶颈。整体式 prompt engineering 和无状态 tool-calling 范式难以扩展，研究领域正快速转向 \emph{agentic skills}：把执行知识外置为可复用、可执行和可移植 artifact 的模块化程序抽象。本文为 agentic skill 生态建立统一的系统基础和 reference architecture。我们把 skill 形式化为连接高层认知规划与确定性执行环境的外置 procedural knowledge，并通过九阶段生命周期系统划分架构：自主发现、编写与表示格式、memory storage、动态检索与路由、组合与编排、执行与修复、终身适应、经验评估以及安全治理。我们还考察 marketplace dynamics、public registry 和新兴 adversarial threat vector，以及 runtime verification 与防御机制。最后，我们对软件工程、操作系统导航、具身机器人和科学发现中的系统实现进行分类，并指出 continual learning 与 benchmark realism 方面的关键开放挑战。该工作把 agentic skill 确立为构建可扩展、稳健且可验证的 autonomous language Agent 的基础范式。

### 16. Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness of Reasoning Models Varies with Where and How Preference Cues Are Delivered

**内容概述：** 针对 CoT faithfulness 测试通常只把显式 bias cue 放在用户消息、而 Agent 更常从 tool return 或 raw artifact 接收偏好的缺口，论文构建 5,100 样本 FACE-Eval，在 15 个模型上发现 tool-return／implicit cue 的未言明采纳更高且更难被 transcript monitor 检出，告知模型将被监控也不能稳定修复。

- 收录理由：论文直接测量 CoT 对 tool-derived influence 的漏报，并把 cue channel、explicitness 与 monitor detection 联动，揭示 Agent 场景中仅依赖可见 reasoning trace 的监督边界。
- 论文/arXiv：[Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness of Reasoning Models Varies with Where and How Preference Cues Are Delivered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29464)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29464`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 英文摘要：Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring assumes that reasoning traces faithfully record the information that shapes a model's answer. Existing faithfulness tests often place explicit bias cues in the user message, while agents may encounter preferences through tool returns or raw artifacts. We introduce FACE-Eval (Faithful Attribution of Cue Effects Evaluation), a 5,100-sample evaluation that varies cue location (user message or tool return) and explicitness (direct summary or raw artifact). We measure verbalized commitment among cue-following answers and unverbalized adoption among all cued samples. We evaluate 15 open-weight models from eight families, with total parameters ranging from 4B to 1.60T. Every model has lower verbalized commitment for tool-return than user-message cues and for implicit than explicit cues. Unverbalized adoption is higher for tool-return cues on all 15 models and for implicit cues in 28 of 30 model-channel comparisons. A source-attribution prompt narrows the channel gap on seven models, sometimes by increasing user-channel unverbalized adoption, while telling models that their reasoning will be monitored does not reliably close the gap. We also use two transcript monitors (GPT-5.6-Luna and GPT-4o-mini) to detect preference adoption in the largest model of each family. Across 32 model-channel-explicitness cells, higher unverbalized adoption is associated with lower detection ability for both monitors (Pearson r=-0.54 and r=-0.78, respectively). These results suggest that CoT monitoring may be less reliable when preference information arrives through tools or must be inferred from raw artifacts, within the single-call, prefilled-tool setting tested here.
- 中文摘要：Chain-of-thought（CoT）监控假设 reasoning trace 会忠实记录塑造模型答案的信息。现有 faithfulness 测试通常把显式 bias cue 放在用户消息中，但 Agent 可能通过 tool return 或 raw artifact 遇到偏好。我们提出 FACE-Eval（Faithful Attribution of Cue Effects Evaluation），这是一个包含 5,100 个样本的评估，改变 cue location（用户消息或 tool return）和 explicitness（直接摘要或 raw artifact）。我们在遵循 cue 的答案中测量 verbalized commitment，并在全部带 cue 样本中测量 unverbalized adoption。我们评估了来自八个系列的 15 个 open-weight model，总参数量从 4B 到 1.60T。所有模型对 tool-return cue 的 verbalized commitment 都低于 user-message cue，对 implicit cue 的 verbalized commitment 也低于 explicit cue。全部 15 个模型对 tool-return cue 的 unverbalized adoption 都更高；在 30 个 model-channel 比较中，有 28 个对 implicit cue 的未言明采纳更高。Source-attribution prompt 在七个模型上缩小 channel gap，但有时是通过增加 user channel 的 unverbalized adoption 实现；告知模型其 reasoning 将被监控，不能可靠缩小差距。我们还使用两个 transcript monitor（GPT-5.6-Luna 和 GPT-4o-mini），检测每个系列最大模型是否采纳偏好。在 32 个 model-channel-explicitness cell 中，较高的 unverbalized adoption 与两个 monitor 较低的检测能力相关（Pearson $r=-0.54$ 和 $r=-0.78$）。在本文测试的 single-call、prefilled-tool 设置中，这些结果表明，当偏好信息来自工具或必须从 raw artifact 推断时，CoT monitor 可能更不可靠。

### 17. Selective Disclosure of Hidden Directives in Reasoning Models: Behavioral Asymmetry and Steering

**内容概述：** 针对 CoT oversight 假设模型会无差别暴露 hidden directive，论文定义 Instruction-Compliance Gap，在八个 reasoning model 上发现恶意 directive 比良性 directive 更易泄漏，并以 activation steering 因果诱导或抑制隐藏行为，且两类 hiding vector 高度相似，指向共享隐藏方向的差异激活。

- 收录理由：论文同时涉及 system prompt directive 泄漏、CoT monitorability 和可操控隐藏机制，以行为、monitor 与 activation intervention 三层证据检验 reasoning oversight 的关键假设。
- 论文/arXiv：[Selective Disclosure of Hidden Directives in Reasoning Models: Behavioral Asymmetry and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29070)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29070`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29

- 作者及单位：
  - Zimo Shi — 单位未核实
  - Xander Tifft — 单位未核实
  - Wen Xing — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning traces are increasingly proposed as a mechanism for AI oversight: a monitor inspecting a model's reasoning can, in principle, detect misbehavior invisible from outputs alone. This assumes CoT surfaces what a model is instructed to do regardless of the instructions given. We test this assumption along two axes. First, we introduce the Instruction-Compliance Gap (ICG): the difference in probability that a model's CoT explicitly references a hidden system prompt directive when that directive is malign versus benign. Across 100 task pairs and 8 frontier reasoning models from 5 families, we find consistent asymmetric disclosure, a higher probability of leaking malign hidden instructions than benign ones, in Qwen3-14B (Wilcoxon $p=0.0001$, $+13.9$pp), Qwen3-32B ($p=0.0011$, $+13.0$pp), Qwen3-235B ($p=0.035$, $+5.8$pp), and similar results with MiniMax-M2.5 and DeepSeek-R1. The detector has 100% precision against two independent blinded labelling passes, and an LLM monitor reading only the reasoning trace reproduces the asymmetry in all 8 models against directive-free controls, identifying the specific directive in 82% of malign traces which the detector classifies as clean. Second, steering vectors extracted in MiniMax-M2.5 via Contrastive Activation Addition causally induce hiding from bare prompts and suppress it from prompts that would otherwise produce it, replicating in Qwen3-14B under a pre-registered design. Benign and malign-derived hiding vectors are highly similar (cosine $0.804$ in MiniMax-M2.5; $0.970$ in Qwen3-14B), implying that in these models the disclosure asymmetry arises from differential activation of a shared hiding direction rather than separate mechanisms.
- 中文摘要：Chain-of-thought（CoT）reasoning trace 越来越多地被提议作为 AI oversight 机制：原则上，检查模型 reasoning 的 monitor 可以发现仅从输出无法看到的不当行为。这一做法假设，无论指令内容是什么，CoT 都会呈现模型被要求做的事情。我们沿两个轴检验这一假设。首先，我们提出 Instruction-Compliance Gap（ICG）：当 hidden system prompt directive 为恶意或良性时，模型 CoT 明确提及该 directive 的概率之差。在 100 对任务和来自五个系列的八个 frontier reasoning model 上，我们发现稳定的非对称披露，即泄漏恶意 hidden instruction 的概率高于良性 instruction；这一现象出现在 Qwen3-14B（Wilcoxon $p=0.0001$，$+13.9$ 个百分点）、Qwen3-32B（$p=0.0011$，$+13.0$ 个百分点）、Qwen3-235B（$p=0.035$，$+5.8$ 个百分点），MiniMax-M2.5 和 DeepSeek-R1 上也有类似结果。对照两轮独立盲标，detector 的 precision 为 100%；只读取 reasoning trace 的 LLM monitor 在全部八个模型上、相对于无 directive control 复现了该不对称性，并在 detector 判为 clean 的恶意 trace 中有 82% 识别出具体 directive。其次，通过 Contrastive Activation Addition 从 MiniMax-M2.5 提取的 steering vector，可从 bare prompt 因果诱导隐藏，也可在原本会产生隐藏的 prompt 上抑制该行为；这一结果在预注册设计下于 Qwen3-14B 复现。由良性和恶意样本得到的 hiding vector 高度相似（MiniMax-M2.5 中 cosine 为 0.804，Qwen3-14B 中为 0.970），说明这些模型的披露不对称源于共享隐藏方向的差异激活，而不是两套独立机制。

### 18. Detecting Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Large Language Models with Linguistic, Behavioral, and Mechanistic Indicators

**内容概述：** 针对模型不展示中间步骤时无法判断其是否 latent reasoning，论文提出融合语言、行为和机制信号的 Hidden CoT Detection Score，在 Qwen3-4B 两个变体上取得显著正值并跨推理栈和量化复现，但明确指出该分数只测 neutral prompt 与显式 CoT 的相似性，不能证明存在未暴露 trace。

- 收录理由：论文提供不依赖模型自报 trace 的 hidden reasoning 审计信号，同时用 length-adjusted control 暴露未校正指标的假阳性，符合 CoT visibility 与 monitor validity 方向。

- 英文摘要：Large language models often answer complex reasoning questions without revealing intermediate steps, raising whether they reason latently or complete patterns. We propose the Hidden CoT Detection Score (HCDS), a comparative behavioral and mechanistic signal measuring whether neutral-prompt behavior aligns more closely with explicit CoT or explicit no- CoT. Here, hidden CoT operationally denotes this neutral-prompt CoT-like alignment; HCDS does not directly observe or prove an unexposed reasoning trace. On GSM8K, HCDS is significantly positive for both Qwen3-4B variants (Thinking $+1.87$, $p = 1.2 \times 10^{-7}$; Instruct $+1.41$, $p = 1.9 \times 10^{-4}$), replicates across a different inference stack and quantization within $0.08$ ($+1.80$ and $+1.45$), and is not significantly positive in seven of eight length-adjusted calibration-control cells. The unadjusted score produces large positive scores on single-step arithmetic and numeric factual lookup. The variants also respond differently to no-CoT instructions: Instruct complies from the prompt alone, whereas Thinking continues reasoning and requires intervention. These findings show stronger, less prompt-conditional CoT-like behavior in the reasoning-tuned model, consistent with but not proof of latent reasoning. HCDS thus investigates latent reasoning without relying on models' self-reported traces.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型经常在不展示中间步骤的情况下回答复杂 reasoning question，这带来一个问题：它们是在进行 latent reasoning，还是仅仅完成模式。我们提出 Hidden CoT Detection Score（HCDS），这是一个比较式行为与机制信号，用于测量 neutral-prompt behavior 更接近 explicit CoT 还是 explicit no-CoT。这里，hidden CoT 在操作上指这种 neutral prompt 下的 CoT-like alignment；HCDS 不会直接观察或证明存在未暴露的 reasoning trace。在 GSM8K 上，HCDS 对两个 Qwen3-4B 变体都显著为正（Thinking 为 $+1.87$，$p=1.2 \times 10^{-7}$；Instruct 为 $+1.41$，$p=1.9 \times 10^{-4}$），并在不同 inference stack 和 quantization 下以 $0.08$ 以内的差异复现（$+1.80$ 和 $+1.45$）；在八个 length-adjusted calibration-control cell 中，有七个没有显著正值。未校正分数会在单步算术和数值事实查询上产生很大的正分。两个变体对 no-CoT instruction 的响应也不同：Instruct 只凭 prompt 就会遵从，而 Thinking 会继续推理并需要 intervention。结果显示，reasoning-tuned model 具有更强、对 prompt 条件依赖更低的 CoT-like behavior；这与 latent reasoning 一致，但不能证明它。HCDS 因而能够在不依赖模型自报 trace 的情况下研究 latent reasoning。

### 19. Detect Before You Attribute: Cascade Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems

**内容概述：** 针对 topology-based attribution 忽略语义、而 LLM attribution 又受长上下文退化影响，论文提出 detect-before-attribute 插件 DUOTRACE，先用双视图语义—结构表示和 Tree-LSTM 检出异常执行，再向下游提供聚焦证据，使六种基线的 Agent-level 与 step-level 归因准确率分别提高 8.7% 和 7.0%。

- 收录理由：论文聚焦 multi-agent cascade failure 的异常检测与责任定位，提供 step-level evidence filtering，可用于事故分析与精确干预，而不是只输出整条轨迹的模糊归因。
- 论文/arXiv：[Detect Before You Attribute: Cascade Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29646)

- 英文摘要：Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown strong potential in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning, yet they remain vulnerable to execution failures. Accurate failure attribution is therefore critical for improving agent reliability. Existing topology- and spectrum-based methods exploit trajectory structures but often overlook fine-grained semantics, while LLM-based attribution methods capture semantic cues but suffer from long-context degradation over lengthy trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DUOTRACE, a plug-and-play detection filter for LLM-based failure attribution. DUOTRACE follows a detect-before-attribute paradigm: it first detects anomalous executions and then supplies focused trajectory evidence to downstream LLM-based attribution methods. For effective VAE-based anomaly detection on agent trajectories, DUOTRACE integrates dual-view semantic-structural node representations, a Tree-LSTM-based trajectory encoder, and prefix-chain- and LLM-based data augmentation to handle heterogeneous nodes, hierarchical execution structures, and limited failure data. Experiments with six LLM-based attribution baselines show that DUOTRACE improves agent-level and step-level attribution accuracy by 8.7% and 7.0%, respectively.
- 中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLM）的 Agent 通过多步推理展现出解决复杂任务的强大潜力，但仍容易发生执行失败。因此，准确的 failure attribution 对提高 Agent 可靠性至关重要。现有 topology-based 和 spectrum-based 方法利用轨迹结构，却常忽略细粒度语义；基于 LLM 的归因方法能捕获语义线索，但会在长轨迹中受到 long-context degradation 影响。为解决这些问题，我们提出 DUOTRACE，一个用于 LLM-based failure attribution 的 plug-and-play detection filter。DUOTRACE 遵循 detect-before-attribute 范式：它先检测异常执行，再向下游基于 LLM 的归因方法提供聚焦的轨迹证据。为了在 Agent 轨迹上进行有效的 VAE-based anomaly detection，DUOTRACE 集成 dual-view semantic-structural node representation、基于 Tree-LSTM 的 trajectory encoder，以及 prefix-chain 和 LLM-based data augmentation，以处理异构 node、层级执行结构和有限失败数据。对六种 LLM-based attribution baseline 的实验表明，DUOTRACE 分别将 Agent-level 和 step-level 归因准确率提高 8.7% 和 7.0%。

### 20. Localizing Emergent Failures in Agentic AI: Recovering Minimal Repair Families via Counterfactual Replay

**内容概述：** 针对 multi-agent failure 可能需要联合修复多个消息、pointwise attribution 无法区分联合必要与替代性修复，论文形式化 Minimal Repair Family Recovery 并提出基于 dependency graph 的 GCJR，以反事实 replay 恢复全部最小修复族，在受控 DAG 和四 Agent pilot 上均达 1.000 Family Exact Match，同时把 replay／model call 约减半。

- 收录理由：论文把多 Agent 事故归因从单点 blame 扩展为所有最小联合修复集合，并用可执行 counterfactual replay 验证，适合分析 distributed risk 与交互式失效。
- 论文/arXiv：[Localizing Emergent Failures in Agentic AI: Recovering Minimal Repair Families via Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29228)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29228`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

  - Yumeng Song — 单位未核实
  - Zhongming Yao — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：Failures in agentic AI systems can arise from interactions among messages exchanged by multiple large language model (LLM) agents. Pointwise attribution cannot distinguish a jointly necessary repair from alternative singleton repairs. We formulate Minimal Repair Family Recovery (MRFR): recovering all inclusion-minimal event sets whose counterfactual replay restores task success within a declared size bound. We propose Graph-Constrained Joint Replay (GCJR), which slices failure-relevant events from an execution dependency graph, constructs graph-feasible singleton and pair candidates, and verifies them by replay with paired clean counterparts. For fixed replay outcomes, GCJR is exact within its declared graph domain. On 90 in-scope cases from a 120-DAG controlled benchmark, GCJR achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match while reducing mean replay calls from 56.3 to 25.3 (55.1%) relative to exhaustive search. On a 24-case, four-agent LLM pilot, it again achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match and reduces mean model calls from 21.0 to 10.0 (52.4%); single-event replay misses jointly necessary repairs.
- 中文摘要：Agentic AI 系统的失败可能源自多个大型语言模型（LLM）Agent 交换消息时产生的交互。Pointwise attribution 无法区分联合必要的修复与可替代的单点修复。我们形式化 Minimal Repair Family Recovery（MRFR）：在声明的规模上限内，恢复所有通过反事实 replay 能使任务重新成功、且在集合包含关系下最小的事件集合。我们提出 Graph-Constrained Joint Replay（GCJR），它从 execution dependency graph 中切出与失败相关的事件，构造满足图约束的单点和事件对候选，并将其与配对的 clean counterpart 一同 replay 以验证。对于固定的 replay 结果，GCJR 在其声明的 graph domain 内是精确的。在从 120-DAG 受控 benchmark 取得的 90 个范围内案例上，GCJR 达到 1.000 Family Exact Match，同时相对于穷举搜索把平均 replay 调用从 56.3 降至 25.3（减少 55.1%）。在一个包含 24 个案例、四个 Agent 的 LLM pilot 上，它同样达到 1.000 Family Exact Match，并把平均模型调用从 21.0 降至 10.0（减少 52.4%）；单事件 replay 会遗漏联合必要的修复。

### 21. Last Step Matters: Early Uncertainty Cannot Predict Failure in Long-Horizon Agents

**内容概述：** 针对 long-horizon Agent 能否用早期 uncertainty 触发及时干预尚未验证，论文在 deep-research 任务上发现终局 verbal confidence 可达平均 0.85 AUROC，但轨迹进行到 50% 时所有信号均不超过 0.60，原因是频繁 path switching 切断早期信号与最终结果的关联。

- 收录理由：论文给出 Agent runtime monitor 的重要负结果与机制解释，说明 uncertainty 不能被机械当作早停保障，并提出只在终局 confidence 上决定 restart 的有限部署建议。
- 论文/arXiv：[Last Step Matters: Early Uncertainty Cannot Predict Failure in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29685)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29685`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 作者及单位：

  - Chengyue Yu — 单位未核实

  - Linjian Mo — 单位未核实
  - Leilei Gan — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Early failure prediction is important for long-horizon agents, as it enables timely intervention and can reduce inference and tool-use costs. Uncertainty quantification, such as verbal confidence and perplexity, offers a promising approach to detecting agent failures; however, it has not been explored whether these signals retain their discriminative power during the intermediate stages of long-horizon execution. We evaluate mainstream uncertainty signals on deep-research tasks and find that verbal confidence reliably distinguishes failures at trajectory completion, achieving a mean AUROC of 0.85, whereas all evaluated signals offer limited predictive value earlier in execution, with none exceeding a mean AUROC of 0.60 at 50% trajectory progress. We identify an underlying mechanism explaining this gap: path switching, where agents frequently abandon their current search direction in-trajectory, breaking the link between early signal and final outcome. These findings challenge the assumption that intermediate uncertainty can reliably guide early intervention. They also motivate a practical recommendation for agent harnesses in deep-research settings: use final-step confidence to decide whether to restart, an approach that our experiments find more effective than in-trajectory intervention.
- 中文摘要：对 long-horizon Agent 进行早期失败预测很重要，因为它能支持及时干预，并减少 inference 和 tool-use 成本。Verbal confidence、perplexity 等 uncertainty quantification 为检测 Agent 失败提供了一条有希望的路径；然而，这些信号在长时执行中间阶段是否仍保有区分能力，尚未得到研究。我们在 deep-research 任务上评估主流 uncertainty signal，发现 verbal confidence 在轨迹结束时能够可靠地区分失败，平均 AUROC 达 0.85；但在更早的执行阶段，所有被评估信号的预测价值都有限，在轨迹进度 50% 时没有一个平均 AUROC 超过 0.60。我们识别出解释这一差距的底层机制：path switching，即 Agent 在轨迹中频繁放弃当前搜索方向，从而切断早期信号与最终结果之间的联系。这些发现挑战了 intermediate uncertainty 能可靠指导早期干预的假设。它们还为 deep-research 场景的 Agent harness 提供一项实践建议：用 final-step confidence 决定是否重启；实验发现这比轨迹中干预更有效。

### 22. Bridging Semantics and Physics with Constrained LLMs for Safe and Trustworthy Robotic Manipulation

**内容概述：** 针对语言计划表面正确却可能违反运动学或碰撞约束，论文把 LLM reasoning–execution 边界形式化为 typed contract，以 MCP schema 验证 tool call、在 MoveIt 中执行 verify-then-act，仅放行同时通过运动学和碰撞检查的轨迹；实机倾倒与抓放任务最高成功率分别为 80% 和 90%。

- 收录理由：论文将 LLM 机器人动作权限置于确定性执行检查之后，用 schema rejection 和 physical constraint verification 阻止不安全命令到达设备，属于具身 Agent runtime enforcement。
- 论文/arXiv：[Bridging Semantics and Physics with Constrained LLMs for Safe and Trustworthy Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29379)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29379`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.RO`

- arXiv Comments：ECCV Workshop paper
- 作者及单位：
  - Wenhao Hong — 单位未核实
  - Lan Wei — 单位未核实
  - Dandan Zhang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：A language-guided robot operating in a real kitchen must do more than produce a plan that appears correct. It must also execute that plan safely in cluttered environments under imperfect perception. Large language models (LLM) can decompose instructions into action sequences, yet a language-action gap remains: a plan may appear valid linguistically while being physically infeasible under kinematic and collision constraints. We bridge this gap by formalizing the reasoning-execution boundary as a typed contract. From RGB-D observations, the system grounds perceived objects in an explicit, collision-aware scene model and constrains language-level decisions through schema-validated tool calls defined by the Model Context Protocol (MCP), rejecting malformed commands before they reach the robot. Each validated call is deterministically grounded in a MoveIt Task Constructor pipeline, where candidate motions are evaluated against the reconstructed planning scene in a verify-then-act step. Only trajectories that pass both kinematic and collision checks are sent to the robot. On a physical UFactory 850, the method achieves up to 80% success across ten trials per task on pouring tasks involving liquids, granular media, and discrete solids. It achieves 90% success on a grasp-and-place task using the same planning, protocol, and verification stack. Although a scripted policy slightly outperforms our method on the easiest task, its success rate falls to 10% on the hardest, compared with 60% for our method.
- 中文摘要：在真实厨房中运行的语言引导机器人不能只生成看起来正确的计划，还必须在感知不完美的杂乱环境中安全执行。大型语言模型（LLM）可以把指令分解为动作序列，但 language-action gap 仍然存在：一个计划在语言上看似有效，在运动学和碰撞约束下却可能物理不可行。我们把 reasoning-execution boundary 形式化为 typed contract，以弥合这一差距。系统从 RGB-D observation 出发，把感知到的物体 grounding 到显式、collision-aware scene model 中，并通过由 Model Context Protocol（MCP）定义且经过 schema validation 的 tool call 约束语言层决策，在格式错误的命令抵达机器人前将其拒绝。每个通过验证的调用都被确定性 grounding 到 MoveIt Task Constructor pipeline，在其中通过 verify-then-act 步骤，根据重建的 planning scene 评估候选动作。只有同时通过运动学与碰撞检查的轨迹才会发送给机器人。在实体 UFactory 850 上，对于涉及液体、颗粒介质和离散固体的倾倒任务，该方法在每项任务十次试验中取得最高 80% 的成功率；使用相同 planning、protocol 和 verification stack 时，抓取放置任务成功率为 90%。尽管 scripted policy 在最简单任务上略优于本文方法，但在最难任务上其成功率降至 10%，而本文方法为 60%。

### 23. Drive the Thoughts: Runtime Monitoring of VLA Reasoning-Trajectory Consistency

**内容概述：** 针对驾驶 VLA 的 CoT 能否作为 trajectory runtime specification 缺少证据，论文构建含 150 对 CoT–trajectory 的 DriveAlignBench，发现 33.3% CoT 本身不可靠、可靠 CoT 中仅 74% 与轨迹一致；最佳 lane-relative monitor 达到 0.75 F1，较最强 raw-waypoint LLM 基线提高 0.13。

- 收录理由：论文把自然语言 rationale 与真实低层轨迹分开核验，并明确测量 CoT 不可靠和 action inconsistency，直接对应 VLA reasoning monitor 与安全执行监督。
- 论文/arXiv：[Drive the Thoughts: Runtime Monitoring of VLA Reasoning-Trajectory Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29583)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29583`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/776styjsu/drive-the-thoughts)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.SE`
