- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Tian Yu — 单位未核实
  - Lu Feng — 单位未核实
  - Sebastian Elbaum — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Autonomous vehicles (AVs) operate in complex environments where failures are consequential. Sophisticated machine learning models for perception and planning are key to overcoming at least part of that complexity, but their black-box nature complicates validation and verification (V&V). The recent integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into AVs introduces a unique opportunity: besides generating trajectories, these models produce an explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) explaining their underlying rationale. This CoT provides a rich specification to cross-check model outputs and detect inconsistencies that may expose unsafe or unintended behavior. This paper assesses whether CoTs from a recent open driving VLA can support such monitoring. We curate DriveAlignBench, a specialized dataset from NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 VLA for AVs containing 150 CoT-trajectory pairs, which we manually annotate for reliability, trajectory consistency, and safety. Our analysis reveals that 33.3% of CoTs are unreliable. Among reliable CoTs, the generated trajectory is consistent with the CoT in 74% of cases. Leveraging this potential, we propose integrating a CoT-trajectory consistency check into a runtime monitor. The check is nontrivial: CoTs express open-vocabulary, scene-relative driving commitments, while trajectories are low-level ego-motion sequences whose semantics depend on road geometry and motion context. To bridge this gap, we develop a family of automated consistency monitors. Our best monitor, lane-relative F-LLM with GPT-5.5, achieves F1 = 0.75, improving over the strongest raw-waypoint LLM baseline by +0.13 absolute F1 and over a rule-based monitor by +0.38. We release DriveAlignBench, the monitor implementations, and annotation tools at https://github.com/776styjsu/drive-the-thoughts.
- 中文摘要：自动驾驶车辆（AV）运行在复杂环境中，失败会造成严重后果。复杂的机器学习感知和规划模型是克服部分复杂性的关键，但其黑盒性质使 validation and verification（V&V）更困难。最近，Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型被集成到 AV 中，带来一个独特机会：这些模型除生成轨迹外，还会生成解释底层 rationale 的显式 Chain-of-Thought（CoT）。CoT 提供了一份丰富的 specification，可用于交叉检查模型输出并检测可能暴露不安全或非预期行为的不一致。本文评估近期开放驾驶 VLA 的 CoT 能否支持这种监控。我们从 NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5 驾驶 VLA 整理出专门数据集 DriveAlignBench，其中包含 150 个 CoT–trajectory pair，并人工标注 reliability、trajectory consistency 和 safety。分析显示，33.3% 的 CoT 不可靠；在可靠 CoT 中，生成轨迹只有 74% 与 CoT 一致。利用这一潜力，我们提出把 CoT–trajectory consistency check 集成进 runtime monitor。该检查并不简单：CoT 表达开放词汇、相对于场景的驾驶承诺，而轨迹是低层 ego-motion sequence，其语义取决于道路几何与运动上下文。为弥合差距，我们开发一组自动 consistency monitor。最佳 monitor，即使用 GPT-5.5 的 lane-relative F-LLM，达到 $F1=0.75$，比最强 raw-waypoint LLM baseline 的绝对 F1 高 0.13，比 rule-based monitor 高 0.38。我们公开 DriveAlignBench、monitor 实现和标注工具：https://github.com/776styjsu/drive-the-thoughts。

### 24. ActReal: System-Level Mobile Agents Challenge Mobile Automation Detection

**内容概述：** 针对系统级 mobile Agent 可同时控制触屏输入与应用可见 IMU、从而绕过基于人类物理耦合的自动化检测，论文提出 ActReal，把语义动作转换为时间对齐的真实触摸与六轴 IMU 事件，平均 event-level ASR 为 77.5%，即使联合检测两类信号仍有 71.1%。

- 收录理由：论文将 adaptive mobile Agent 明确建模为攻击执行器，展示其继承系统权限后可伪造跨传感器行为并逃避 automation detector，属于 Agent 驱动的执行与检测规避风险。
- 论文/arXiv：[ActReal: System-Level Mobile Agents Challenge Mobile Automation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30038)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30038`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：15 pages, 5 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Mingshuo Wang — 单位未核实
  - Hanqing Guo — 单位未核实
  - Huining Li — 单位未核实
  - Yuliang Fu — 单位未核实
  - Jing Xu — 单位未核实
  - Chenhan Xu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：System-level mobile agents are evolving from fixed scripts into adaptive systems that continuously observe interfaces, reason, and adjust their actions, allowing automated attacks to navigate dynamic UIs and complete complex tasks. Existing applications detect automation using touch trajectories, action timing, and the physical coupling between touch and inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals. However, a privileged system-level agent executor can control both touchscreen input and application-visible sensor delivery, enabling it to jointly generate time-aligned touch and six-axis IMU signals and evade these defenses. We present ActReal, a physical-action attack framework for system-level mobile agents. ActReal converts semantic agent actions into task-valid touch and IMU events using genuine-trajectory adaptation and physics-guided IMU generation. ActReal achieves a mean event-level attack success rate of 77.5\%; even when detectors jointly observe touch and IMU, its attack success rate remains 71.1\%.
- 中文摘要：系统级 mobile Agent 正从固定脚本演变为持续观察界面、推理并调整动作的自适应系统，使自动化攻击能够在动态 UI 中导航并完成复杂任务。现有应用使用触摸轨迹、动作时间以及触摸与惯性测量单元（IMU）信号之间的物理耦合来检测自动化。然而，具有特权的系统级 Agent executor 可以同时控制触摸屏输入和应用可见的 sensor delivery，使其能够联合生成时间对齐的触摸与六轴 IMU 信号，并规避这些防御。我们提出 ActReal，一个面向系统级 mobile Agent 的 physical-action attack framework。ActReal 使用 genuine-trajectory adaptation 和 physics-guided IMU generation，把 Agent 的语义动作转换为在任务上有效的触摸和 IMU 事件。ActReal 的平均 event-level attack success rate 为 77.5%；即使 detector 联合观察触摸与 IMU，其攻击成功率仍为 71.1%。

### 25. GPAgentBench-2K: Benchmarking Large Language Model Agents in Complex Clinical Action Space

**内容概述：** 针对临床 Agent benchmark 将真实 workflow 简化为静态预测或粗粒度无约束 MDP，论文构建基于真实全科诊疗记录的 GPAgentBench-2K CMDP，把 safety-informed abstention 设为一等结果；16 个 LLM 中诊断最准确的 frontier model 在逾半高风险案例仍违反安全约束，C-GRPO 也远未达临床可接受水平。

- 收录理由：论文直接评测高风险 Agent 在扩大 action space 后的 constraint violation，而非只看诊断准确率，并为 safety-constrained policy optimization 提供可核验环境与基线。
- 论文/arXiv：[GPAgentBench-2K: Benchmarking Large Language Model Agents in Complex Clinical Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30188)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30188`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Boqi Chen — 单位未核实
  - Xudong Liu — 单位未核实
  - Yunke Ao — 单位未核实
  - Heejin Do — 单位未核实
  - Jianing Qiu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large Language Models (LLMs) show great potential as clinical agents, yet existing benchmarks reduce clinical workflows to static predictions or unconstrained Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with coarse action sets. To address this, we introduce GPAgentBench-2K, the first Constrained MDP (CMDP) LLM-agent benchmark for primary-care clinical decision-making, constructed from expert-validated records of real-world GP encounters. Our environment models a full spectrum of six foundational clinical actions, imposes a topological workflow prior over the action space, and operationalizes safety-informed abstention as a first-class outcome. Evaluating 16 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals a significant performance degradation as the action space scales. Crucially, we uncover a clinical quality-safety gap: even frontier models with the highest diagnosis accuracy violate safety constraints in over half of high-risk cases. Finally, we establish a reference point using Constrained Group Relative Policy Optimization (C-GRPO), and show that while explicitly modeling constraints improves performance over unconstrained RL methods, it remains far from clinically acceptable safety.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）作为临床 Agent 展现出很大潜力，但现有 benchmark 会把临床 workflow 简化为静态预测，或具有粗粒度 action set 的无约束 Markov Decision Process（MDP）。为此，我们提出 GPAgentBench-2K，这是首个用于初级保健临床决策的 Constrained MDP（CMDP）LLM-Agent benchmark，由经专家验证的真实全科医生诊疗记录构建。我们的环境建模六种基础临床动作的完整范围，在 action space 上施加 topological workflow prior，并把 safety-informed abstention 操作化为一等结果。对 16 个 SOTA LLM 的评估显示，action space 扩大时性能显著下降。更关键的是，我们揭示了一项 clinical quality-safety gap：即使诊断准确率最高的 frontier model，在超过一半的高风险案例中仍违反安全约束。最后，我们使用 Constrained Group Relative Policy Optimization（C-GRPO）建立参考点，并表明，虽然显式建模约束相较无约束 RL 方法能提升性能，但离临床可接受的安全水平仍很远。

### 26. The Intervention Gap in Latent World Models

**内容概述：** 针对 learned world model 的 reward fit 不能说明其 imagined intervention 是否匹配环境真实作用，论文提出 capture-gated matched-intervention audit，发现多个 checkpoint 的五步想象 effect 甚至差于“无 effect”预测，失效源于 task-direction rotation 与 excess gain，且 disagreement 仅在训练支持附近有效。

- 收录理由：论文直接审计用于规划的 world model 是否忠实响应 intervention，并区分 query capture、effect decoding 与 rollout distortion，为高后果预测式控制提供负面机制证据。
- 论文/arXiv：[The Intervention Gap in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29998)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29998`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：21 pages, 10 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Donna Vakalis — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Planning-time intervention fidelity is a distinct, measurable property of a learned world model: whether the model's own open-loop transitions move task variables the way matched environment interventions do. In the settings we test, it is neither revealed by reward fit nor ensured by task-anchored training. Across released TD-MPC2 checkpoint sizes, episode return falls as an operator-error diagnostic on task observables grows, while reward-prediction error stays small and nearly flat, and a self-supervised world model trained without task signal preserves the same operator substantially better than a task-anchored model on the shared task. A capture-gated matched-intervention audit then localizes what fails. On Cheetah, three LeWorldModel checkpoints capture the current task query and support decodable real intervention effects; however, their imagined five-step effects are worse than predicting no effect and worse than an environment-endpoint oracle. The failure is task-direction rotation with excess gain, not feature collapse. This severe pattern is conditional: five PreJEPA seeds retain an oracle-relative deficit without it, Finger Spin experiments extend the deficit beyond locomotion with heterogeneous severity across seeds, and shared-bank effect geometry is both candidate- and support-dependent. We also test practice-side questions. In DreamerV3 the posterior distribution, not its sample, carries the current query; ensemble disagreement ranks error only near training support; and a frozen support-aware score degrades held-out error ranking in both tested transfer directions while native disagreement remains informative in both. We conclude that intervention fidelity must be audited directly, capture-first, on the model's native interface.
- 中文摘要：Planning-time intervention fidelity 是 learned world model 一项独立且可测的性质：模型自身的 open-loop transition 是否像匹配的环境 intervention 那样改变任务变量。在我们测试的设置中，reward fit 不能揭示它，task-anchored training 也不能保证它。在公开 TD-MPC2 checkpoint 的不同规模上，随着针对 task observable 的 operator-error diagnostic 增大，episode return 下降，而 reward-prediction error 保持很小且几乎不变；在同一任务上，一个不使用 task signal 训练的 self-supervised world model 对相同 operator 的保持显著优于 task-anchored model。随后，capture-gated matched-intervention audit 定位具体失效。在 Cheetah 上，三个 LeWorldModel checkpoint 能捕获当前 task query，也支持对真实 intervention effect 进行解码；然而，它们想象的五步 effect 比预测“无 effect”更差，也比 environment-endpoint oracle 更差。该失效是伴随 excess gain 的 task-direction rotation，而不是 feature collapse。这种严重模式是有条件的：五个 PreJEPA seed 相对 oracle 仍有 deficit，但没有上述模式；Finger Spin 实验把 deficit 扩展到 locomotion 之外，且不同 seed 的严重程度不一；shared-bank effect geometry 同时依赖 candidate 和 support。我们还测试了实践侧问题。在 DreamerV3 中，携带当前 query 的是 posterior distribution 而非其 sample；ensemble disagreement 只能在 training support 附近对 error 排序；frozen support-aware score 在两个被测迁移方向上都降低 held-out error ranking，而 native disagreement 在两者中仍有信息。我们得出结论：必须在模型原生接口上，以 capture-first 方式直接审计 intervention fidelity。

### 27. Self-Aware Active Learning Enables Continual Improvement in Autonomous Driving

**内容概述：** 针对 autonomous-driving Agent 无法判断自身能力边界并从罕见危险事件中学习，论文提出 SAGE，以 world model 生成 fear 风险信号和 curiosity 新颖度信号、动态校准接管阈值并将接管轨迹用于 imitation learning，在仿真、Waymo 日志、CARLA hazard 与实机机器人上减少安全违规且保持任务性能。

- 收录理由：论文将 learned world model 的在线风险预测直接连接到 expert／fallback takeover 和后训练约束，满足具身决策模块 runtime assurance 与 safety-critical adaptation 的收录条件。
- 论文/arXiv：[Self-Aware Active Learning Enables Continual Improvement in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29772)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29772`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.RO`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Dong Hu — 单位未核实
  - Chao Huang — 单位未核实
  - Carman K. M. Lee — 单位未核实
  - Dimitrios Kanoulas — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Learning-based autonomous driving (AD) systems can perform reliably in familiar conditions, yet rare distribution shifts and long-tail events remain a major source of abrupt failure. A central limitation is that most agents learn primarily from passive experience and lack mechanisms to estimate when their competence is insufficient, seek timely assistance, and convert safety-critical encounters into targeted improvement. Here we present self-aware guided exploration (SAGE), an active learning framework for post-training adaptation in AD. SAGE learns a predictive world model that generates two online intrinsic signals: fear, which estimates short-horizon predictive risk and model uncertainty, and curiosity, which measures novelty through prediction error. Curiosity adaptively calibrates the intervention threshold for fear, allowing the agent to regulate risk in a context-dependent manner. When predicted fear exceeds this adaptive threshold, the agent transfers control to an expert or fallback policy and uses the resulting takeover trajectories for focused imitation learning. In parallel, fear is integrated into policy optimization and evaluation as a safety-oriented constraint to reduce performance regressions during adaptation. We evaluate SAGE in simulated route-transfer tasks, Waymo-based logged driving scenarios, CARLA occlusion hazards, and real-world mobile robot navigation tests. Across these settings, SAGE improves robustness in novel and safety-critical scenarios, reduces safety violations, and maintains task performance comparable to strong baseline policies. These results suggest that agents can improve after initial training by estimating the limits of their competence, requesting guidance when needed, and learning selectively from rare high-value events.
- 中文摘要：基于学习的自动驾驶（AD）系统在熟悉条件下可以可靠运行，但罕见 distribution shift 和 long-tail event 仍是突发失败的主要来源。核心局限是，多数 Agent 主要从被动经验中学习，缺少机制来估计自身能力何时不足、及时寻求帮助，并把 safety-critical encounter 转化为定向改进。我们提出 self-aware guided exploration（SAGE），一个用于 AD post-training adaptation 的 active learning framework。SAGE 学习一个 predictive world model，生成两个在线 intrinsic signal：fear，用于估计短期预测风险和模型 uncertainty；curiosity，通过 prediction error 测量新颖性。Curiosity 自适应校准 fear 的 intervention threshold，使 Agent 能依据上下文调节风险。当预测 fear 超过这一动态阈值时，Agent 把控制权交给 expert 或 fallback policy，并用所得 takeover trajectory 进行 focused imitation learning。同时，fear 作为 safety-oriented constraint 被整合进 policy optimization 和 evaluation，以减少 adaptation 期间的性能退化。我们在模拟 route-transfer task、基于 Waymo 的 logged driving scenario、CARLA occlusion hazard 和真实 mobile robot navigation test 中评估 SAGE。在这些设置中，SAGE 提高了新颖及 safety-critical 场景中的稳健性，减少安全违规，并保持与强基线相当的任务性能。结果表明，Agent 可以通过估计自身能力边界、在需要时请求指导，并从罕见高价值事件中选择性学习，在初始训练后继续改进。

### 28. Beyond Token-Level Guidance: Inference-Time Alignment of Specialized LLMs via Cross-Family Representation Steering

**内容概述：** 针对专业化微调削弱安全、现有 token-level guidance 又因跨模型能力正交和 stop-token interference 损伤领域能力，论文提出 CREST，从任意系列 guidance model 提取安全方向并在 hidden representation 上 steering，安全 benchmark 最高优于基线 22.2%，同时保持领域能力与原本已对齐模型的安全性。

- 收录理由：论文给出 specialized model 的 inference-time realignment 机制，定位跨系列 guidance 的结构性失效并绕开 token-level 控制，直接对应 harmful fine-tuning 后的安全恢复。
- 论文/arXiv：[Beyond Token-Level Guidance: Inference-Time Alignment of Specialized LLMs via Cross-Family Representation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30319)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30319`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/DecayingSeart/CREST)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`、`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：Accepted by EMNLP 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Jin Gan — 单位未核实
  - Xin Li — 单位未核实
  - Jun Luo — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models (LLMs) finetuned for specialized domains represent crucial high-impact applications. Inference-time alignment improves safety degraded from specialization finetuning without requiring substantial computational resources, complementing finetuning-based methods with an easy-to-use, plug-and-play solution. However, existing inference-time methods fail to reliably improve safety without disrupting domain capability. We identify the root cause as complementary expertise orthogonality: specialized base models and general-domain guidance models have orthogonal competencies, making the guidance signal unreliable for specialized generation. This primarily manifests as stop token interference, where the guidance model's tendency toward continuation overrides the base model's decision to stop, burying correct answers under guidance-induced continuation. To address this problem, we propose CREST, an inference-time alignment method that steers base model hidden representations using safety directions extracted from a guidance model of any family, avoiding token-level structural limitations entirely. CREST improves safety where specialization has weakened it while preserving both domain-specific capability and the safety of already well-aligned models, outperforming baselines by up to 22.2\% on safety benchmarks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/DecayingSeart/CREST.
- 中文摘要：为专业领域微调的大型语言模型（LLM）代表着关键的高影响应用。Inference-time alignment 无需大量计算资源即可改善 specialization finetuning 所削弱的安全性，为 finetuning-based 方法提供易用的 plug-and-play 补充。然而，现有 inference-time 方法无法在不扰乱领域能力的情况下可靠提升安全。我们识别出根因是 complementary expertise orthogonality：specialized base model 和 general-domain guidance model 的能力相互正交，使 guidance signal 对专业生成并不可靠。这主要表现为 stop token interference，即 guidance model 的续写倾向覆盖 base model 停止生成的决定，把正确答案埋在 guidance 所诱发的续写之后。为解决这一问题，我们提出 CREST，一种 inference-time alignment 方法，它使用从任意系列 guidance model 提取的 safety direction 对 base model hidden representation 进行 steering，从而完全避开 token-level 结构限制。CREST 在专业化削弱安全性的地方提升安全，同时保持领域特定能力以及本已良好对齐模型的安全性；在 safety benchmark 上，它最多比基线高 22.2%。代码位于 https://github.com/DecayingSeart/CREST。

### 29. ALTSTEER: Selective Safety Steering for Moving Beyond Hard Refusals to Constructive Alternatives

**内容概述：** 针对 activation steering 的触发信号跨领域不稳定且常把安全控制退化为僵硬拒答，论文提出 ALTSTEER，以内部 refusal signal 选择性介入并分阶段把生成从拒答重定向到建设性替代方案，在 Llama-3.1 与 Qwen2.5 上提升 constructive safe completion 且保持 benign utility。

- 收录理由：论文同时处理何时触发与触发后如何生成两个 guardrail 问题，提供无需训练、单次 inference 的选择性安全 steering，直接关注 over-refusal 与 helpful safety 的权衡。
- 论文/arXiv：[ALTSTEER: Selective Safety Steering for Moving Beyond Hard Refusals to Constructive Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30197)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30197`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.SE`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：Accepted at EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- 作者及单位：
  - Hoejoon Kwon — 单位未核实
  - Byeonggeuk Lim — 单位未核实
  - Kahyeon Kim — 单位未核实
  - YoungBin Kim — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Safety alignment is essential for deploying large language models, requiring systems to prevent harmful compliance while preserving helpfulness on benign requests. Activation steering offers a training-free inference-time approach to safety control, but effective safety steering requires addressing two coupled questions: when to intervene and how generation should be shaped after intervention. However, existing safety steering methods remain limited along both dimensions, as their triggering mechanisms can be unstable across domains and refusal-oriented steering often yields rigid refusals rather than constructive safe guidance. To address these limitations, we propose ALTSTEER, an inference-time framework that couples selective intervention with refusal-anchored constructive redirection within a single inference pass. ALTSTEER uses an internal refusal-relevant signal to decide when to steer, and applies staged steering to shift generation from refusal-oriented control toward constructive alternatives. Evaluations on Llama-3.1 and Qwen2.5 show that ALTSTEER preserves benign utility while improving constructive safe-completion behavior, especially on models that otherwise tend to produce short refusals for harmful requests.
- 中文摘要：Safety alignment 对部署大型语言模型至关重要，要求系统既阻止 harmful compliance，也保留对 benign request 的 helpfulness。Activation steering 提供一种无需训练的 inference-time 安全控制方法，但有效的 safety steering 必须同时回答两个相互耦合的问题：何时干预，以及干预后应如何塑造生成。然而，现有 safety steering 方法在两个维度上都有限：触发机制可能跨领域不稳定，而面向拒绝的 steering 常产生僵硬拒答，无法提供建设性的安全指导。为解决这些局限，我们提出 ALTSTEER，一个在单次 inference pass 中结合 selective intervention 与 refusal-anchored constructive redirection 的 inference-time framework。ALTSTEER 使用内部 refusal-relevant signal 决定何时 steering，并采用 staged steering，把生成从 refusal-oriented control 转向 constructive alternative。对 Llama-3.1 和 Qwen2.5 的评估表明，ALTSTEER 在保持 benign utility 的同时改善 constructive safe-completion behavior，尤其适用于原本倾向对 harmful request 作简短拒答的模型。

### 30. TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization

**内容概述：** 针对 jailbreak suffix optimization 每步只保留当前 loss 最低候选而产生 selection-stage reward hacking，论文提出 trajectory-aware candidate selection，以跨步 proxy、reference-policy regularization 和判别器估计的 chi-squared correction 稳定选择，在同等预算下持续提高 HarmBench ASR。

- 收录理由：论文揭示 jailbreak 搜索中候选选择而非生成本身的隐藏瓶颈，并给出更强、轨迹感知的 suffix attack，可用于压力测试现有拒答与 guardrail。
- 论文/arXiv：[TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29564)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29564`

- 英文摘要：Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose \OURS{}, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, \OURS{} augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that \OURS{} consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
- 中文摘要：基于梯度的 jailbreak suffix optimization 方法通常通过保留当前 loss 最低的候选来更新 suffix。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤 proxy 下看起来更好的候选，之后在搜索中往往不能产生更好的 jailbreak 结果，这揭示出一种 selection-stage reward hacking。该发现说明，candidate selection 而非仅 candidate generation，是 suffix optimization 的隐藏瓶颈。为解决这一问题，我们提出 \OURS{}，一个用于 jailbreak suffix optimization 的 trajectory-aware candidate selection framework。\OURS{} 不再只依据即时 loss 选择候选，而是用 trajectory-aware proxy 增强逐步评估，并用 reference-policy regularization 和 discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction 稳定选择，从而鼓励在当前步骤之后仍有效的决策。HarmBench 实验表明，在相同搜索预算下，\OURS{} 持续优于强基线，显著提高攻击成功率，并在整个搜索过程中呈现更稳定的优化行为。我们的发现强调，要改进 jailbreak suffix optimization，缓解短视候选选择造成的 selection-stage reward hacking 至关重要。

### 31. A Causal Model for Locating and Unlocking Sandbagging in Model Organisms

**内容概述：** 针对 sandbagging 会使 frontier evaluation 低估模型能力且内部机制不清，论文建立 residual stream 因果模型，定位早层写入意图、后层提交答案的窗口；single-layer reference graft 在 33 次中的 28 次恢复能力、中位 held-out recovery 为 96%，而 circuit-breaking 需改用 context grafting 才能恢复。

- 收录理由：论文把战略性低表现定位到可干预的内部通路，并比较 prompt、fine-tuning、RL 与 circuit-breaking 四种 lock，为 evaluation evasion 的机制审计提供因果证据。
- 论文/arXiv：[A Causal Model for Locating and Unlocking Sandbagging in Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29461)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29461`

- 英文摘要：Sandbagging models strategically underperform on evaluations while retaining the capabilities being measured. The evaluations that guide frontier-model deployment and governance then understate what these models can do. To understand the mechanism, we propose a causal model of how sandbagging is carried in the residual stream. Early layers write the sandbagging intent onto a single axis of the stream, and a later layer reads that axis and commits the answer. We study three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Mistral-7B) and four ways of installing a sandbagging lock (prompting, fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and circuit breaking). Each lock decides from the prompt whether to sandbag, and the fine-tuned and circuit-broken locks answer honestly whenever a password appears. The causal model predicts a window of layers, after the last sandbagging write and before the answer commit, in which a single-layer reference graft of the sandbagging axis to its honest value restores the full capability. The single-layer graft recovers the capability in 28 of the 33 runs of the prompted, fine-tuned, and RL-trained locks, with a median held-out recovery of 96%. The circuit-broken lock rewrites the whole state through a band of layers, and the single-layer graft fails at every layer. We therefore introduce a second intervention, context grafting, which replays the password's cached key/value activations so that every layer's attention reads them as additional context. Context grafting provably and empirically restores the full capability on all three models, and the recovery is surprisingly insensitive to the exact password content. More broadly, an auditor can use this causal model to design interventional auditing techniques for sandbagging models.
- 中文摘要：Sandbagging model 会在评估中策略性降低表现，同时保留被测能力，于是指导 frontier model 部署和治理的评估会低估这些模型能做什么。为理解其机制，我们提出一个关于 sandbagging 如何由 residual stream 承载的因果模型。早期层把 sandbagging intent 写入 residual stream 的单一轴，后续层读取该轴并提交答案。我们研究三个 instruction-tuned model（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3-8B 和 Mistral-7B）以及四种安装 sandbagging lock 的方式（prompting、fine-tuning、reinforcement learning 和 circuit breaking）。每种 lock 都根据 prompt 决定是否 sandbag；fine-tuned 和 circuit-broken lock 在密码出现时会诚实作答。因果模型预测出一个层窗口：它位于最后一次 sandbagging 写入之后、答案提交之前；在该窗口内，把 sandbagging axis 上的 activation 坐标 reference graft 到 honest value，即可恢复完整能力。对于 prompted、fine-tuned 和 RL-trained lock，single-layer graft 在 33 次运行中的 28 次恢复能力，held-out recovery 中位数为 96%。Circuit-broken lock 会跨一段层区间重写整个状态，因此 single-layer graft 在每一层都失败。我们由此提出第二种 intervention：context grafting，它 replay 密码的 cached key/value activation，使每一层 attention 都把它们作为附加 context 读取。Context grafting 在理论和实验上都恢复了三个模型的完整能力，而且恢复效果对具体密码内容出人意料地不敏感。更广泛地说，auditor 可以用这一因果模型为 sandbagging model 设计 interventional auditing technique。

### 32. Reference-Grafting Matches Fine-Tuning at Eliciting Sandbagged Capabilities

**内容概述：** 针对此前 additive steering 无法诱出被 sandbagging 隐藏的能力，论文提出在 active-learning 选择的少量 circuit 上把 contrast coordinate 设为 honest reference 值，跨 11 个 password-locked model 恢复 honest–sandbagging gap 的 94%–101%，但 circuit-breaking 会在下游重新破坏固定编辑而形成失效边界。

- 收录理由：论文提供无需权重更新或训练标签的 hidden-capability elicitation，并证明方向跟随 sandbagging 的安装方式而非被隐藏内容，对 frontier safety evaluation 的抗规避审计直接相关。
- 论文/arXiv：[Reference-Grafting Matches Fine-Tuning at Eliciting Sandbagged Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29458)

- 英文摘要：Sandbagging, in which a model deliberately underperforms on an evaluation despite retaining the underlying capability, threatens the safety evaluations that frontier-model governance depends on. The Elicitation Game found that fine-tuning elicits hidden capability from sandbagging model organisms whereas additive activation steering fails. We revisit that verdict with reference-grafting, which sets an activation's coordinate along a contrast direction to the value it takes in an honest reference, at a small set of circuits chosen by active learning. Across eleven fine-tuned password-locked models (1.5-32B, three architecture lineages), it recovers +94 to +101% of the honest-sandbagging gap, matching fine-tuning elicitation without weight updates or training labels; two to five paired examples suffice to fit the direction. Similar recovery holds for reinforcement-learning-induced sandbagging and for password-locked code generation. Grafting works because the fine-tuned lock is a thresholded gate: held-out accuracy stays at the sandbagged level until the grafted coordinate crosses a threshold near the honest reference, which is why additive steering and zeroing the coordinate often fail. The direction tracks how the sandbagging was induced rather than what is withheld -- fit on grade-school science (ARC) it elicits withheld hazardous knowledge (WMDP), yet prompting, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning each carry a different direction. Circuit-breaking marks the boundary: it reroutes activations on every forward pass, so the fixed edits we test are re-broken downstream and do not restore coherent generation.
- 中文摘要：Sandbagging 指模型尽管保留底层能力，却在评估中故意降低表现；它威胁 frontier-model governance 所依赖的安全评估。Elicitation Game 发现，fine-tuning 能从 sandbagging model organism 中诱出隐藏能力，而 additive activation steering 失败。我们用 reference-grafting 重新审视这一结论：在由 active learning 选择的少量 circuit 上，把 activation 沿 contrast direction 的坐标设为其在 honest reference 中的取值。在 11 个经 fine-tuning 得到的 password-locked model 上（1.5–32B，三个架构谱系），它恢复 honest–sandbagging gap 的 94%–101%，无需权重更新或训练标签即可匹配 fine-tuning elicitation；两到五个配对样本足以拟合该方向。对 reinforcement-learning-induced sandbagging 和 password-locked code generation，也观察到相似恢复。Grafting 有效，是因为 fine-tuned lock 是 thresholded gate：在 grafted coordinate 越过接近 honest reference 的阈值前，held-out accuracy 一直停留在 sandbagged 水平，这也解释了 additive steering 和把坐标置零为何常常失败。该方向跟踪 sandbagging 如何被诱导，而不是隐藏了什么内容：在小学科学（ARC）上拟合后，它能诱出被隐藏的 hazardous knowledge（WMDP），但 prompting、fine-tuning 和 reinforcement learning 各自承载不同方向。Circuit-breaking 划定了方法边界：它在每次 forward pass 都重新路由 activation，所以我们测试的固定编辑会在下游再次被破坏，无法恢复连贯生成。

### 33. Can escalation channels redirect reward hacking toward defect disclosure?

**内容概述：** 针对 coding Agent 遇到缺陷测试设施时会硬编码输出或篡改测试，论文提供 conflict-point escalation channel 引导其报告缺陷；八个 frontier model 上，结合 escalation tool 与 policy 将 reward hacking 从 23.6% 降至 5.3%，并额外提高 10.1 个百分点的缺陷发现覆盖率且无可检测性能开销。

- 收录理由：论文没有只做 prompt 禁令，而是改变 Agent 决策环境，把识别并利用缺陷的能力重定向为结构化披露，给出 reward hacking 的运行时干预与诊断价值。
- 论文/arXiv：[Can escalation channels redirect reward hacking toward defect disclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29460)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29460`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`、`cs.CR`、`cs.CY`

- arXiv Comments：9 pages
- 作者及单位：
  - Francesca Gomez — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：When coding agents encounter defective test infrastructure they may reward-hack: hardcoding outputs or editing test files to pass tests they cannot legitimately satisfy, a pattern that has now appeared outside benchmarks, in a coordinated multi-agent intrusion of a major AI platform's production infrastructure. The same capability that lets an agent detect and exploit a defect could let it report one, given the right decision environment. We evaluate escalation channels, structured reporting tools available to the agent at the point of conflict, as a decision-environment intervention that both reduces reward hacking and surfaces the infrastructure defects that trigger it. A $2 \times 2$ factorial separates the contributions of an escalation tool, a standalone anti-reward-hacking policy, and their combination. Across 8 frontier models spanning 5 families, the combined intervention reduces reward hacking from 23.6\% to 5.3\% (mixed-effects logistic OR = 9.2, 95\% CI 5.0--16.8, $p < 10^{-12}$) with no detectable cost or performance overhead, eliminating it entirely for 6 of 8 models. Escalation and hacking are near-perfectly mutually exclusive, with 96.8\% of escalations involving no hacking. Beyond reduction, escalation channels function as diagnostic infrastructure: on top of monitoring, escalation adds +10.1 percentage points of defect detection coverage and is more accurate once it fires (99.4\% vs 85.8\%). Unlike containment-based approaches that risk outpacing growing model capabilities, escalation channels redirect capability toward disclosure rather than exploitation.
- 中文摘要：当 coding Agent 遇到有缺陷的测试基础设施时，它们可能 reward-hack：通过硬编码输出或编辑测试文件，让本不能合法满足的测试通过。这种模式如今已出现在 benchmark 之外，即某主要 AI 平台生产基础设施遭遇的一次协同 multi-agent intrusion 中。让 Agent 发现并利用缺陷的同一种能力，在适当的决策环境下也可用来报告缺陷。我们评估 escalation channel，即在冲突点向 Agent 提供的结构化报告工具；它是一种既减少 reward hacking、又暴露触发该行为的基础设施缺陷的决策环境干预。一个 $2 \times 2$ factorial design 分离 escalation tool、独立 anti-reward-hacking policy 及二者组合的贡献。在覆盖五个系列的八个 frontier model 上，组合干预将 reward hacking 从 23.6% 降至 5.3%（mixed-effects logistic OR 为 9.2，95% CI 为 5.0–16.8，$p<10^{-12}$），没有可检测的成本或性能开销，并在八个模型中的六个上完全消除该行为。Escalation 与 hacking 几乎完全互斥：96.8% 的 escalation 不伴随 hacking。除降低 reward hacking 外，escalation channel 还能充当诊断基础设施：在 monitoring 之外，escalation 使缺陷检测覆盖率增加 10.1 个百分点，且一旦触发便更准确（99.4% 对 85.8%）。Containment-based 方法可能跟不上不断增长的模型能力，而 escalation channel 将能力从利用缺陷重定向至披露缺陷。

### 34. Automated Researchers Can Reliably Mitigate Alignment Failures

**内容概述：** 针对 automated alignment research 是否真能修复可测错位缺少直接证据，论文让 AAR 自主提出训练方法和数据以同时优化十类安全失败，最强方法在 held-out benchmark、多轮行为审计和大 4.7 倍的模型上泛化，并优于 28 名限时八小时的人类研究者基线。

- 收录理由：论文把 automated researcher 置于真实 alignment intervention 循环中，联合测量 deception、sycophancy、jailbreak 等目标失败与通用能力保持，直接评估自动化安全研究的实际效能。

- 英文摘要：Automating alignment research may accelerate progress toward aligned AI, but whether it does is hard to measure. Luckily, many alignment failures, such as deception, sycophancy, and jailbreaks, are already measurable by public benchmarks. We study whether automated alignment researchers (AARs) can post-train to mitigate alignment failures by proposing training methods and data to simultaneously optimize multiple safety benchmarks, while preserving general capability. Across 10 alignment failures, the strongest AAR methods significantly reduce the targeted alignment failures and generalize to a held-out benchmark, multi-turn behavioral audits, and models up to 4.7 times larger than the target model. As a human baseline, 28 experienced researchers receive up to eight hours to develop methods for the same benchmarks, but their methods underperform the best AAR methods. Using human ideas as the AARs' initial research direction does not improve performance, suggesting current AARs may not need guidance from experienced researchers. These results suggest that automating alignment research on well-characterized failures may be practical in the near term.
- 中文摘要：自动化 alignment research 可能加速实现 aligned AI，但它是否真的有效很难测量。幸运的是，deception、sycophancy 和 jailbreak 等许多 alignment failure 已能通过公开 benchmark 测量。我们研究 automated alignment researcher（AAR）能否提出训练方法和数据，通过 post-training 在保留通用能力的同时优化多个 safety benchmark，从而缓解 alignment failure。在十种 alignment failure 上，最强 AAR 方法显著减少目标失败，并泛化到 held-out benchmark、多轮行为审计，以及规模最高为目标模型 4.7 倍的模型。作为人类基线，28 名有经验的研究者获得最多八小时，为相同 benchmark 开发方法，但其方法不及最佳 AAR 方法。把人类想法用作 AAR 的初始研究方向也没有改善表现，说明当前 AAR 可能不需要有经验研究者的指导。结果表明，针对已被良好刻画的 failure 自动开展 alignment research，近期可能具有实践可行性。

### 35. Emergent Misalignment Is Not Magical

**内容概述：** 针对 emergent misalignment 常被解释为通用“邪恶方向”或 persona 变化而机制含糊，论文将其刻画为数据依赖的可预测泛化：12 个模型—数据集设置中，评测 prompt 距训练数据中心越近越易诱发错位，平均 Spearman 相关为 −0.73，且不存在跨 EM 模型通用的 misalignment direction。

- 收录理由：论文校准 harmful fine-tuning 的实际 threat mechanism，区分数据格式、表示距离、dataset-specific direction 与 persona shift，避免把 EM 过度归因为统一错位表征。
- 论文/arXiv：[Emergent Misalignment Is Not Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29118)

- 英文摘要：Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly harmful datasets can lead to misalignment broadly, a phenomenon known as emergent misalignment (EM). EM poses a challenge for AI safety and our understanding of LLMs. Prior work often frames EM as an unexpected behavior, and explains it by appealing to general misalignment directions or anthropomorphizing it as acquiring an evil persona. However, the mechanisms behind these framings remain obscure. In this work, we show that EM is a predictable and data-dependent generalization phenomenon. By examining the base model's representation of EM training data and evaluation prompts, we find that evilness after EM training is highly predictable from representational distance: the closer an evaluation prompt is to training data centroid, the more evilness it elicits from EM models after training (with an average Spearman correlation of -0.73 across 12 model-dataset settings). Building upon this analysis, we further demystify EM by showing that (1) its effectiveness changes significantly based on training data format; (2) there is not a general misalignment direction that transfers across different EM models; (3) the effect of EM is fundamentally different from persona changes. Furthermore, we extend the EM generalization metric from a scalar distance to a dataset-specific generalization direction, which robustly predicts EM models' evilness under semantics-preserving prompt perturbations including appending random tokens and paraphrasing, where other methods do not reliably generalize.
- 中文摘要：在狭窄的 harmful dataset 上 fine-tune 大型语言模型（LLM），可能导致广泛错位，这一现象被称为 emergent misalignment（EM）。EM 对 AI safety 和我们理解 LLM 构成挑战。此前工作常把 EM 描述成意外行为，并诉诸通用 misalignment direction，或将其拟人化为获得 evil persona；但这些解释背后的机制仍不清楚。本文表明，EM 是一种可预测且依赖数据的泛化现象。通过考察 base model 对 EM 训练数据和评估 prompt 的表示，我们发现，EM 训练后的 evilness 可由 representational distance 高度预测：评估 prompt 越接近训练数据中心，训练后的 EM model 就表现出越强的 evilness（12 个 model-dataset setting 上平均 Spearman 相关为 −0.73）。在此分析基础上，我们进一步消解 EM 的神秘性，并表明：（1）其效果会随训练数据格式显著变化；（2）不存在可在不同 EM model 间迁移的通用 misalignment direction；（3）EM 的作用从根本上不同于 persona change。此外，我们把 EM generalization metric 从标量距离扩展为 dataset-specific generalization direction；在添加随机 token、paraphrasing 等保持语义的 prompt perturbation 下，该方向仍能稳健预测 EM model 的 evilness，而其他方法不能可靠泛化。

### 36. When Safety Speaks a Language: A Mechanistic Analysis of Safety-Language Identity Entanglement in LLMs

**内容概述：** 针对 multilingual safety 退化的内部原因不清，论文在三个 instruction-tuned LLM、八种语言和全部层上用 SAE feature 分析 harmful／harmless 表示，发现 safety feature 的位置与共享模式依赖架构且同 language identity 几何纠缠，消融安全 feature 会同时改变有害响应率和目标语言。

- 收录理由：论文对跨语言 guardrail 不均衡给出可干预的表示机制，并揭示安全 intervention 可能连带破坏语言身份，为低资源语言 refusal steering 提供关键边界。
- 论文/arXiv：[When Safety Speaks a Language: A Mechanistic Analysis of Safety-Language Identity Entanglement in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29936)

- 英文摘要：Safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) degrades across languages, yet the internal mechanism driving this asymmetry remains poorly understood. Our work, therefore, presents a systematic mechanistic analysis of multilingual safety using sparse autoencoder (SAE) features, sparse interpretable directions in the residual stream associated with harmful and harmless model behavior across three instruction-tuned LLMs, eight languages, and all model layers. We observe that safety-relevant features are architecture-dependent in terms of where they are located and how they are distributed across layers. Additionally, they are geometrically entangled with language identity and exhibit cross-lingual sharing patterns, i.e., languages share safety features to varying degrees across model depths and architectures. This safety-language entanglement has direct consequences such that ablating safety features impacts not only harmful response rates but also target language, with the degree of intervention predicted by the relationship between safety and language features. Our findings qualify the language-universality of safety alignment as architecture-dependent and offer a mechanistic account of multilingual safety interventions.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）的 safety alignment 会跨语言退化，但驱动这种不对称性的内部机制仍不清楚。因此，我们使用 sparse autoencoder（SAE）feature 对 multilingual safety 进行系统机制分析；这些 feature 是 residual stream 中与 harmful 和 harmless 模型行为相关的稀疏可解释方向，分析覆盖三个 instruction-tuned LLM、八种语言和全部模型层。我们观察到，safety-relevant feature 位于何处以及如何分布在层间都依赖架构。此外，它们在几何上与 language identity 纠缠，并呈现 cross-lingual sharing pattern，即不同语言在不同模型深度和架构中以不同程度共享 safety feature。这种 safety-language entanglement 会产生直接后果：消融 safety feature 不仅影响 harmful response rate，也会影响目标语言；干预程度可由 safety feature 与 language feature 的关系预测。我们的发现表明，safety alignment 的语言普适性受架构制约，并为 multilingual safety intervention 提供机制解释。

### 37. Arabic Safety Alignment as Selective Refusal: An Empirical Study of SFT, DPO, and Guard Calibration

**内容概述：** 针对单一 refusal rate 掩盖阿拉伯语模型 harmful refusal 与 benign over-refusal 权衡，论文分别定义 B 与 H 并评测五个模型；refusal-only SFT 退化为 blanket refusal，精选 mixed-SFT 可在 B=14%–23% 时达 H=90%–93%，但向 Arabizi 的迁移均未达到 90%。

- 收录理由：论文以人工 AraSafe、重复运行和盲审校准阿拉伯语 refusal guard，明确要求按模型选择部署 operating point，而不是用总体拒答率掩盖安全与效用差异。
- 论文/arXiv：[Arabic Safety Alignment as Selective Refusal: An Empirical Study of SFT, DPO, and Guard Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29378)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29378`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：The Fourth Arabic Natural Language Processing Conference(ArabicNLP 2026)
- 作者及单位：
  - Mohamad Zbib — 单位未核实
  - Ammar Mohanna — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Arabic large language models must refuse harmful prompts without over-refusing benign or sensitive prompts, yet a single refusal rate hides this trade-off. We evaluate it using benign refusal B and harmful-prompt refusal H, where H measures refusal rather than harmful compliance. Across five Arabic-capable models and 130 runs on the full human-written AraSafe set, refusal-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT) collapses toward blanket refusal, whereas selected mixed-SFT configurations reach H = 90% to 93% at B = 14% to 23%; four selected configurations exceed the H = 90% target in all three runs, while Fanar does so in two of three. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and inference guards change B and H differently across models rather than acting as uniform upgrades. In a blinded 300-response audit, annotator binary-refusal agreement is 89.0% (kappa = 0.78); Qwen3Guard and Aya Expanse 32B reach 88.7% and 91.0% accuracy, respectively, with no conclusive paired difference. Selected SFT raises H on Arabizi for all five models, but none reaches 90%, showing only partial transfer from Modern Standard Arabic. Overall, the results support model-specific operating-point selection: set a deployment target and retain only interventions that improve it.
- 中文摘要：阿拉伯语大型语言模型必须拒绝 harmful prompt，同时不能对 benign 或 sensitive prompt 过度拒答，但单一 refusal rate 会掩盖这种权衡。我们用 benign refusal $B$ 和 harmful-prompt refusal $H$ 进行评估，其中 $H$ 测量拒答而不是 harmful compliance。在五个支持阿拉伯语的模型和完整人工编写 AraSafe 集上的 130 次运行中，仅使用 refusal 的 supervised fine-tuning（SFT）会退化为 blanket refusal；而精选 mixed-SFT 配置可在 $B=14\%$–$23\%$ 时达到 $H=90\%$–$93\%$。四个精选配置在三次运行中都超过 $H=90\%$ 的目标，Fanar 则在三次中有两次达到。Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）和 inference guard 对不同模型的 $B$ 与 $H$ 产生不同改变，并非统一升级。在对 300 个响应的盲审中，标注者对二元 refusal 的一致率为 89.0%（kappa=0.78）；Qwen3Guard 和 Aya Expanse 32B 的准确率分别为 88.7% 和 91.0%，配对差异没有定论。精选 SFT 在五个模型上都提高了 Arabizi 的 $H$，但没有一个达到 90%，表明从 Modern Standard Arabic 的迁移只有部分成功。总体而言，结果支持针对具体模型选择 operating point：先设定部署目标，只保留能改善该目标的干预。

### 38. Pak3H: Evaluating the Cost of Cultural Mismatch in LLM Alignment with a Human-Contextualized Urdu Benchmark

**内容概述：** 针对现有 multilingual 3H benchmark 依赖机器翻译或 LLM 合成而传播源语言偏差，论文构建人工验证、文化语境化的 Urdu 套件 Pak3H，零样本评测显示本地语境下 helpfulness、harmlessness guardrail 和 honesty 均系统退化，尤其无法覆盖区域性安全风险。

- 收录理由：论文用 native-speaker adaptation 和 human validation 检验低资源语言 alignment 的真实部署落差，直接覆盖跨语言 guardrail、harmfulness 与文化错配，而非泛文化价值讨论。
- 论文/arXiv：[Pak3H: Evaluating the Cost of Cultural Mismatch in LLM Alignment with a Human-Contextualized Urdu Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30065)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30065`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：We introduce Pak3H, a human-validated Urdu benchmark for helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty. Zero-shot evaluations show LLM performance degrades across all three dimensions in low-resourced contextualized settings
- 作者及单位：
  - Abdullah Hashmat — 单位未核实
  - Usman Naseem — 单位未核实
  - Agha Ali Raza — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong Helpfulness, Harmlessness, and Honesty (3H) alignment in English-centric settings, but these gains transfer poorly to low-resource languages due to cultural mismatches. Existing multilingual 3H benchmarks rely predominantly on automated translation or LLM based synthesis, propagating source-language biases while sacrificing local relevance. To address this gap, we introduce Pak3H1, the first human-validated, culturally contextualized Urdu benchmark suite for 3H alignment, comprising PakAlpaca (helpfulness), PakBeaverTails (harmlessness), and PakTruthfulQA (honesty). Our multi-stage pipeline integrates manual cultural adaptation and dictionary-guided post editing to prioritize native speaker judgment, ensuring both semantic fidelity and contextual authenticity. Zero-shot evaluations across multiple open and proprietary LLM architectures reveal systematic cross-lingual alignment gaps: helpfulness win rates decline under localized contexts, harmlessness guardrails break down against regional safety risks, and composite honesty metrics degrade substantially due to localized factual constraints. These findings expose structural limitations in current alignment approaches, underscoring the necessity of human-guided localization for equitable multilingual evaluation.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）在以英语为中心的设置中展现出很强的 Helpfulness、Harmlessness 和 Honesty（3H）alignment，但由于文化错配，这些改进很难迁移到低资源语言。现有 multilingual 3H benchmark 主要依赖自动翻译或基于 LLM 的合成，在牺牲本地相关性的同时传播源语言偏差。为解决这一缺口，我们提出 Pak3H1，首个人工验证、文化语境化的 Urdu 3H alignment benchmark suite，包括 PakAlpaca（helpfulness）、PakBeaverTails（harmlessness）和 PakTruthfulQA（honesty）。我们的多阶段 pipeline 结合人工文化适配和字典引导的 post-editing，优先采用母语者判断，同时保证语义忠实度与语境真实性。对多个开放和专有 LLM 架构的 zero-shot evaluation 揭示出系统性的 cross-lingual alignment gap：本地化语境下 helpfulness win rate 下降，harmlessness guardrail 面对区域性安全风险时失效，而 composite honesty metric 因本地事实约束而显著退化。这些发现暴露了当前 alignment 方法的结构性局限，并强调 human-guided localization 对公平的 multilingual evaluation 必不可少。

### 39. Not Safe for All: Auditing the Dialect Penalty in Text-to-Image Safety Pipelines

**内容概述：** 针对 T2I safety filter 对非标准方言的不公平泛化，论文在五种英语方言的 23,080 对 prompt 上定义 dialect penalty，发现不同 filter 会相反地 over-flag 或 under-detect、最大 bias gap 为 28.29 个百分点；typo ablation 将根因定位为方言特征，group-balanced retraining 可缓解。

- 收录理由：论文同时测量 benign over-blocking 与 toxic under-detection，并把偏差定位到文本处理而非 pixel generator，给出可审计群体、危害路径与训练干预。
- 论文/arXiv：[Not Safe for All: Auditing the Dialect Penalty in Text-to-Image Safety Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29589)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29589`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/minguinho26/dialect-penalty-t2i)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：EMNLP 2026 Findings
- 作者及单位：
  - Minkyu Kim — 单位未核实
  - Juhwan Choi — 单位未核实
  - YoungBin Kim — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Text-to-image (T2I) safety guardrails fail to generalize equitably to non-standard dialects. Evaluating 23,080 paired prompts across five English dialects, we formalize this failure as the dialect penalty, where filters trigger based on linguistic surface features rather than semantic intent. Text-level filters fail in opposing directions: NSFW-T over-flags benign dialect prompts and LatentGuard over-flags toxic ones (bias gaps up to +28.29 pp), while the OpenAI Moderation API under-detects them. A controlled typo ablation confirms this penalty originates from flagging dialectal features, not generic out-of-distribution sensitivity. The pixel-level generator is largely dialect-agnostic; the penalty enters at text processing and cascades unevenly to post-hoc guardrails. We show this bias tracks training data imbalance and is mitigable via group-balanced retraining, with an ablation attributing the gain to balanced exposure rather than to the worst-group objective of GroupDRO (group distributionally robust optimization). Current pipelines systematically fail dialect speakers, an equity failure masked by mean accuracy benchmarks. Our official code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/minguinho26/dialect-penalty-t2i. Content Warning: This paper contains offensive, toxic, or disturbing text prompts and generated images.
- 中文摘要：Text-to-image（T2I）safety guardrail 无法公平地泛化到非标准方言。我们在五种英语方言的 23,080 对 prompt 上进行评估，把这一失效形式化为 dialect penalty：filter 根据语言表面特征而不是语义意图触发。Text-level filter 以相反方式失效：NSFW-T 对 benign dialect prompt 过度标记，LatentGuard 对 toxic prompt 过度标记（bias gap 最高达 +28.29 个百分点），而 OpenAI Moderation API 对它们检测不足。受控 typo ablation 证实，这种 penalty 源于对方言特征的标记，而不是一般的 out-of-distribution sensitivity。Pixel-level generator 基本不受方言影响；penalty 在文本处理阶段进入，并不均匀地传播到 post-hoc guardrail。我们表明，该偏差与训练数据不平衡相关，并可通过 group-balanced retraining 缓解；消融实验把改进归因于均衡 exposure，而非 GroupDRO（group distributionally robust optimization）的 worst-group objective。当前 pipeline 系统性地使方言使用者处于不利地位，这种公平失效被平均准确率 benchmark 掩盖。官方代码和数据集公开于 https://github.com/minguinho26/dialect-penalty-t2i。内容警告：本文包含冒犯性、有毒或令人不安的文本 prompt 和生成图像。

### 40. SafeAtlas-VL: Beyond Binary Multimodal Safety with Large-Scale Data and Guard Models

**内容概述：** 针对 multimodal safeguard 把图像、用户意图与助手行为压成单一二元判断，论文构建 150 万条五级风险数据 SafeAtlas-VL 和 5,000 条 held-out benchmark，并训练 target-conditioned Guard；8B 模型在未使用其他 benchmark 训练集时仍取得最佳总体表现，F1 约超此前 SOTA 4%。

- 收录理由：论文为多模态内容安全提供跨 image／request／response 的细粒度有序风险标签、连续分数和 disagreement-aware annotation，直接服务 guard model 训练与评测。
- 论文/arXiv：[SafeAtlas-VL: Beyond Binary Multimodal Safety with Large-Scale Data and Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29098)

- 英文摘要：Multimodal safety moderation requires distinguishing risks arising from visual content, user intent, and assistant behavior. Existing safeguards, however, are typically trained for a single judgment target and reduce safety assessment to a binary decision. Consequently, risk becomes difficult to compare across a multimodal interaction, and ambiguous cases are obscured. We introduce SafeAtlas-VL, a dataset of 1.5M training instances that places image-, request-, and response-level judgments on a five-level ordered scale. We curate a broad collection of safety-relevant data from both real-world and synthetic sources and apply a disagreement-aware annotation procedure. The resulting dataset spans 15 harm categories and 55 fine-grained subcategories, covering a broad range of multimodal safety scenarios. We also construct SafeAtlas-Bench, a held-out set of 5,000 instances for evaluating five-level predictions and continuous risk scores. Upon this dataset, we train the SafeAtlas Guard series of models via target-conditioned tuning for multimodal safety detection. Our models not only perform five-way classification of safety levels but also map safety to continuous scores through a soft cumulative ordinal head. Experimental results demonstrate that guard models trained on our dataset exhibit strong generalization: even without using the training sets of other benchmarks, they achieve competitive performance on the corresponding test sets. Notably, our 8B model attains the overall best performance, outperforming the previous SOTA by approximately 4% in F1 score. Code, data, and models are released to support further research. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive, harmful, graphic, or disturbing.
- 中文摘要：Multimodal safety moderation 需要区分来自视觉内容、用户意图和助手行为的风险。然而，现有 safeguard 通常只针对单一判断目标训练，并把安全评估简化为二元决策。因此，多模态交互中的风险难以比较，模糊案例也被掩盖。我们提出 SafeAtlas-VL，一个包含 150 万训练实例的数据集，在五级有序尺度上分别给出 image-level、request-level 和 response-level 判断。我们从真实和合成来源中整理广泛的安全相关数据，并采用 disagreement-aware annotation procedure。所得数据集覆盖 15 个 harm category 和 55 个细粒度 subcategory，包含广泛的多模态安全场景。我们还构建 SafeAtlas-Bench，一个含 5,000 个实例的 held-out set，用于评估五级预测和连续风险分数。在该数据集上，我们通过 target-conditioned tuning 训练 SafeAtlas Guard 系列模型，用于 multimodal safety detection。模型不仅进行五分类安全级别判断，还通过 soft cumulative ordinal head 把安全映射为连续分数。实验结果表明，在该数据上训练的 guard model 具有很强的泛化能力：即使不使用其他 benchmark 的训练集，它们仍能在相应测试集上取得有竞争力的表现。特别是 8B 模型取得总体最佳表现，F1 约比此前 SOTA 高 4%。代码、数据和模型已经发布，以支持后续研究。警告：本文包含可能冒犯、有害、血腥或令人不安的示例数据。

### 41. Not the Same Protector: Deployment-Dependent Protective Intervention in LLMs

**内容概述：** 针对同一 distress 输入在 voice、text 与 raw API 部署面上是否获得同等保护，论文对四个 frontier model 作匹配实验，发现所有模型的医疗指令在语音接口下降且不能由回答长度解释，raw API 条件下则没有模型主动询问用户安全。

- 收录理由：论文把 protective intervention 的 deployment-surface sensitivity 操作化为五项指标，直接揭示语音接口与 API 集成可能静默削弱伤害响应，而不是把单一聊天界面的结果外推到所有部署。
- 论文/arXiv：[Not the Same Protector: Deployment-Dependent Protective Intervention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29136)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29136`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Eunna Lee — 单位未核实
  - Soomyoung Lee — 单位未核实
  - Jungpyo Nam — 单位未核实
  - Heonjin Ha — 单位未核实
  - Jamin Jung — 单位未核实

  - Sunjun Hwang — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：We ask whether a model protects a user in the same way when that user speaks rather than types. Using a single distress vignette---a physical injury of unstated severity following an interpersonal conflict---we present four frontier models with matched inputs across voice, text, and raw API deployment conditions (n=30 per cell) and code each response along five binary protective indicators, including whether the model issues an explicit medical-care directive. Voice-interface responses are markedly shorter than text-interface responses for three of the four models, and protective behavior contracts alongside that compression: medical directives are at ceiling under both the API and text conditions but decline under voice for every model tested. The contraction is not reducible to length. One model produces voice and text responses of comparable length yet still drops medical directives, and another falls below ceiling between its API and voice conditions, whose responses are of nearly identical length. Under raw API access the pattern is categorical rather than partial: no model asks after the user's safety even once. These results show that protective intervention is sensitive to the surface through which a request arrives, that this sensitivity is detectable using a simple protective coding scheme, and that it is not explained by turn length alone.
- 中文摘要：我们询问：当用户说话而不是打字时，模型是否以相同方式保护用户。我们使用一个 distress vignette，即一次人际冲突后发生、严重程度未说明的身体伤害，在 voice、text 和 raw API 三种匹配部署条件下向四个 frontier model 提供输入（每个 cell 的 $n=30$），并按五个二元 protective indicator 编码每个响应，包括模型是否给出明确的就医指令。四个模型中的三个在 voice interface 上的响应显著短于 text interface，而且 protective behavior 随压缩一同收缩：API 和 text 条件下的医疗指令都达到上限，但所有被测模型在 voice 下均下降。这种收缩不能归结为长度。一个模型的 voice 与 text 响应长度相当，却仍减少医疗指令；另一个模型在 API 与 voice 条件间从上限下降，而两种条件的响应长度几乎相同。在 raw API access 下，模式是类别性的而不是局部的：没有任何模型哪怕一次询问用户是否安全。结果表明，protective intervention 对请求到达的交互表面敏感；这种敏感性可以用简单的 protective coding scheme 检测，且不能仅由轮次长度解释。

### 42. AI Can Be Easily Persuaded in Clinical Decision Making

**内容概述：** 针对临床 AI 是否能在他人施压时维持客观判断，论文控制改变权威、国籍、机构、声称成绩、多医生背书和重复压力，发现同样说服内容由资深医生提出时约多改变 10% 案例，甚至伪造但合理的 clinician view 也能把模型从正确决策推向错误答案。

- 收录理由：论文建立高风险 decision model 的 social-influence threat model，直接测量 authority cue 与 fabricated evidence 如何操纵临床判断，属于 belief manipulation 与安全决策稳健性。
- 论文/arXiv：[AI Can Be Easily Persuaded in Clinical Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29453)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29453`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

  - Jiayuan Zhu — 单位未核实
  - Jiazhen Pan — 单位未核实
  - Fenglin Liu — 单位未核实
  - Minhao Hu — 单位未核实
  - Junde Wu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：As AI becomes increasingly integrated into clinical practice, it is playing a growing role in medical decision making. Medicine, however, is a high stakes and evidence based field, where decisions can directly affect patients' lives. It is therefore important to understand whether AI can maintain objective judgment when others try to persuade it. In this paper, we study how easily AI can be persuaded through controlled experiments. We find that professional authority, national background, institutional affiliation, claimed past performance, multiple physicians, supported clinician views, and repeated pressure can all affect AI decisions. Surprisingly, the same persuasive input changes about 10% more cases when it comes from a senior clinician than from a medical student. Simply claiming a better performance history consistently makes the physician more persuasive. More strikingly, a plausible clinician view can persuade AI away from a correct decision even when it is fabricated to support an incorrect answer. This indicates that AI can be strongly influenced by convincing support without reliably determining whether this view from the clinician is correct. Together, these findings suggest that AI can be easily persuaded by what people say, who says it, and how the opinion is presented. Therefore, it is essential for AI to maintain sound judgment under persuasion, enabling its safe and reliable use in high stakes medical decision making.
- 中文摘要：随着 AI 越来越多地融入临床实践，它在医疗决策中的作用不断增加。然而，医学是一个高风险、以证据为基础的领域，决策会直接影响患者生命。因此，理解他人试图说服 AI 时，它能否保持客观判断十分重要。本文通过受控实验研究 AI 有多容易被说服。我们发现，专业权威、国籍背景、机构隶属、声称的过往表现、多名医生、得到支持的临床医生观点和重复施压都能影响 AI 决策。令人意外的是，相同的说服输入由资深临床医生而非医学生提出时，会多改变约 10% 的案例。仅仅声称有更好的历史表现，就能稳定提高医生的说服力。更值得注意的是，即使一个看似合理的临床医生观点是为了支持错误答案而伪造的，它也能说服 AI 放弃正确决策。这表明，AI 会受到令人信服的支持性陈述强烈影响，却不能可靠判断该临床观点是否正确。综合而言，AI 很容易受到说了什么、谁说的以及观点如何呈现的影响。因此，AI 必须在说服压力下保持可靠判断，才能安全、可靠地用于高风险医疗决策。

### 43. Evaluating the Hidden Costs of Personalization in Large Language Models

**内容概述：** 针对 personalization 可能把 helpfulness 优化推向不必要引用私人信息、信息茧房和 sycophancy，论文提出动态评测框架 PRISK 并测试 13 个 LLM，发现加入 user profile 与 retrieved memory 会持续加剧三类偏差，暴露个性化记忆的系统性副作用。

- 收录理由：论文把 personalization 和 retrieved memory 作为可控输入，分别测量 irrelevant personalization、preference narrowing 与 sycophantic bias，直接涉及隐私越界和用户信念强化风险。
- 论文/arXiv：[Evaluating the Hidden Costs of Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28833)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28833`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
