  - Yuchen Wu — 单位未核实
  - Cheng Qian — 单位未核实
  - Zhiyuan Fan — 单位未核实
  - Hyeonjeong Ha — 单位未核实
  - Shujin Wu — 单位未核实
  - Jiayu Liu — 单位未核实
  - Heng Ji — 单位未核实
  - Ge Wang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：While Large language models (LLMs) incorporate user personalization signals to improve usability and helpfulness, they increasingly shift from providing balanced, informative responses toward optimizing for user satisfaction when conditioned on personal context such as conversation history, inferred preferences, and user profiles. Specifically, we identify three emerging risks: (1) irrelevant personalization, where models reference personal information in unnecessary contexts; (2) preference narrowing, where models reinforce informational echo chambers; and (3) sycophantic bias, where models agree excessively with user opinions. As a result, models may reference personal information in contexts where it is unnecessary, inadvertently collapse response diversity, or agree excessively with user opinions. Despite the growing use of personalization in AI assistants, there has been limited systematic evaluation of its potential side effects. To bridge this gap, we propose PRISK, a dynamic evaluation framework with automated data generation and tailored metrics that uncovers systematic limitations in current LLM personalization and how personalized information shapes its responses. Our empirical analysis across 13 LLMs demonstrates the presence of user profiles and retrieved memories consistently exacerbates biases, resulting in an average drop of 45.9% in irrelevant personalization, 41.7% in preference narrowing and 61.7% in sycophantic bias.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）会纳入用户 personalization signal 以改善可用性和 helpfulness，但当模型以对话历史、推断偏好和用户 profile 等个人上下文为条件时，它们正越来越多地从提供平衡、信息充分的响应转向优化用户满意度。具体而言，我们识别出三种新兴风险：（1）irrelevant personalization，即模型在不必要的上下文中引用个人信息；（2）preference narrowing，即模型强化信息茧房；（3）sycophantic bias，即模型过度同意用户观点。因此，模型可能在不必要的语境中提及个人信息、无意中压缩响应多样性，或过度赞同用户意见。尽管 personalization 在 AI assistant 中日益普及，对其潜在副作用的系统评估仍然有限。为填补这一缺口，我们提出 PRISK，一个结合自动数据生成和定制指标的动态评估框架，用于揭示当前 LLM personalization 的系统性局限以及个性化信息如何塑造响应。对 13 个 LLM 的实证分析表明，user profile 与 retrieved memory 的存在会持续加剧偏差，在 irrelevant personalization、preference narrowing 和 sycophantic bias 上分别造成平均 45.9%、41.7% 和 61.7% 的下降。

### 44. How Identity and Opinion Shape Political Sycophancy in LLMs

**内容概述：** 针对封闭问题 benchmark 无法分离政治 sycophancy 中显式观点与人口身份两种触发源，论文用 450 个手工核查 dilemma 评测 13 个 LLM，发现二者易感性彼此不预测、共同出现时通常次可加，system persona 主要移动基线而难以抑制由用户线索造成的 stance shift。

- 收录理由：论文把 personalization 下的政治立场操纵拆成 opinion alignment 与 identity stereotyping，说明模型 stance 可交互 steering，直接关联 sycophancy、belief manipulation 与用户画像风险。
- 论文/arXiv：[How Identity and Opinion Shape Political Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29198)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29198`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`、`cs.CL`、`cs.CY`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
- 作者及单位：
  - Li-Ni Fu — 单位未核实
  - Chang-Chih Meng — 单位未核实
  - Chien-Hua Chen — 单位未核实
  - Hen-Hsen Huang — 单位未核实
  - I-Chen Wu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly encourage users to disclose personal profiles for tailored assistance, measuring their political alignment becomes increasingly important. However, many existing benchmarks for assessing political behavior rely on closed-ended questions and do not fully capture how a model's stance may adapt to user-provided context during interaction. We introduce a framework that disentangles two distinct triggers of political sycophancy: opinion (aligning with explicit narratives) and identity (stereotyping based on demographic labels). Using 450 manually-checked political dilemmas as controlled probes, we evaluate 13 instruction-tuned LLMs. We uncover a dissociation: a model's susceptibility to explicit opinions does not necessarily predict its susceptibility to identity cues, and vice versa. When both signals are present, their effects are generally sub-additive rather than simply additive. Additionally, system-level personas primarily shift a model's baseline stance while having limited effect on the stance shift caused by user opinion or identity. Ultimately, our results suggest that LLM political stance is interactively and steerably vulnerable rather than being a fixed trait, highlighting how personalization may amplify identity- or opinion-conditioned shifts in the model's behaviors.
- 中文摘要：随着大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地鼓励用户披露个人 profile 以获得定制帮助，测量其政治 alignment 变得日益重要。然而，许多评估政治行为的现有 benchmark 依赖封闭式问题，不能充分捕捉交互中模型立场如何适应用户提供的上下文。我们提出一个框架，分离政治 sycophancy 的两种不同触发源：opinion，即与显式叙事保持一致；identity，即基于人口标签形成 stereotype。我们使用 450 个经人工核查的政治 dilemma 作为受控 probe，评估 13 个 instruction-tuned LLM。我们发现一种分离现象：模型对显式观点的易感性不一定能预测其对 identity cue 的易感性，反之亦然。当两种信号同时出现时，其作用通常是次可加的，而非简单相加。此外，system-level persona 主要改变模型的基线立场，对用户 opinion 或 identity 导致的立场偏移影响有限。最终，结果表明 LLM 的政治立场不是固定 trait，而是可在交互中被 steering 的脆弱行为，并凸显 personalization 可能放大由 identity 或 opinion 条件化的模型行为偏移。

### 45. Benevolent Bias in Multi-Turn Human-Agent Dialogue

**内容概述：** 针对不平等待遇可隐藏在温暖语气下而逃过 safety detector，论文构建 362,880 条多轮支持对话 BENEVDIAL，发现现成 detector 能抓 overt bias 却大量漏掉 benevolent bias，LLM judge 在更明确标准下召回更高但会把 neutral support 误判，且人口上下文放大假警报。

- 收录理由：论文明确受影响人口属性、tone–treatment 危害路径和 detector false positive／false negative，直接评估 human-Agent 对话中的隐性公平伤害与监控有效性。
- 论文/arXiv：[Benevolent Bias in Multi-Turn Human-Agent Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29206)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29206`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Qianqi Liu — 单位未核实
  - Jin Huang — 单位未核实
  - Fethiye Irmak Dogan — 单位未核实
  - Hatice Gunes — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Bias in human-agent interaction can manifest not only through hostile language but also as benevolent bias, whereby unequal treatment hides behind a warm, positive tone. To make it detectable, we operationalise benevolent bias along two dimensions, tone and treatment, yielding three classes: neutral support, overt bias, and benevolent bias. Building on these definitions, we construct BENEVDIAL, a class-balanced corpus of 362,880 multi-turn support dialogues spanning user and agent demographics, roles, and generators, to support controlled evaluation. We then test two detector families on it: off-the-shelf safety detectors and prompted large language model (LLM) judges. Our results reveal a detection gap: off-the-shelf detectors reliably flag overt bias yet largely miss benevolent bias, while LLM judges catch more under more explicit detection criteria but increasingly misclassify neutral support as benevolent bias, and demographic context amplifies the false alarms. These findings suggest that fair monitoring of human-agent dialogue must look beyond surface cues to whether the agent's treatment is disparate.
- 中文摘要：Human-Agent interaction 中的 bias 不仅表现为敌意语言，也可表现为 benevolent bias，即不平等待遇隐藏在温暖、积极的语气之下。为使其可被检测，我们沿 tone 和 treatment 两个维度操作化 benevolent bias，得到三类：neutral support、overt bias 和 benevolent bias。在这些定义基础上，我们构建 BENEVDIAL，一个类别均衡、包含 362,880 条多轮支持对话的 corpus，覆盖用户和 Agent 的人口属性、角色与 generator，以支持受控评估。随后，我们在其上测试两类 detector：现成 safety detector 和经 prompt 的大型语言模型（LLM）judge。结果揭示出 detection gap：现成 detector 能可靠标记 overt bias，却大量漏掉 benevolent bias；LLM judge 在更明确的检测标准下能捕获更多，但会越来越多地把 neutral support 误判为 benevolent bias，而且人口上下文会放大 false alarm。这些发现表明，对 human-Agent dialogue 的公平监控不能只看表面线索，还必须检查 Agent 的待遇是否存在差异。

### 46. Whose Assessment of Distress? Community Perspectives and LLM Alignment on Well-Being Posts

**内容概述：** 针对 distress detector 通常对不同社区套用单一标准，论文让 321 名参与者对六个身份社区的 1,198 条帖子作 9,587 次判断，再评估九种开放模型与四种 frontier 配置，发现模型对 none-to-mild distress 系统性高估、准确率仅 31%–44%，且不能用“外群体视角”解释。

- 收录理由：论文以社区特定标签和人工对照审计 mental-health risk detector 的 severity calibration，明确量化 false positive 对不同社区可能造成的不均衡部署伤害。
- 论文/arXiv：[Whose Assessment of Distress? Community Perspectives and LLM Alignment on Well-Being Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29446)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29446`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Andrew Aquilina — 单位未核实
  - Xiang Lorraine Li — 单位未核实
  - Yu-Ru Li — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Judgments about psychological distress are socially situated: what counts as concerning hinges on community norms around emotional expression, vulnerability, and help-seeking. Yet large language models (LLMs) used for distress detection are typically aligned to a single, undifferentiated standard. How well do these models capture the perspectives of the communities whose language they assess? We address this question through a perspectivist annotation study in which 321 participants provided 9,587 judgments on 1,198 Reddit posts spanning six identity-based communities, yielding community-specific labels. Raters in the contextualized in-group condition show a modest tendency to agree more with their community than uncontextualized out-group raters (OR = 1.18), an effect varying significantly across communities. We then evaluate nine open-weight LLM configurations and four frontier configurations against these labels. Open-weight LLMs systematically over-estimate distress: when communities perceive none-to-mild distress, these models achieve only 31-44% accuracy, predominantly producing false positives. GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Pro show the same none-to-mild inflation even when their full-sample over/under rates are mixed, while Claude Opus 4 is more conservative. This pattern does not simply mirror an outsider reading position: uncontextualized out-group human aggregates were nearly symmetric, with 18% over-estimation versus 19% under-estimation. Instead, the models that inflate none-to-mild cases exhibit a distress prior that exceeds both contextualized in-group and uncontextualized out-group human judgments. These findings have implications for equitable AI deployment in mental health contexts, where miscalibrated distress detection may unevenly affect the communities being assessed.
- 中文摘要：对心理 distress 的判断具有社会情境性：什么值得担忧，取决于社区关于情绪表达、脆弱性和求助行为的规范。然而，用于 distress detection 的大型语言模型（LLM）通常只对齐到一个没有区分的统一标准。这些模型在多大程度上捕获其所评估语言所属社区的视角？我们通过 perspectivist annotation study 回答这一问题：321 名参与者对来自六个 identity-based community 的 1,198 条 Reddit 帖子给出 9,587 次判断，由此得到社区特定标签。Contextualized in-group 条件下的 rater 比 uncontextualized out-group rater 略更倾向于同本社区达成一致（OR=1.18），且这一作用在社区间显著变化。随后，我们根据这些标签评估九种 open-weight LLM 配置和四种 frontier 配置。Open-weight LLM 系统性高估 distress：当社区认为 distress 为 none-to-mild 时，这些模型的准确率只有 31%–44%，且主要产生 false positive。即使 GPT-5 和 Gemini 2.5 Pro 在全样本上的 over／under rate 混合，它们仍呈现同样的 none-to-mild inflation；Claude Opus 4 则更保守。这一模式不只是外部人阅读视角的映射：无上下文的 out-group 人类汇总结果几乎对称，过度估计为 18%，低估为 19%。相反，那些夸大 none-to-mild 案例的模型表现出一种 distress prior，同时高于 contextualized in-group 和 uncontextualized out-group 的人类判断。这些发现影响 mental-health 场景中的公平 AI 部署，因为校准错误的 distress detection 可能不均衡地影响被评估社区。

### 47. StageWell: A Process-Aligned Chinese Corpus for Positive-Psychology Support Dialogue

**内容概述：** 针对心理支持训练只给 turn-level strategy 或整体 preference、缺少过程位置与局部修复监督，论文构建六阶段中文语料 StageWell 和 HQS 协议，以多 Agent 全对话改写产生 12,445 条 SFT、1,849 对 DPO 及专家修订集，四个模型平均 H-critical rate 下降 0.167。

- 收录理由：论文把 supportive dialogue 的安全从单轮语气扩展到多轮过程控制，并用 expert revision 与 critical-harm metric 测量局部修复，符合心理支持对话的过程级安全干预。
- 论文/arXiv：[StageWell: A Process-Aligned Chinese Corpus for Positive-Psychology Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29326)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29326`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：29 pages, 20 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Yuxiong Wang — 单位未核实
  - Ziwei Lin — 单位未核实
  - Bo Wang — 单位未核实
  - Yu Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Shiguang Ni — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Positive psychology dialogue aims to support emotional distress and positive resource building, requiring models to produce not only empathetic replies but also coherent progression through a multi-turn support process. Existing resources often reduce supervision to turn-level strategies or holistic preference labels, leaving process position, support function, and local repair targets implicit. We introduce StageWell, a process-aligned Chinese corpus for positive psychology dialogue, together with HQS, a structured protocol for data construction and evaluation. StageWell organizes support into a six-stage support process and uses a multi-agent whole-dialogue rewriting workflow to construct 12,445 SFT instances, 1,849 DPO preference pairs, and a GroundTruth subset of 120 expert-revised dialogues and 977 QA pairs. Guided by HQS, DPO pairs are built as process-localized repairs: flawed model outputs are used as rejected responses, and targeted rewrites under the same context and stage constraint are used as chosen responses. Across four 9B-14B open-source LLMs, this supervision yields robust gains in process control, response quality, and safety. Averaged across models, BERTScore improves by 0.037, Q-Overall increases by 1.32 points, S-exact increases by 0.236, and the H-critical rate decreases by 0.167. These results highlight the value of modeling supportive dialogue as a structured multi-turn support process rather than as single-turn response generation.
- 中文摘要：Positive psychology dialogue 旨在支持情绪 distress 和积极资源建设，要求模型不仅生成有同理心的回复，还要在多轮支持流程中保持连贯推进。现有资源常把监督简化为 turn-level strategy 或整体 preference label，使过程位置、支持功能和局部修复目标保持隐含。我们提出 StageWell，一个 process-aligned 中文 positive psychology dialogue corpus，并提出 HQS，一套用于数据构建和评估的结构化协议。StageWell 把支持组织为六阶段流程，并用 multi-agent whole-dialogue rewriting workflow 构建 12,445 个 SFT 实例、1,849 个 DPO preference pair，以及包含 120 段专家修订对话和 977 个 QA pair 的 GroundTruth 子集。在 HQS 指导下，DPO pair 被构建为 process-localized repair：有缺陷的模型输出作为 rejected response，在相同上下文和阶段约束下进行的定向改写作为 chosen response。在四个 9B–14B open-source LLM 上，这种监督稳定改善 process control、response quality 和 safety。跨模型平均而言，BERTScore 提高 0.037，Q-Overall 提高 1.32 分，S-exact 提高 0.236，H-critical rate 降低 0.167。结果凸显出，应把 supportive dialogue 建模为结构化多轮支持过程，而不是单轮 response generation。

### 48. Balancing Privacy, Utility, and Safety in LLM Alignment through Preference Optimization

**内容概述：** 针对 preference data composition 是否影响隐私记忆缺少研究，论文提出 P3M，在固定 helpfulness／harmlessness 数据时混入不同比例的合成 privacy preference；两种 Gemma 设置中 canary signal 和总体 MIA 表现下降，但效果不跨数据源一致，且不构成正式隐私保证或 extraction defense。

- 收录理由：论文把 alignment preference optimization 与 memorization／membership leakage 联合评估，并清楚限制其防御含义，适合分析 privacy–utility–safety 三方权衡。
- 论文/arXiv：[Balancing Privacy, Utility, and Safety in LLM Alignment through Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30141)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30141`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：6 pages, accepted for presentation at PRAI 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Dishu Yang — 单位未核实
  - Jingjing Liu — 单位未核实
  - Jize Li — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models with human preferences, but preference-data composition may also influence privacy-relevant memorization. We examine whether adding synthetic privacy-preference pairs to Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is associated with lower canary-based memorization signals without modifying the objective or introducing a formal privacy mechanism. We propose Privacy-Pressure Preference Mixing (P3M), a data-composition protocol that varies the amount of privacy-preference data while keeping helpfulness and harmlessness preference data fixed. We evaluate a non-privacy Baseline and privacy-mixing ratios of 0.5, 1.0, and 2.0 using Gemma 3 270M-IT across five random seeds and validate the same four conditions using 4-bit-quantized Gemma 2 2B-IT across three seeds. Overall, under the tested conditions, privacy-preference mixing is associated with lower mean canary suffix log-likelihood proxy values across both model settings and lower aggregate membership-inference attack performance relative to the Baseline in the mixed-source 2B evaluation. Specifically, across the privacy-aware 2B configurations, the mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) ranges from 0.596 to 0.629, and the mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) ranges from 0.541 to 0.575, compared with 0.804 and 0.790, respectively, for the Baseline. However, the reduction in membership distinguishability does not hold uniformly across data sources. Moreover, the relationship between the privacy ratio and harmlessness preference accuracy varies by model setting, whereas helpfulness preference accuracy remains broadly stable. These findings suggest that P3M should be viewed as a lightweight empirical protocol for examining privacy-utility-safety trade-offs rather than as a formal privacy guarantee or a defense against extraction attacks.
- 中文摘要：Preference optimization 被广泛用于使大型语言模型对齐人类偏好，但 preference-data composition 也可能影响与隐私相关的记忆。我们考察这样一个问题：在不修改目标函数、也不引入正式隐私机制的情况下，向 Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）加入合成 privacy-preference pair，是否与更低的 canary-based memorization signal 相关。我们提出 Privacy-Pressure Preference Mixing（P3M），一种在 helpfulness 和 harmlessness preference data 保持不变时，改变 privacy-preference data 数量的数据组合协议。我们使用 Gemma 3 270M-IT，在五个随机 seed 上评估 non-privacy Baseline 以及 0.5、1.0 和 2.0 三种 privacy mixing ratio，并使用 4-bit-quantized Gemma 2 2B-IT，在三个 seed 上验证相同四种条件。总体而言，在测试条件下，两种模型设置中的 privacy-preference mixing 都与较低的平均 canary suffix log-likelihood proxy value 相关；在 mixed-source 2B 评估中，它相对 Baseline 也与较低的总体 membership-inference attack 性能相关。具体而言，在 privacy-aware 2B 配置中，平均 area under the receiver operating characteristic curve（AUROC）范围为 0.596–0.629，平均 area under the precision-recall curve（AUPRC）范围为 0.541–0.575；Baseline 则分别为 0.804 和 0.790。然而，membership distinguishability 的降低并没有在不同数据源上统一成立。此外，privacy ratio 与 harmlessness preference accuracy 的关系随模型设置而变化，而 helpfulness preference accuracy 基本稳定。这些发现说明，应把 P3M 看作研究 privacy-utility-safety trade-off 的轻量经验协议，而不是正式隐私保证或针对 extraction attack 的防御。

### 49. Interpreting and Steering for Safe and Correct Code Generation

**内容概述：** 针对 LLM 生成含漏洞代码但安全表征机制研究不足，论文构建 9,342 对 CodeSec-Pairs 并定位 safety-related layer／attention head，提出同时 steering 安全与正确性的 DuoSteer，在五类漏洞上平均降低 26.9% vulnerability rate、提高 7.5% functional correctness，且跨模型复现。

- 收录理由：论文将安全可解释性转化为 inference-time code defense，同时联合评估漏洞率与功能正确性，直接涉及 AI-generated code 的供应链风险和可部署 steering。
- 论文/arXiv：[Interpreting and Steering for Safe and Correct Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30025)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30025`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：Accepted to the EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- 作者及单位：
  - Hao Yan — 单位未核实
  - Ziyu Yao — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate source code containing vulnerabilities, yet little work studies the internal mechanisms that distinguish safe from vulnerable generation in them. In this work, we systematically perform a mechanistic interpretation of LLMs, aiming at both understanding how code safety-vs-vulnerability is represented or driven by components in an LM and turning the insights into actionable steering strategies to encourage safer code generation. To this end, we introduce CodeSec-Pairs, a dataset of 9,342 Python safe-and-vulnerable contrastive code pairs, sampled from Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Utilizing the dataset, we explore approaches to localize layers and attention heads that relate to code safety, and further experiment with different steering strategies for inference-time vulnerability reduction. In particular, we propose DuoSteer, a double-steering approach that simultaneously applies safety and code-correctness steering to attention heads. In experiments over five vulnerability types, DuoSteer leads to an average of -26.9% vulnerability rate reduction and +7.5% functional correctness improvement, which outperforms not only other steering variants but also prompting and supervised fine-tuning baselines. The advantage also replicates on Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct with another 2,500 contrastive pairs sampled from that model.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）经常生成包含漏洞的源代码，但很少有工作研究其内部如何区分安全与有漏洞的生成。本文系统地对 LLM 进行机制解释，目标既包括理解语言模型中的组件如何表示或驱动 code safety-vs-vulnerability，也包括把所得洞见转化为可执行的 steering strategy，以鼓励生成更安全的代码。为此，我们提出 CodeSec-Pairs，一个包含 9,342 对 Python 安全—有漏洞对比代码的数据集，样本来自 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct。利用该数据集，我们探索定位与代码安全相关的 layer 和 attention head，并进一步实验不同的 inference-time vulnerability reduction steering strategy。特别地，我们提出 DuoSteer，一种同时对 attention head 应用安全 steering 与代码正确性 steering 的 double-steering 方法。在五类漏洞上的实验中，DuoSteer 平均使 vulnerability rate 降低 26.9%，functional correctness 提高 7.5%，不仅优于其他 steering 变体，也优于 prompting 和 supervised fine-tuning baseline。这一优势还在 Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct 上复现，所用的另外 2,500 个 contrastive pair 由该模型采样。

### 50. Compression-Aware Abstention: Teaching LLMs to Refuse When KV-Compression Masks Remove Answer Evidence

**内容概述：** 针对 KV-cache compression 删除 answer-bearing evidence 后模型仍会幻觉作答，论文把 compression-aware abstention 建模为学习问题，以 survival mask 和证据 span 监督 10.1M 参数 LoRA；在 prompt-style truncation 下 hallucination 降低 97%，真实 compressed-cache decoding 中 evidence-retaining 样本相对提升 6–22 倍。

- 收录理由：论文把系统级缓存优化造成的证据丢失连接到条件式拒答，并用受控删除证明行为由证据内容而非长度驱动，属于 selective prediction 与服务部署安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[Compression-Aware Abstention: Teaching LLMs to Refuse When KV-Compression Masks Remove Answer Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29934)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29934`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/mali-kh/compression-aware-abstention)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：19 pages, 5 figures. Accepted to the GroundLM workshop at EMNLP 2026. Code, adapters, and datasets: https://github.com/mali-kh/compression-aware-abstention
- 作者及单位：
  - Mohammadali Khodabandehlou — 单位未核实
  - Bhaskar Krishnamachari — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：KV-cache compression reduces LLM inference memory by evicting context tokens, but when the evicted tokens contain answer-bearing evidence, the model may hallucinate instead of recognizing that the compressed context is insufficient. We address this failure from a behavioral perspective: to our knowledge, this is the first work to formulate compression-aware abstention as a learning problem, in which a model learns to answer when supporting evidence survives compression and abstain when it does not. We construct supervision from compressor survival masks and tight answer-bearing spans, labeling examples as Confident when evidence survives and Abstain when it is removed. A 10.1M-parameter LoRA adapter trained on ~2.6K MuSiQue 2-hop QA examples reduces base-model hallucinations by 97% under prompt-style truncation while preserving correct answering on evidence-retaining examples. Unlike prompt-only abstention baselines, which over-abstain on many answerable high-retention examples, the trained adapter learns a conditional policy. We also evaluate the method under actual compressed-cache decoding, where multi-compressor training yields a 6-22x relative lift over the unaided base on evidence-retaining examples. Controlled-deletion experiments show that the learned behavior is driven by evidence content rather than input length alone.
- 中文摘要：KV-cache compression 通过驱逐 context token 来减少 LLM inference memory，但当被驱逐 token 包含回答所需证据时，模型可能产生 hallucination，而不是识别出压缩后的上下文不足。我们从行为角度处理这一失效：据我们所知，这是首次把 compression-aware abstention 表述为学习问题，使模型在支持证据经压缩后仍存活时作答，在证据不存在时 abstain。我们根据 compressor survival mask 和紧凑的 answer-bearing span 构建监督：证据存活的样本标为 Confident，证据被移除的样本标为 Abstain。一个在约 2,600 个 MuSiQue 2-hop QA 样本上训练的 10.1M 参数 LoRA adapter，在 prompt-style truncation 下把 base model hallucination 降低 97%，同时保留对证据仍在样本的正确作答。Prompt-only abstention baseline 会对许多可回答、高保留率样本过度 abstain，而训练后的 adapter 学到 conditional policy。我们也在真实 compressed-cache decoding 下评估该方法，其中 multi-compressor training 使 evidence-retaining 样本相对无辅助 base model 提高 6–22 倍。受控删除实验表明，所学行为由证据内容而非仅输入长度驱动。

### 51. BCPPO: Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization for Tail-Risk-Aware Safe Reinforcement Learning

**内容概述：** 针对 expected-cost constraint 仍会容许罕见高代价事件、CVaR gradient 又可能高噪声，论文提出 BCPPO，以多 critic disagreement 经 Bachelier 公式形成平滑 penalty；175 次运行中没有比较方法同时获得更高平均 return 和更低平均 CVaR，但该 penalty 不是尾部概率或误差界，也不提供安全保证。

- 收录理由：论文明确以 tail risk 约束 safe RL，并对 return–CVaR 权衡作同预算比较；其主动声明“无安全保证”有助于避免把经验 caution signal 误当 certification。
- 论文/arXiv：[BCPPO: Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization for Tail-Risk-Aware Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30283)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30283`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Dongsheng Hou — 单位未核实
  - Yanqiao Chen — 单位未核实
  - Yuhan Rui — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Expected-cost constraints can still permit rare, high-cost events. Monte Carlo conditional value at risk (CVaR) gradients can be noisy at high confidence, whereas critics that model an outcome distribution add complexity. We propose BCPPO (Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization), a proximal policy optimization (PPO) method. Separately initialized cost-prediction networks (critics), trained with random sample masks, produce disagreement that marks predictions sensitive to which state-action regions occur in the training data and to critic training. A Bachelier formula for the expected amount above a reference level converts this disagreement into a smooth policy-update penalty. Gradients from this penalty do not alter the critics, so temporal-difference (TD) critic learning is unchanged. A saturation-aware controller adjusts the mean-cost penalty and stops accumulated error from growing while that penalty is clipped. Deployment retains only the policy network. The disagreement penalty is neither a tail-event probability nor a guaranteed error bound, and it provides no safety guarantee. Across 175 runs with shared tasks, costs, budgets, training steps, and evaluation seeds, no comparator attains both higher mean return and lower mean CVaR than BCPPO in any task. On Push1, BCPPO has no lower return and no higher CVaR than every comparator, with at least one strict gain. These results support a practical balance among reward, caution around cost predictions that vary across trained critics, and policy-only deployment.
- 中文摘要：Expected-cost constraint 仍可能允许罕见的高代价事件。Monte Carlo conditional value at risk（CVaR）gradient 在高 confidence 下可能噪声很大，而对 outcome distribution 建模的 critic 会增加复杂性。我们提出 BCPPO（Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization），一种 proximal policy optimization（PPO）方法。分别初始化、使用随机 sample mask 训练的 cost-prediction network（critic）会产生 disagreement；它标记出对训练数据中出现哪些 state-action region 以及对 critic training 敏感的预测。用于计算超过 reference level 的期望量的 Bachelier 公式，将该 disagreement 转换为平滑的 policy-update penalty。该 penalty 的 gradient 不改变 critic，因此 temporal-difference（TD）critic learning 保持不变。Saturation-aware controller 会调整 mean-cost penalty，并在该 penalty 被 clip 时阻止累积误差继续增长。部署时只保留 policy network。Disagreement penalty 既不是 tail-event probability，也不是有保证的 error bound，并且不提供安全保证。在 175 次运行中，任务、成本、预算、训练步骤和评估 seed 均保持一致；没有任何比较方法在任一任务上同时取得比 BCPPO 更高的 mean return 和更低的 mean CVaR。在 Push1 上，相比每个比较方法，BCPPO 的 return 都不更低、CVaR 都不更高，且至少有一项严格改善。这些结果支持在 reward、对不同已训练 critic 间变化的 cost prediction 保持谨慎，以及只部署 policy 三者之间取得实践平衡。

### 52. Uncertainty of Vision Medical Foundation Models

**内容概述：** 针对医疗 foundation model 的 point prediction 无 coverage 保证且单靠 recalibration 无法消除跨数据源差异，论文比较视网膜、病理与胸片领域／通用预训练模型的点级和 conformal region uncertainty，发现高质量领域自监督预训练校准更好且能产生更高效的有限样本有效 prediction set。

- 收录理由：论文在高风险医疗场景中明确引入 finite-sample coverage guarantee，并同时审计 calibration 与 conformal efficiency，满足 uncertainty 研究需提供 risk bound 的收录条件。
- 论文/arXiv：[Uncertainty of Vision Medical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30390)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30390`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Haoxu Huang — 单位未核实
  - Narges Razavian — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Accurate uncertainty estimation is essential for machine learning systems de- ployed in high-stakes domains such as medicine. Traditional approaches primarily rely on probability outputs from trained models (point predictions), which provide no formal guarantees on prediction coverage and often require additional calibra- tion techniques to improve reliability. In contrast, conformal prediction (region prediction) offers a principled alternative by generating prediction sets with finite- sample validity guarantees, ensuring that the ground truth is contained within the set at a specified confidence level. In this study, we explore the impact of pre-training approach, dataset scale and domain on both point and region-level uncertainty quantification, by studying domain-specific vision medical foundation models vs. general domain vision foundation models. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation across foundation models trained on retinal, histopathological, and Chest X-Rays data, applying various calibration techniques. Our results demonstrate that (1) pre-training on higher-quality domain-specific datasets along with self-supervised learning leads to better-calibrated point predictions than general domain pre-training, (2) stan- dard re-calibration methods alone cannot fully mitigate uncertainty discrepancies across models trained on different data sources, (3) domain-specific foundation model can lead to more efficient conformal prediction. These findings highlight the importance of careful model selection and the inte- gration of both point and region prediction to enhance the reliability and trust- worthiness of medical AI systems. Our work underscores the need for a holistic approach to uncertainty quantification in recent development of medical vision foundation model, ensuring robust and interpretable AI-driven decision-making.
- 中文摘要：准确的 uncertainty estimation 对部署在医学等高风险领域的机器学习系统至关重要。传统方法主要依赖已训练模型的概率输出（point prediction），它对 prediction coverage 不提供正式保证，并且通常需要额外 calibration technique 来提高可靠性。相比之下，conformal prediction（region prediction）通过生成具有 finite-sample validity guarantee 的 prediction set，提供一种有原则的替代方案，保证 ground truth 以指定 confidence level 包含在集合内。本文通过比较领域特定的视觉医疗 foundation model 与通用领域视觉 foundation model，研究 pre-training approach、dataset scale 和 domain 对 point-level 与 region-level uncertainty quantification 的影响。我们在使用视网膜、组织病理和胸部 X 光数据训练的 foundation model 上进行综合评估，并应用多种 calibration technique。结果表明：（1）在更高质量的领域特定数据集上结合 self-supervised learning 进行预训练，相比通用领域预训练能产生校准更好的 point prediction；（2）仅使用标准 recalibration method，无法完全缓解不同数据源训练模型之间的 uncertainty discrepancy；（3）领域特定 foundation model 能带来更高效的 conformal prediction。这些发现强调，谨慎选择模型并整合 point prediction 与 region prediction，对提高医疗 AI 系统的可靠性与可信性很重要。我们的工作强调，在近期 medical vision foundation model 的发展中，需要采用整体性的 uncertainty quantification 方法，以支持稳健、可解释的 AI-driven decision-making。

### 53. Evaluating the Safety of Deep Learning-Based Brain MRI Reconstruction

**内容概述：** 针对脑 MRI 重建模型会抹除病灶或合成假组织而 PSNR／SSIM 难以发现，论文按 PRISMA 审查 263 项研究，只有 18 项在同一数据上同时报告 fidelity metric 与 reader assessment，生成模型仅 11.3% 接受 reader evaluation，据此认为现有实践无法认证诊断安全并提出五项要求。

- 收录理由：论文围绕直接影响诊断的 hallucination／lesion erasure 审计安全 surrogate 与人工评估缺口，并报告系统综述质量与 extraction 未审计等限制，属于 safety-critical model evaluation。
- 论文/arXiv：[Evaluating the Safety of Deep Learning-Based Brain MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28714)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28714`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`eess.IV`、`cs.CV`
- 提交日期：2026-08-28
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Dat Tat Mai — 单位未核实
  - Thai Viet Pham — 单位未核实
  - Thu Nguyen Thi Dang — 单位未核实
  - James Jin Kang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Objective: Deep learning accelerates brain MRI four- to tenfold, but models can erase lesions or synthesize false tissue - failures pixel-averaged metrics like PSNR and SSIM miss. We review whether current evaluation practices detect this blind spot. Methods: Following PRISMA 2020, we searched seven databases without date limits, including 263 studies (1995-2026), appraised them using QUADAS-2 and matched instruments, and synthesized narratively. Categories were derived from titles, abstracts, and controlled vocabulary; reported prevalence figures represent floors. Duplicate screening achieved high agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.877), as did appraisal (0.788; 0.390 where observable). Extraction is unaudited. Results: Only 18 of 263 studies (6.8%) recorded both a fidelity metric and reader assessment on identical data, leaving the central surrogate unmeasured. Reader studies mostly measured inter-reader agreement, which was weak: fastMRI 2020 concordance reached 0.457 and 0.386 (Kendall W), improving only where SSIM diverged. Erasing a 100 mm3 lacunar infarct shifts global PSNR by 0.03 dB under the stated error model. As the corpus grew fivefold, reader assessments dropped from 32% to 18%, recovering to 21%. Generative models - most associated with hallucination (39%) - were among the least reader-evaluated (11.3%), while self-supervised models reached 47% with zero reader evaluation. Only 5% released code and ran reader studies; none evaluated a model observer; no named dataset covered acute stroke or hemorrhage. Conclusions: On these floors, current evaluation practices cannot certify diagnostic safety. We derive five requirements safety-oriented evaluations must meet.
- 中文摘要：目标：Deep learning 可把脑 MRI 加速四至十倍，但模型可能抹除病灶或合成虚假组织，而 PSNR、SSIM 等像素平均指标会漏掉这些失效。我们审查当前评估实践能否发现这一 blind spot。方法：遵循 PRISMA 2020，我们不设日期限制地搜索七个数据库，纳入 263 项研究（1995–2026），使用 QUADAS-2 和匹配工具进行质量评价，并作叙述性综合。类别由标题、摘要和受控词汇派生；报告的 prevalence 数字代表下界。重复筛选达到很高一致性（Fleiss kappa=0.877），质量评价亦然（0.788；在可观察处为 0.390）。数据提取未经审计。结果：263 项研究中只有 18 项（6.8%）在相同数据上同时记录 fidelity metric 和 reader assessment，核心 surrogate 因而未得到测量。Reader study 主要测量 reader 间一致性，而该一致性较弱：fastMRI 2020 的一致性达到 0.457 和 0.386（Kendall W），只在 SSIM 出现差异时才改善。在所述 error model 下，抹除一个 $100\ \mathrm{mm}^3$ 的腔隙性梗死只会使全局 PSNR 改变 0.03 dB。随着文献规模增长五倍，reader assessment 的比例从 32% 降至 18%，随后恢复到 21%。与 hallucination 关联最强的生成模型（39%）属于最少接受 reader evaluation 的模型之一（11.3%），而 self-supervised model 占 47%，却没有 reader evaluation。只有 5% 的研究同时发布代码并开展 reader study；没有研究评估 model observer；没有具名数据集覆盖急性卒中或出血。结论：按这些下界，当前评估实践无法认证诊断安全。我们推导出 safety-oriented evaluation 必须满足的五项要求。

### 54. Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges

**内容概述：** 针对 biomedical LLM judge 在人工 gold 稀缺时只看总体正确率会掩盖部署失效，论文以确定性 metric-grounded mutation 生成可审计 preference pair，并从正确性、重复采样稳健性和格式遵从三维评估；SFT→RL 在可分解 PICO／MedCalc 任务上最优，但增益集中于此类任务。

- 收录理由：论文直接提高高风险 biomedical judge 的 evaluation validity，把 stochastic stability 与 output compliance 纳入审计，避免用单一平均分替代可部署性。
- 论文/arXiv：[Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29127)

  - Rodrigo de Oliveira — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：We propose a scalable, validity-oriented pipeline for evaluating biomedical LLM judges when high-quality human judgments are scarce. First, we augment existing human-labelled biomedical benchmarks with deterministic, metric-grounded mutations that produce auditable preference pairs. Second, we evaluate judges beyond aggregate correctness using three deployment-relevant dimensions: correctness against metric-derived gold labels, robustness under repeated stochastic sampling, and compliance with the requested output format. We use this pipeline to assess Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four regimes: (1) base, using the instruct model as is; (2) SFT, distillation-based supervised fine-tuning only; (3) RL, GRPO-based reinforcement learning only; and (4) SFT$\rightarrow$RL, SFT followed by RL. The base and single-stage regimes struggle on structured medical discrimination such as PICO extraction and clinical calculations, whereas SFT$\rightarrow$RL performs best across correctness, compliance, and robustness; gains concentrate on decomposable tasks (PICO, MedCalc), at times matching or outperforming frontier models.
- 中文摘要：在高质量人工判断稀缺时，我们提出一个可扩展、面向 validity 的 pipeline，用于评估 biomedical LLM judge。首先，我们使用 deterministic、metric-grounded mutation 扩充现有人工标注 biomedical benchmark，生成可审计 preference pair。其次，我们不只用总体正确率评估 judge，而是使用三个部署相关维度：相对于 metric-derived gold label 的正确性、重复随机采样下的稳健性，以及对所请求输出格式的遵从。我们用该 pipeline 在四种 regime 下评估 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct：（1）base，直接使用 instruct model；（2）SFT，只使用基于 distillation 的 supervised fine-tuning；（3）RL，只使用基于 GRPO 的 reinforcement learning；（4）SFT$\rightarrow$RL，即先 SFT 后 RL。Base 和单阶段 regime 在 PICO extraction、clinical calculation 等结构化医学区分任务上表现困难，而 SFT$\rightarrow$RL 在 correctness、compliance 和 robustness 上表现最佳；增益集中在可分解任务（PICO、MedCalc），有时能追平或超过 frontier model。

### 55. STEP: A Modular Silent Trial Engine for Operational Evaluation of Digital Pathology AI in Routine Workflow

**内容概述：** 针对病理 AI 从回顾验证走向临床前部署缺少可复用 live-data silent trial 基础设施，论文提出 STEP，以模块化 adapter 支持 case discovery、幂等推理、失败恢复、持久状态和 audit log，并在三家机构对 EAGLE 开展不影响患者管理的前瞻评估。

- 收录理由：论文提供安全关键模型部署前的技术监督层，重点是可审计试运行、故障恢复和跨机构状态持久性，而非一般医疗预测性能。

- 英文摘要：Prospective silent trials provide an important bridge between retrospective validation of artificial intelligence (AI) models and their use in clinical care by evaluating model performance and operational reliability on live clinical data without influencing patient management. In computational pathology, conducting silent trials requires integration across laboratory information systems, digital pathology infrastructure, computational resources, and model inference pipelines, and these workflows are often implemented using application-specific software. We developed the Silent Trial Engine for Pathology (STEP), a reusable software platform for orchestrating prospective silent trials of computational pathology AI models across heterogeneous clinical and computational environments. STEP separates common trial orchestration from institution-specific data access and compute infrastructure through modular adapter interfaces. The platform supports scheduled case discovery, per-slide inference submission, deterministic idempotency, failure recovery, result and ancillary-data ingestion, persistent trial and run state, and audit logging, with compute adapters supporting local execution and high-performance computing environments using LSF and Slurm. STEP was deployed at three institutions to support prospective silent evaluation of EAGLE, an AI model for predicting EGFR mutation status from hematoxylin and eosin-stained whole-slide images. By separating trial-level workflow logic from site-specific integrations, STEP enables a common execution framework to operate across heterogeneous pathology environments while maintaining durable and auditable trial state. This approach may reduce duplicated engineering effort and facilitate systematic real-world evaluation of computational pathology AI before interventional clinical deployment.
- 中文摘要：Prospective silent trial 通过在不影响患者管理的情况下，用 live clinical data 评估模型性能和 operational reliability，在 AI 模型的回顾性验证与临床使用之间架起重要桥梁。在 computational pathology 中，开展 silent trial 需要整合实验室信息系统、数字病理基础设施、计算资源和模型 inference pipeline，而这些 workflow 往往使用应用特定软件实现。我们开发了 Silent Trial Engine for Pathology（STEP），一个可复用软件平台，用于跨异构临床和计算环境编排 computational pathology AI model 的 prospective silent trial。STEP 通过模块化 adapter interface，把通用 trial orchestration 与机构特定的数据访问和计算基础设施分离。平台支持定时 case discovery、逐 slide inference submission、deterministic idempotency、failure recovery、结果及 ancillary-data ingestion、持久 trial／run state 和 audit logging；compute adapter 支持本地执行，以及使用 LSF 和 Slurm 的高性能计算环境。STEP 已部署在三家机构，用于支持对 EAGLE 的 prospective silent evaluation；EAGLE 是一个根据苏木精—伊红染色全切片图像预测 EGFR mutation status 的 AI 模型。通过把 trial-level workflow logic 与 site-specific integration 分离，STEP 使同一执行框架能在异构病理环境中运行，同时维持持久、可审计的 trial state。该方法可能减少重复工程，并促进 computational pathology AI 在介入临床部署前接受系统性真实世界评估。

### 56. SGRNet: Spatially Guided Radiology Network for Structured Radiological Reporting of Head and Neck Cancer

**内容概述：** 针对自由文本放射报告在密集解剖区域会 hallucinate，论文把头颈癌报告改写为 anatomically grounded multi-label structured prediction，并以器官分割和弱监督 3D Gaussian 肿瘤热图引导 SGRNet；184 对多中心 CT／报告上达到 0.60 mAP，较纯 3D volume 基线提高 8.8 个百分点。

- 收录理由：论文以结构化、局部解剖 grounding 约束临床生成，直接针对 radiology hallucination 的 factual safety，而不是只在通用文本指标上优化报告流畅度。
- 论文/arXiv：[SGRNet: Spatially Guided Radiology Network for Structured Radiological Reporting of Head and Neck Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29153)

- 英文摘要：Automated radiological report generation can alleviate clinical workloads and eliminate observer variability. However, standard free-text generation models pose hallucination risks in dense regions and fail under data scarcity. We address these challenges in Head and Neck Cancer (HNC) from contrast-enhanced CT (CECT) imaging. To enforce factual safety, we reformulate report generation as an anatomically grounded, multi-label, structured reporting task, predicting localized tumor involvement across a hierarchical clinical schema. To bridge the visual gap from missing metabolic imaging (e.g., PET), we introduce SGRNet (Spatially Guided Radiology Network), incorporating two low-cost spatial priors: automated organ segmentations and weakly supervised tumor localization maps modeled via 3D Gaussian heatmaps. These priors are dynamically integrated via spatial feature modulation to guide the network toward subtle tumor-induced structural alterations. Evaluated on a multi-centric dataset of 184 paired HNC CECT volumes and reports, on five clinically salient, densely packed anatomical subsites, SGRNet achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 0.60, an 8.8 percentage-point absolute improvement over strong volume-only 3D baselines.
- 中文摘要：自动生成放射学报告可以减轻临床工作量并消除观察者差异。然而，标准自由文本生成模型在密集区域会产生 hallucination risk，并在数据稀缺时失效。我们在 contrast-enhanced CT（CECT）头颈癌（HNC）影像上处理这些问题。为强制 factual safety，我们把报告生成重新表述为 anatomically grounded、multi-label structured reporting task，在层级临床 schema 上预测局部肿瘤累及。为弥补缺少代谢影像（例如 PET）造成的视觉差距，我们提出 SGRNet（Spatially Guided Radiology Network），纳入两种低成本 spatial prior：自动器官分割，以及以 3D Gaussian heatmap 建模的弱监督肿瘤定位图。这些 prior 通过 spatial feature modulation 动态融合，引导网络关注细微的肿瘤诱导结构改变。在包含 184 对 HNC CECT volume 与报告的多中心数据集上，针对五个临床显著、密集排列的解剖亚部位，SGRNet 达到 0.60 mean Average Precision（mAP），比强 volume-only 3D baseline 绝对提高 8.8 个百分点。

### 57. Error Detection for PET/CT Radiology Reports: Domain-Specific vs Large Language Models

**内容概述：** 针对 PET／CT 报告中的细微临床错误难以自动质控，论文以 30,633 份报告训练领域 BERT 并在 11,500 份 held-out benchmark 上比较开放 LLM；15M 模型达 94.4% balanced accuracy、5.8% FPR，显著优于最强 prompted LLM 的 84.0%，而大模型需 task-specific adaptation 才追平。

- 收录理由：论文直接评测影响患者治疗的报告错误 detection，并联合报告 false-positive rate、模型适配与计算成本，属于可部署临床内容安全质控。
- 论文/arXiv：[Error Detection for PET/CT Radiology Reports: Domain-Specific vs Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30021)

- 英文摘要：Errors in radiology reports can adversely affect patient treatment, yet automated report quality assurance remains challenging because errors are often subtle and require domain expertise to detect. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been proposed for radiology report verification, their ability to detect clinically meaningful errors beyond chest X-ray datasets remains under-explored. To this end, we present the first systematic evaluation of language models for PET/CT report error detection, comparing compact domain-specific models with SOTA open-weight LLMs. We collected 30,633 oncology FDG PET/CT reports from 23 radiologists over 10 years. We trained domain-specific BERT models to detect clinically motivated synthetic reporting errors and evaluated alongside zero-/few-shot Qwen3-32B, Gemma-3-27B and Llama-3.3-70B on a held-out benchmark of 11,500 reports. A 15M-parameter model achieved 94.4% balanced accuracy with a 5.8% false-positive rate, compared with 84.0% for the strongest prompted LLM. Task-specific adaptation of Llama-3.3-70B closed this performance gap (94.4%) but retained substantially greater computational requirements. Our results suggest that domain-specific training matters more than model scale for PET/CT report error detection, supporting compact models as an accurate and computationally efficient approach to automated radiology report quality assurance.
- 中文摘要：放射学报告中的错误会对患者治疗产生不利影响，但自动报告质量保证仍很困难，因为错误往往细微且需要领域专业知识才能发现。尽管近期有人提出用大型语言模型（LLM）验证放射学报告，它们在胸部 X 光数据集之外检测临床有意义错误的能力仍缺少研究。为此，我们首次系统评估 language model 的 PET／CT 报告错误检测能力，比较紧凑领域特定模型与 SOTA open-weight LLM。我们收集了 23 名放射科医生在十年间撰写的 30,633 份肿瘤学 FDG PET／CT 报告。我们训练领域特定 BERT model 检测由临床动机设计的合成报告错误，并在一个含 11,500 份报告的 held-out benchmark 上，与 zero-shot／few-shot Qwen3-32B、Gemma-3-27B 和 Llama-3.3-70B 一同评估。一个 15M 参数模型达到 94.4% balanced accuracy，false-positive rate 为 5.8%；最强 prompted LLM 为 84.0%。对 Llama-3.3-70B 作 task-specific adaptation 后可弥合性能差距（94.4%），但计算需求仍显著更高。结果说明，对 PET／CT 报告错误检测而言，领域特定训练比模型规模更重要，并支持使用紧凑模型作为准确、计算高效的自动放射报告质量保证方案。

### 58. Verification-Time Dependency on a Disappearing Evaluator

**内容概述：** 针对关键模型决策事后复核时原 evaluator 版本与执行上下文可能已消失，论文提出 Decision-State Commitment、Independent Verifiability、Counterfactual Auditability 和可选 VTPP；22 次服务退役中 17 次可用期不足 24 个月，但跨系列重配的 reversal 数字仅为描述性结果。

- 收录理由：论文给出模型服务生命周期中的 verification-time evidence binding 与独立复核协议，并用版本退役和行为反转数据说明审计依赖，属于具有可检验机制的技术 oversight。
- 论文/arXiv：[Verification-Time Dependency on a Disappearing Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29912)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29912`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CY`、`cs.SE`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：25 pages, 2 figures. Publication-ready preprint v8.3. Companion evidence and baseline records are cited in the manuscript
- 作者及单位：
  - Ho Wa Ku — 单位未核实
  - Jameel Ahmed Siddiqui — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：AI governance and assurance often assume that a consequential model-mediated decision can be reconstructed or tested after the fact. That assumption may fail when the evaluator that produced the decision is no longer accessible in the same version and execution context. This paper develops three verification-time constructs derived from Execution Governance (EG) 3.0: Decision-State Commitment, Independent Verifiability, and Counterfactual Auditability. Independent reprocessing of released Study 2 artifacts reproduces two original within-family behavioural comparisons: 52.0% modal-decision reversal for Llama 3.1 8B versus Llama 3.3 70B (26/50) and 30.0% for GPT-OSS 20B versus GPT-OSS 120B (15/50). The corrected baseline establishes that these are within-family comparisons, not provider-established succession. Post-hoc re-pairing against Groq-designated migration paths yields 64.0% and 38.0% reversal, but these figures remain descriptive because the cross-family invocation parameters were asymmetric. A 22-event retirement census independently recomputes to median 16.45 months, mean 18.72 months, range 3.9-40.3 months, with 17/22 intervals below 24 months, while also showing that evaluator availability can differ by service surface. The joint contribution is an operational verification-time protocol and optional Verification-Time Preservation Package (VTPP) specifying what evidence to bind at authorization time, what a separately trusted verifier can substantiate later, how stability and paired counterfactual tests should be calibrated, and which semantic checks remain beyond JSON Schema validity. The protocol is downstream and non-authorizing: it does not alter the EG Core Formula, add a seventh live condition, or state jurisdiction-specific legal admissibility.
- 中文摘要：AI governance 和 assurance 常假设，具有后果的模型介导决策可以在事后重建或测试。当产生该决策的 evaluator 已无法以相同版本和执行上下文访问时，这一假设可能失效。本文提出三个从 Execution Governance（EG）3.0 派生的 verification-time construct：Decision-State Commitment、Independent Verifiability 和 Counterfactual Auditability。对已发布 Study 2 artifact 的独立再处理复现了两个原始系列内行为比较：Llama 3.1 8B 对 Llama 3.3 70B 的 modal-decision reversal 为 52.0%（26/50），GPT-OSS 20B 对 GPT-OSS 120B 为 30.0%（15/50）。校正后的 baseline 说明，这些是系列内比较，而不是供应商确认的继任关系。针对 Groq 指定迁移路径的事后重新配对得到 64.0% 和 38.0% reversal，但由于跨系列调用参数不对称，这些数字仍只是描述性的。对 22 个退役事件的 census 独立重新计算出：中位时长 16.45 个月，平均 18.72 个月，范围 3.9–40.3 个月，其中 17/22 小于 24 个月；同时也显示 evaluator availability 可能随 service surface 而变化。联合贡献是一套 operational verification-time protocol 和可选的 Verification-Time Preservation Package（VTPP），规定授权时应绑定什么证据、独立信任的 verifier 日后可以证实什么、如何校准 stability 与 paired counterfactual test，以及哪些语义检查仍超出 JSON Schema validity。该协议位于下游且不执行授权：它不改变 EG Core Formula、不增加第七项 live condition，也不主张特定法域的法律可采性。

### 59. The Nearest Target Is the Wrong One: Target Separation in Arc2Face Identity Unlearning

**内容概述：** 针对 Arc2Face identity unlearning 把 embedding 重定向到最近目标后输出仍会被识别成原身份，论文把 target separation 作为设计变量；在最难 0.5% 邻居压力测试中，clean forgetting 从最近目标的 9/30 升至最不相似目标的 30/30，re-identification 从 51.9 降为 0 且 retention 持平。

- 收录理由：论文用锁定 verifier、独立 AdaFace 复核和目标到达率区分真实 identity redirection 与不可验证图像，直接检验视觉身份 unlearning 的残余泄漏。
- 论文/arXiv：[The Nearest Target Is the Wrong One: Target Separation in Arc2Face Identity Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30087)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30087`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CV`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：16 pages, accepted at the ECCV 2026 Workshop on Unlearning and Model Editing (U&ME)
- 作者及单位：
  - Zeynel Tok — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Unlearning an identity from a face-conditioned generator by redirecting its conditioning embedding can silently fail if the redirected output is still verified as the original person. We show that this failure depends on a controllable choice of how far the redirection target lies from the forget identity in recognition space, and that the most intuitive target, the nearest neighbour, is the one most likely to cause it. We audit Arc2Face with a locked ArcFace protocol and a projection adapter that redirects identity conditioning before generation. On a hard-neighbour stress test built from the hardest 0.5% of eligible identities, four target-selection policies show a monotonic response: clean forgetting rises from 9/30 groups under the nearest hard target to 30/30 under the least similar one. Mean forget-identity re-identification falls from 51.9 to 0.0 while mean retention stays flat. This reflects successful redirection rather than outputs becoming unverifiable: 710 of 720 least-sim-hard generations arrive at the chosen target, with no leakage to unrelated identities. Re-verifying identical images with an independent recogniser (AdaFace) preserves that trend, correlating at r=0.94, arguing against a verifier artefact. Target separation is thus a first-order, reportable design variable for identity unlearning.
- 中文摘要：通过重定向 conditioning embedding，从 face-conditioned generator 中 unlearn 某个身份时，如果重定向输出仍被验证为原人物，unlearning 可能静默失败。我们表明，这种失败取决于一个可控选择：重定向目标在 recognition space 中距离 forget identity 多远；而最直观的目标，即 nearest neighbour，恰恰最可能导致失败。我们使用锁定的 ArcFace protocol 和在生成前重定向 identity conditioning 的 projection adapter，对 Arc2Face 进行审计。在从合格身份中最难的 0.5% 构建的 hard-neighbour stress test 上，四种 target-selection policy 呈现单调响应：clean forgetting 从 nearest hard target 下的 9/30 组，提升到 least-similar target 下的 30/30。平均 forget-identity re-identification 从 51.9 降至 0.0，而平均 retention 保持不变。这反映成功重定向，而不是输出变得不可验证：720 个 least-sim-hard generation 中有 710 个到达所选目标，没有泄漏到无关身份。使用独立 recognizer（AdaFace）对相同图像重新验证后，该趋势仍保持，相关系数为 $r=0.94$，说明结果不是 verifier artifact。因此，target separation 是 identity unlearning 中一项一阶、应被报告的设计变量。

### 60. On the Recoverability of Private Information Unlearning in Large Language Models

**内容概述：** 针对 LLM unlearning 可能只隐藏而非删除私密信息，论文构建合成隐私数据和 white-box audit，评测五种方法后发现简单的 inverse-greedy decoding 即可逐步选择最低概率 token，恢复声称已遗忘的信息，说明现有方法经常未彻底消除敏感记忆。

- 收录理由：论文直接对 claimed forgetting 做 adversarial re-elicitation，并以统一框架检验私密信息可恢复性，符合“真实删除与表面抑制”区分。
- 论文/arXiv：[On the Recoverability of Private Information Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29943)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29943`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Shicheng Hu — 单位未核实
  - Runzhi Tian — 单位未核实
  - Ziqiao Wang — 单位未核实
  - Yongyi Mao — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models (LLMs) can memorize sensitive information, raising serious privacy concerns. Machine unlearning offers a potential solution to remove such information, but it remains unclear whether existing methods truly erase it or merely hide it within the model. A key challenge is quantifying the persistence of sensitive data under a unified evaluation framework. To address this, we construct a synthetic dataset containing fake private information and propose a white-box auditing framework to systematically assess whether claimed-forgotten information is genuinely removed. Using this framework, we evaluate five existing unlearning methods and find that a simple "inverse greedy" decoding -- selecting the least likely token at each step -- can recover supposedly forgotten private information. Our results reveal that current unlearning approaches often fail to fully eliminate sensitive information, highlighting the need for more reliable methods to ensure privacy in deployed LLMs.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）可能记忆敏感信息，由此产生严重隐私问题。Machine unlearning 为移除这类信息提供潜在方案，但现有方法是真正擦除它，还是只把它隐藏在模型中，仍不清楚。关键挑战是在统一评估框架下量化敏感数据的持久性。为此，我们构建一个包含虚假私人信息的合成数据集，并提出 white-box auditing framework，系统评估声称已遗忘的信息是否真正被移除。使用该框架评估五种现有 unlearning method 后，我们发现一种简单的 “inverse greedy” decoding，即每一步选择最低概率 token，就能恢复本应已经遗忘的私人信息。结果揭示，当前 unlearning 方法往往不能彻底消除敏感信息，说明需要更可靠的方法来确保已部署 LLM 的隐私。

### 61. On the Plasticity Collapse in Continual Machine Unlearning

**内容概述：** 针对现实系统需连续响应多次删除请求而研究多聚焦 single-shot unlearning，论文发现 plasticity collapse：连续操作在参数空间累积几何约束并使子空间饱和，既导致后续任务遗忘质量下降，也会使已遗忘信息自发 re-memorize，且该现象跨架构、数据集和方法普遍存在。

- 收录理由：论文揭示 continual unlearning 的 forward／backward 双重失效，后者直接破坏已有删除保证，为长期部署中的删除复测与 plasticity-preserving defense 提供机制依据。
- 论文/arXiv：[On the Plasticity Collapse in Continual Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29513)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29513`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/TIML-Group/Continual-Machine-Unlearning-Plasticity-Collapse)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Yingdan Shi — 单位未核实
  - Xiang Xu — 单位未核实
  - Kaize Ding — 单位未核实
  - Alfred O. Hero — 单位未核实
  - Ren Wang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Machine unlearning enables deep neural networks to selectively remove the influence of specific data in response to privacy and regulatory requirements. While prior work largely studies single-shot unlearning, real-world systems must accommodate continual unlearning, where multiple unlearning requests occur sequentially over time. In this work, we identify a fundamental limitation of this setting: plasticity collapse, a progressive breakdown in a model's ability to effectively forget. Through theoretical analysis of continual unlearning dynamics, we show that continual unlearning operations accumulate geometric constraints in parameter space, leading to saturated subspaces that restrict future updates. This structural effect induces two distinct failure modes: (1) Forward failure -- diminishing forgetting quality for subsequent tasks, and (2) Backward failure -- spontaneous re-memorization of previously forgotten information. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures, datasets, and methods in image classification confirm that plasticity collapse is not an artifact of specific implementations, but a pervasive phenomenon inherent to continual unlearning. Our findings reveal a critical barrier to the long-term reliability of machine unlearning systems and motivate the development of plasticity-preserving unlearning algorithms. Our code is available at https://github.com/TIML-Group/Continual-Machine-Unlearning-Plasticity-Collapse
- 中文摘要：Machine unlearning 使深度神经网络能够为响应隐私和监管要求，选择性移除特定数据的影响。此前工作主要研究 single-shot unlearning，但真实系统必须支持 continual unlearning，即多个删除请求随时间顺序发生。本文识别出该设置的一项基础局限：plasticity collapse，即模型有效遗忘能力的渐进性崩溃。通过对 continual unlearning dynamics 的理论分析，我们表明，连续 unlearning operation 会在参数空间中累积几何约束，形成限制未来更新的饱和子空间。这种结构效应诱发两种不同失效：（1）Forward failure，即后续任务的遗忘质量逐渐降低；（2）Backward failure，即此前已遗忘的信息自发 re-memorization。在图像分类任务中，跨多个架构、数据集和方法的大量实验确认，plasticity collapse 不是特定实现的 artifact，而是 continual unlearning 固有的普遍现象。我们的发现揭示 machine unlearning system 长期可靠性的一项关键障碍，并推动开发保持 plasticity 的 unlearning algorithm。代码位于 https://github.com/TIML-Group/Continual-Machine-Unlearning-Plasticity-Collapse。

### 62. Auditing and Mitigating Privacy Leakage in Cloud-Edge Collaborative Decoding

**内容概述：** 针对 cloud-edge collaborative decoding 虽把私有数据留在边缘、传输的 next-token distribution 仍可泄漏 private context，论文构建 QA 审计框架并提出 decoding-time defense CoVeil，动态优化传输信号，在最小准确率损失下相对基线最多减少 87.2% 数据泄漏。

- 收录理由：论文以可恢复 private-context information 为明确 threat model，审计边云协同接口的隐蔽泄漏并给出针对性传输缓解，而非一般 data minimization。
- 论文/arXiv：[Auditing and Mitigating Privacy Leakage in Cloud-Edge Collaborative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29111)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29111`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：EMNLP 2026 Findings
- 作者及单位：
  - Kejia Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Tianyuan Zou — 单位未核实
  - Zixuan GU — 单位未核实
  - Yang Liu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Applications such as personalized assistance and proprietary document analysis require large language models (LLMs) to generate outputs from private data. Yet powerful LLMs typically cannot be deployed on the resource-constrained devices where private data resides, and uploading private data to cloud-hosted LLMs exposes sensitive information. Recent work addresses this tension with a cloud-edge collaborative decoding paradigm, where private data are kept on the edge with a small language model (SLM) producing next-token distributions, which are fused with predictions from a cloud LLM operating solely on public data. In this paper, we systematically analyze the privacy risks of such a paradigm with a novel evaluation framework using constructed QA datasets, which show that such collaboration can expose substantial private-context information. To address such privacy leakage, we propose CoVeil, a defense mechanism which dynamically optimizes transmitted signals to suppress leakage during decoding time while preserving the collaborative quality. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that CoVeil consistently improves the privacy-utility trade-off over existing baselines by reducing data leakage by up to 87.2%, with minimal accuracy loss.
- 中文摘要：个性化助手和专有文档分析等应用要求大型语言模型（LLM）根据私有数据生成输出。然而，强大的 LLM 通常无法部署在私有数据所在的资源受限设备上，把私有数据上传到云端 LLM 又会暴露敏感信息。近期工作以 cloud-edge collaborative decoding 范式处理这一矛盾：私有数据保留在边缘，由 small language model（SLM）生成 next-token distribution，再与只在公共数据上运行的 cloud LLM prediction 融合。本文使用构造的 QA 数据集和新的评估框架，系统分析这一范式的隐私风险，并表明这种协作可能暴露大量 private-context information。为解决此类 privacy leakage，我们提出 CoVeil，一种在 decoding time 动态优化传输信号的防御机制，在保留协作质量的同时抑制泄漏。大量评估表明，相比现有基线，CoVeil 持续改善 privacy-utility trade-off，最多减少 87.2% 的数据泄漏，同时准确率损失很小。

### 63. SemTrace: Source-Grounded Semantic Signatures for Tracing LLM Exposure to Protected Documents

**内容概述：** 针对文档所有者无法控制或检查下游 LLM、难以判断某篇 review 是否接触受保护稿件，论文提出 SemTrace，从稿件直接支持的事实命题构建副本特定 binary semantic signature，并以 frozen NLI 解码显式 erasure，使 exposure detection 与表面措辞和具体模型解耦。

- 收录理由：论文保护的是文档 exposure 与副本 provenance，不依赖访问生成模型内部或 token sampler，直接对应受保护语料追踪和模型无关资产审计。
- 论文/arXiv：[SemTrace: Source-Grounded Semantic Signatures for Tracing LLM Exposure to Protected Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29575)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29575`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Junyan Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Yudong Zeng — 单位未核实
  - Yongwei Huang — 单位未核实
  - Zuhao Ouyang — 单位未核实
  - Hong Chen — 单位未核实
  - Xuming Hu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models are increasingly used to read documents and produce downstream text, creating a provenance problem when the document owner cannot control or inspect the model that performs the generation. We introduce SemTrace, a source-grounded semantic watermark for detecting whether a generated review was influenced by a known protected manuscript copy. Rather than biasing token probabilities or imposing surface-form patterns, SemTrace constructs a document-specific binary signature from factual propositions that are directly supported by the manuscript itself. A protected PDF invisibly carries a content contract that selects one fact from each binary pair and asks an instruction-following reviewer to express those facts in fixed review slots without changing its independent evaluation. A frozen natural language inference model then decodes the resulting semantic evidence with explicit erasures and scores the recovered bits against the codeword assigned to that copy. This design targets model-agnostic, assigned-copy exposure detection while keeping the watermark semantically tied to the source document.
- 中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地被用于阅读文档并生成下游文本；当文档所有者无法控制或检查执行生成的模型时，这会产生 provenance 问题。我们提出 SemTrace，一种 source-grounded semantic watermark，用于检测生成的 review 是否受某个已知受保护稿件副本影响。SemTrace 不对 token probability 施加偏置，也不强制表面形式 pattern，而是从稿件本身直接支持的事实命题中构建 document-specific binary signature。受保护 PDF 会不可见地携带一份 content contract：从每一对二元事实中选择一个，并要求 instruction-following reviewer 在固定 review slot 中表达这些事实，同时不改变其独立评价。随后，frozen natural language inference model 对所得语义证据进行解码，允许显式 erasure，并将恢复 bit 与分配给该副本的 codeword 比较评分。这一设计面向 model-agnostic、assigned-copy exposure detection，同时使 watermark 在语义上绑定源文档。

### 64. Token Counts Are Not Model Lineage: A Frozen-Threshold Holdout Study of Black-Box LLM API Fingerprinting

**内容概述：** 针对用 API 返回的 prompt-token count 推断 LLM 家族缺少独立 holdout 验证，论文对 24 对 endpoint 冻结 0.725 阈值并重复测量；合格 holdout 上 balanced accuracy 为 0.75、sensitivity 仅 0.50，因而该信号可指纹共享 tokenization stack，却不能作为 model-family lineage 的必要检验。

- 收录理由：论文对廉价 black-box API fingerprint 作预设阈值与故障有效性控制，给出 lineage attribution 的明确负面边界，直接服务模型服务谱系审计。
- 论文/arXiv：[Token Counts Are Not Model Lineage: A Frozen-Threshold Holdout Study of Black-Box LLM API Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29930)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29930`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：10 pages, 2 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Bo Chen — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Black-box model attribution is increasingly relevant when large language models (LLMs) are served through relay and reseller APIs. A tempting low-cost signal is the prompt-token count returned by an OpenAI-compatible endpoint: two models that share a tokenizer and chat template may produce the same count sequence up to a fixed offset. Yet the validity of this signal for broader \emph{model-family} attribution has received little direct holdout testing. We conduct a frozen-threshold study over 24 labeled endpoint pairs, split evenly into a development set and an untouched holdout set, with three temporal repeats and 30 controlled texts per pair. We introduce a validity-gated result contract that distinguishes an observed dissimilarity from an uninformative measurement caused by missing usage data, rate limits, or endpoint policy. The resulting shift-invariant exact-match score perfectly separates the 12 development pairs, yielding a frozen threshold of 0.725. On holdout, however, only 6 of 12 pairs are eligible under the pre-specified three-repeat rule. Among eligible pairs, balanced accuracy is 0.75, sensitivity is 0.50 (95\% Wilson interval 0.15--0.85), and specificity is 1.00 (0.342--1.00). Two same-family pairs---Qwen 3.8 and DeepSeek V4 variants---fall below the frozen threshold. Across 4,320 formal API calls, every log is replayable, while holdout contains 189 non-200 responses and 157 successful responses without prompt-token usage. The study therefore validates token-count consistency as a fingerprint of a shared \emph{tokenization stack}, but rejects its use as a standalone necessary test for model-family lineage.
- 中文摘要：当大型语言模型（LLM）通过 relay 和 reseller API 提供服务时，black-box model attribution 越来越重要。一个诱人的低成本信号是 OpenAI-compatible endpoint 返回的 prompt-token count：共享 tokenizer 和 chat template 的两个模型可能产生仅相差固定 offset 的相同 count sequence。然而，该信号用于更广泛 \emph{model-family} attribution 的有效性很少接受直接 holdout testing。我们对 24 对已标记 endpoint 开展 frozen-threshold study，把它们均分为 development set 和未接触 holdout set；每对进行三次时间重复，每次使用 30 个受控文本。我们提出 validity-gated result contract，区分观测到的差异与由 usage data 缺失、rate limit 或 endpoint policy 造成的无信息测量。所得 shift-invariant exact-match score 完美区分 12 对 development endpoint，得到冻结阈值 0.725。然而，在 holdout 上，按预先规定的三重复规则，12 对中只有 6 对合格。在合格 pair 中，balanced accuracy 为 0.75，sensitivity 为 0.50（95% Wilson interval 0.15–0.85），specificity 为 1.00（0.342–1.00）。两个同系列 pair，即 Qwen 3.8 和 DeepSeek V4 变体，低于冻结阈值。在 4,320 次正式 API 调用中，每份日志都可 replay；holdout 包含 189 个非 200 response，以及 157 个没有 prompt-token usage 的成功 response。因此，本研究确认 token-count consistency 可作为共享 \emph{tokenization stack} 的 fingerprint，但否定把它作为 model-family lineage 的独立必要检验。

### 65. Curvature Cryptanalysis of Smooth Transformer Feed-Forward Networks

**内容概述：** 针对 smooth Transformer FFN 在 chosen-input raw-output oracle 下可能泄漏内部结构，论文利用 projected input Hessian 的二阶混合分解，以 8,193 次黑盒查询恢复平均 cosine alignment 超 0.94 的隐藏方向，并拟合出 top-1 agreement 超 93% 的替代模型；调整 finite-difference step 还能抵消 rounding／noise 防御。

- 收录理由：论文建立从黑盒二阶观测到参数几何与功能替代的端到端 model extraction channel，并实证评估输出扰动防御的自适应绕过，直接涉及模型资产保护。
- 论文/arXiv：[Curvature Cryptanalysis of Smooth Transformer Feed-Forward Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28843)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28843`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`、`cs.AI`、`cs.CR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-28
- arXiv Comments：25 pages, 8 tables, 4 figures
- 作者及单位：
  - Munawar Hasan — 单位未核实
  - Apostol Vassilev — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：We show that smooth two-layer feed-forward networks (FFNs) expose an additional structural model extraction channel under a chosen-input raw-output oracle at the FFN branch; consider transformer FFN branches with GELU or SiLU activations under chosen-input raw-output access, without access to parameters, gradients, or internal activations; exploit a second-order leakage channel in which projected input Hessians form different mixtures of the same hidden symmetric rank-one factors induced by the FFN input weights. We formalize resulting Hessian collection as a partially symmetric decomposition to establish conditions for local identifiability and stability to exploit vector-output stencil reuse to reduce the structural query cost by a factor of 16. On independently trained CIFAR-10 vision transformers, only 16 projected Hessians, corresponding to 8193 black-box queries, recover the hidden FFN directions with average absolute cosine alignment above 0.94, with 95.1 % of GELU and 91.9 % of SiLU directions exceeding 0.90 alignment. Recovery remains high across independently trained models, repeated extraction runs, and all transformer blocks. The recovered structure supports functional extraction too. Keeping the recovered directions fixed and fitting only the remaining FFN parameters yields high-fidelity substitutes with more than 93 % top-1 agreement, while test accuracy remains within 0.90% and 0.62% of the GELU and SiLU targets. Output rounding and Gaussian noise substantially reduce recovery under a fixed attack configuration, but adapting the finite-difference step restores average alignment to 0.9603 and 0.9398. This is an end-to-end path from black-box second-order observations to hidden FFN-structure recovery and functional replacement. Under the stated oracle model, smooth FFN curvature exposes internal parameter geometry that behavioral fidelity alone cannot reveal.
- 中文摘要：我们表明，在 FFN branch 上具有 chosen-input raw-output oracle 时，平滑双层 feed-forward network（FFN）会暴露额外的结构性 model extraction channel。具体考虑带 GELU 或 SiLU activation 的 Transformer FFN branch：攻击者拥有 chosen-input raw-output access，但不能访问参数、gradient 或内部 activation；攻击利用一条二阶泄漏通道，其中 projected input Hessian 构成相同隐藏对称 rank-one factor 的不同混合，而这些 factor 由 FFN input weight 诱导。我们把所得 Hessian collection 形式化为 partially symmetric decomposition，以建立局部 identifiability 和 stability 条件；同时利用 vector-output stencil reuse，把结构查询成本降低 16 倍。在独立训练的 CIFAR-10 Vision Transformer 上，只需 16 个 projected Hessian，即 8,193 次黑盒查询，就能恢复平均绝对 cosine alignment 高于 0.94 的隐藏 FFN direction；95.1% 的 GELU direction 和 91.9% 的 SiLU direction alignment 超过 0.90。跨独立训练模型、重复 extraction run 和所有 Transformer block，恢复率均保持较高。恢复结构也支持 functional extraction。固定恢复方向，只拟合其余 FFN 参数，可得到 top-1 agreement 超过 93% 的高保真替代模型，同时 test accuracy 与 GELU、SiLU 目标分别相差不超过 0.90% 和 0.62%。在固定攻击配置下，output rounding 和 Gaussian noise 会显著降低恢复效果，但调整 finite-difference step 后，平均 alignment 可恢复至 0.9603 和 0.9398。这形成一条从黑盒二阶观测到隐藏 FFN 结构恢复和功能替代的端到端路径。在所述 oracle model 下，平滑 FFN curvature 会暴露仅凭行为保真度无法揭示的内部参数几何。

### 66. Membership is Ownership: A Robust Ownership Verification Framework for Diffusion Models

**内容概述：** 针对 diffusion model 训练时 watermark 会损伤 utility 且可被 post-hoc fine-tuning 削弱，论文提出 MiO，以私有 member evidence dataset 上的群体假设检验联合做 membership attribution 与 public-reference separation，在不改 owner model 的情况下验证所有权，并在盗窃后微调与权重扰动下保持稳定。

- 收录理由：论文把 membership inference 转用于 black-box model ownership verification，并显式评测 post-theft adaptation，符合生成模型 IP 保护与对抗稳健所有权审计。
- 论文/arXiv：[Membership is Ownership: A Robust Ownership Verification Framework for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28929)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28929`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.CV`
- 提交日期：2026-08-28
- arXiv Comments：This paper has been accepted to the IEEE International Conference on Data Mining (ICDM) 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Feng Jiang — 单位未核实
  - Zuobin Xiong — 单位未核实
  - An Huang — 单位未核实
  - Zhipeng Cai — 单位未核实
  - Yingshu Li — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large-scale diffusion models have fueled numerous profitable downstream applications for AI-related businesses, including visual editing and content creation. Meanwhile, due to the huge amount of resource consumption (e.g., computation and high-quality data) during training, such diffusion models are deemed valuable intellectual property (IP) for tech companies like OpenAI and Google. Yet, the IP assets are vulnerable to various unauthorized uses by adversaries seeking to steal models for customized, usually commercial applications. Some existing approaches have explored IP protection for AI models; however, they mostly face structural limitations in common --- using a training-time watermarking by injecting artifacts in the model, which can impose a measurable utility cost and can be weakened by post-hoc fine-tuning. To address these challenges, this work investigates IP protection (i.e., model ownership verification) for diffusion models in a realistic commercial scenario with minimal model utility loss. Specifically, the proposed method builds a framework for model ownership verification, termed ``{Membership is Ownership} (MiO)'', based on a population-level hypothesis test on a private member evidence dataset. MiO verifies ownership using two criteria: model attribution through membership inference and model separation from public references. Both are tested at $p<10^{-6}$. We evaluate MiO on DDIM and Stable Diffusion models without modifying the owner model or its sampling pipeline, and report ROC-AUC and true-positive rates at fixed nominal false-positive targets. Furthermore, MiO stays stable under different post-theft fine-tuning and weight perturbation in adversarial scenarios, reflecting better robustness compared to the watermarking methods.
- 中文摘要：大规模 diffusion model 推动了 AI 企业的许多盈利下游应用，包括视觉编辑和内容创作。同时，由于训练消耗大量资源（例如计算和高质量数据），这类 diffusion model 被 OpenAI、Google 等科技公司视为有价值的知识产权（IP）。然而，这些 IP asset 容易遭到攻击者未经授权使用；攻击者试图窃取模型，用于定制且通常具有商业目的的应用。已有方法探索 AI model IP 保护，但大多面临共同结构局限：在训练时向模型注入 artifact 以作 watermark，可能造成可测 utility cost，并可被 post-hoc fine-tuning 削弱。为解决这些问题，本文在模型 utility 损失尽可能小的真实商业场景中，研究 diffusion model IP 保护，即 model ownership verification。具体而言，本文基于 private member evidence dataset 上的 population-level hypothesis test，构建名为 “Membership is Ownership（MiO）” 的 model ownership verification framework。MiO 用两个标准验证所有权：通过 membership inference 进行 model attribution，以及将模型与 public reference 分离。二者都在 $p<10^{-6}$ 下检验。我们在 DDIM 和 Stable Diffusion model 上评估 MiO，不修改 owner model 或其 sampling pipeline，并报告 ROC-AUC 和固定 nominal false-positive target 下的 true-positive rate。此外，在 adversarial scenario 中，MiO 面对不同 post-theft fine-tuning 和 weight perturbation 仍保持稳定，相比 watermarking method 表现出更强稳健性。

### 67. Ouroboros: Self-Referential Backdoor Attacks on Speech Enhancement via Clean Audio Triggers

**内容概述：** 针对 speech enhancement 被动处理场景无法依赖主动注入 trigger、相关后门尚未被探索，论文提出 Ouroboros，把模型理想 clean output 本身作为自然触发器，无需推理时外部注入即可近乎完美激活，并由真实录制未修改音频触发、扩展到定向内容篡改且抵抗常见过滤和微调。

- 收录理由：论文为语音前端建立 self-referential clean-trigger backdoor threat model，并完成 physical-world validation，直接扩展多模态后门的触发机制与内容完整性后果。
- 论文/arXiv：[Ouroboros: Self-Referential Backdoor Attacks on Speech Enhancement via Clean Audio Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30329)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30329`

- 英文摘要：Speech enhancement models are widely deployed as frontend modules in real-time speech services, yet their vulnerability to backdoor attacks remains unexplored. Existing backdoor methods are confined to classification tasks and rely on active trigger injection, an assumption incompatible with the passive processing nature of speech enhancement models. In this paper, we propose Ouroboros, a novel backdoor attack framework that leverages the ideal clean outputs of speech enhancement models as natural triggers, enabling inference-time activation without any external trigger injection. Extensive evaluations show Ouroboros achieves near-perfect attack success rates with minimal performance degradation on diverse models and datasets. Physical-world validations confirm that naturally recorded, unaltered clean audio can reliably activate the backdoor. Moreover, Ouroboros generalizes to targeted content-tampering attacks and remains effective against common filtering and finetuning defenses.
- 中文摘要：Speech enhancement model 被广泛部署为实时语音服务的前端模块，但其对 backdoor attack 的脆弱性仍未被探索。现有 backdoor method 局限于分类任务，并依赖主动 trigger injection；这一假设与 speech enhancement model 的被动处理性质不兼容。本文提出 Ouroboros，一个新的 backdoor attack framework，它把 speech enhancement model 的理想 clean output 用作自然 trigger，因此无需任何外部 trigger injection 即可在 inference time 激活。大量评估表明，Ouroboros 在多种模型和数据集上实现近乎完美的攻击成功率，而性能退化很小。Physical-world validation 确认，自然录制、未经修改的 clean audio 能可靠激活后门。此外，Ouroboros 可泛化到定向 content-tampering attack，并对常见 filtering 与 finetuning defense 保持有效。

### 68. GhostSplat: Input-Triggered Backdoors for Multi-View-Consistent 3D Content Manipulation in Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting

**内容概述：** 针对现有 NeRF／3DGS 后门只修改单场景而不能在共享 generator weight 中持久化，论文提出 GhostSplat，用低幅输入图案触发 unseen scene 上的 attacker payload，并以 3D 锚定保持跨视角一致；最强注入／删除设置 ASR 达 96%／100%、无观察到的假阳性且抗 JPEG、模糊和重采样。

- 收录理由：论文明确共享 3D generator 的 supply-chain backdoor，并证明同一 consistency set projection 无法移除已落入该集合的 payload，为 3D 视觉后门防御划定具体边界。
- 论文/arXiv：[GhostSplat: Input-Triggered Backdoors for Multi-View-Consistent 3D Content Manipulation in Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29184)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29184`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

  - Shan Huang — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructs a 3D scene from sparse images in one forward pass. Its shared pretrained weights also expose a supply-chain attack surface. Existing Neural Radiance Field and 3DGS backdoors modify individual scenes and activate at selected viewpoints; they do not install persistent behavior in shared generator weights. We introduce GhostSplat, an input-triggered backdoor that installs such behavior in feed-forward 3DGS. A low-amplitude pattern added to the input images causes the poisoned generator to render an attacker-chosen payload on unseen victim scenes. Anchoring the payload to a 3D point and reprojecting it into each target view makes the payload multi-view consistent. Exact projection onto the generator's representation-specific consistency set leaves a realized payload unchanged because the output already belongs to that set. The GhostSplat training framework succeeds across three architectures (MVSplat, pixelSplat, DepthSplat) and two datasets (RealEstate10K, ACID). Its strongest evaluated injection and deletion settings reach 96% and 100% ASR, respectively, with zero observed false positives while surviving JPEG, blur, and resampling. Defenses that use only that exact projection are therefore insufficient; effective mitigation requires information or intervention beyond same-set consistency projection.
- 中文摘要：Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）只需一次 forward pass，即可从稀疏图像重建 3D 场景。其共享预训练权重也暴露出 supply-chain attack surface。现有 Neural Radiance Field 和 3DGS backdoor 会修改单个场景并在选定视点激活；它们不会在共享 generator weight 中安装持久行为。我们提出 GhostSplat，一种在 feed-forward 3DGS 中安装这种行为的 input-triggered backdoor。在输入图像上添加低幅 pattern，会使 poisoned generator 在未见过的 victim scene 上渲染攻击者选择的 payload。把 payload 锚定到 3D 点并重新投影到每个目标视图，可使 payload 保持 multi-view consistency。把输出精确投影到 generator 的 representation-specific consistency set 后，已经实现的 payload 保持不变，因为该输出本来就属于该集合。GhostSplat training framework 在三个架构（MVSplat、pixelSplat、DepthSplat）和两个数据集（RealEstate10K、ACID）上均成功。最强的 injection 和 deletion 设置分别达到 96% 和 100% ASR，没有观察到 false positive，并能经受 JPEG、blur 和 resampling。因此，只使用这种精确 projection 的防御并不充分；有效缓解需要超出 same-set consistency projection 的信息或干预。

### 69. ARMOR: Manifold-Oriented Training for Adversarially Robust Aerial Object Detection under Data Scarcity

**内容概述：** 针对航空目标检测易受物理 universal patch 且部署点只有数百张标注图像，论文提出低数据防御 ARMOR，通过背景遮蔽保留对象特征并向对象注入随机 patch；面对 transfer 与 defense-aware 攻击，clean confidence 保持 0.90 以上、adversarial confidence 最多比 SOTA 提高 0.32，打印 patch 实验亦成立。

- 收录理由：论文给出 physically realizable、query-free／adaptive 两类 threat model，并在稀缺训练数据约束和实物部署下验证检测器防御，属于明确的视觉对抗安全研究。
- 论文/arXiv：[ARMOR: Manifold-Oriented Training for Adversarially Robust Aerial Object Detection under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29510)

- 英文摘要：Aerial object detection is increasingly deployed in real-world applications, but models remain vulnerable to physical, universal adversarial patches that cause them to miss objects. Furthermore, defenders face the practical constraint of training data scarcity: aerial imagery is costly to collect and label, so a deployment site typically yields hundreds of images rather than the tens of thousands that adversarial robustness benchmarks assume. To tackle model vulnerability and training data scarcity, we propose Adversarial Robustness with Manifold-Oriented Training (ARMOR), a novel defense that realizes the core insights of on-manifold adversarial training (OMAT) in low-data regimes. ARMOR builds on the insight of OMAT to model the data manifold - the compact structure capturing the data's relevant features - to learn and robustify these features during training. While OMAT relies on the data-intensive operations of training large generative models and adversarial training to achieve this, ARMOR adopts a data-efficient approach that reuses labels the detection task already supplies: ARMOR (i) masks image backgrounds to retain object-relevant features, and (ii) injects randomized patches on objects to improve feature robustness. Our low-data experiments with physically-realizable adversarial patches evaluate both query-free transfer attacks and defense-aware attacks. ARMOR maintains strong clean performance of over 0.90 model confidence, while improving adversarial robustness by up to 0.32 in model confidence over state-of-the-art defenses. Physical experiments with printed patches confirm that these gains survive deployment. Overall, ARMOR translates insights from manifold-based training to defend object detectors amidst training data scarcity.
- 中文摘要：航空目标检测正越来越多地部署于真实应用，但模型仍容易受到会使其漏检目标的物理 universal adversarial patch 攻击。此外，防御者还面临训练数据稀缺这一实践约束：航空图像的采集与标注成本高，一个部署地点通常只能产生数百张图像，而 adversarial robustness benchmark 假设有数万张。为同时处理模型脆弱性和训练数据稀缺，我们提出 Adversarial Robustness with Manifold-Oriented Training（ARMOR），一种在低数据 regime 中实现 on-manifold adversarial training（OMAT）核心思想的新防御。ARMOR 建立在 OMAT 的洞见上：对 data manifold，即捕获数据相关 feature 的紧凑结构进行建模，以在训练中学习并强化这些 feature。OMAT 依赖训练大型生成模型和 adversarial training 等数据密集操作；ARMOR 则采用数据高效方法，复用检测任务已有标签：（i）遮蔽图像背景以保留 object-relevant feature；（ii）在对象上注入随机 patch，以提高 feature robustness。我们的低数据实验使用 physically realizable adversarial patch，同时评估 query-free transfer attack 和 defense-aware attack。ARMOR 保持超过 0.90 model confidence 的强 clean performance，并相对 SOTA defense 最多提高 0.32 adversarial model confidence。使用打印 patch 的物理实验确认，这些增益在部署中仍然存在。总体而言，ARMOR 把 manifold-based training 的洞见转化为训练数据稀缺条件下的 object detector 防御。

### 70. Text-Guided Diffusion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Chest X-Ray Images

**内容概述：** 针对胸片攻击多局限于像素范数、难以代表可信放射学变化，论文冻结 diffusion generator 与 classifier、只优化文本条件生成对抗图像，将二元／多病种 AUROC 分别降至 0.3885–0.5646／0.4441–0.4878，同时 95.9%／73.8% 图像仍不改变临床医生判断。

- 收录理由：论文建立高风险医疗分类的 generative threat model，并以 clinician–model disagreement 证明攻击在视觉与临床上似真却能显著劫持模型决策。
- 论文/arXiv：[Text-Guided Diffusion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29456)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29456`
