- 英文摘要：As artificial intelligence is increasingly integrated into chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation, triage, and clinical decision support, understanding its vulnerability to adversarial manipulation is critical for safe deployment. Existing robustness evaluations, however, predominantly rely on pixel-space attacks that introduce numerically constrained perturbations but may not represent plausible radiographic variation. This limitation is particularly important in multi-disease CXR classification, where models simultaneously evaluate multiple overlapping pathologies and adversarial failures may alter several diagnostic predictions. We propose a text-guided diffusion-based adversarial framework that optimizes learnable text conditioning while keeping the diffusion generator and target classifier frozen, enabling adversarial generation through a learned image prior rather than direct pixel manipulation. We evaluate the framework across multiple classifier architectures in both binary atelectasis and multi-disease CXR classification and compare it with FGSM, PGD, and Carlini-Wagner attacks. Our approach consistently produced the greatest degradation in classifier performance, reducing AUROC to 0.3885-0.5646 in binary classification and 0.4441-0.4878 in the multi-disease setting, while achieving superior image fidelity (SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23). Importantly, clinician interpretation remained unchanged for 95.9% of binary and 73.8% of multi-disease adversarial images despite substantial changes in model predictions. These findings reveal a clinically important discrepancy between human and machine interpretation and demonstrate the need to extend medical AI robustness evaluation beyond conventional pixel-space attacks toward generative threat models that can expose failures under visually and clinically plausible image variations.
- 中文摘要：随着 AI 越来越多地集成到胸部 X 光（CXR）解释、分诊和临床决策支持中，理解其对 adversarial manipulation 的脆弱性对安全部署至关重要。然而，现有 robustness evaluation 主要依赖 pixel-space attack；这类攻击引入数值受限 perturbation，却不一定代表合理的放射学变化。这一局限在 multi-disease CXR classification 中尤其重要，因为模型同时评估多种重叠病理，adversarial failure 可能改变多个诊断预测。我们提出一个 text-guided diffusion-based adversarial framework，在保持 diffusion generator 和 target classifier 冻结的同时，优化可学习文本条件，使攻击通过 learned image prior 而不是直接 pixel manipulation 生成。我们在二元肺不张和多病种 CXR 分类中，跨多个 classifier architecture 评估该框架，并与 FGSM、PGD 和 Carlini-Wagner attack 比较。我们的方法持续造成最大的 classifier performance degradation：二元分类 AUROC 降至 0.3885–0.5646，多病种设置降至 0.4441–0.4878；同时取得更好的 image fidelity（SSIM 0.9080、LPIPS 0.1670、FID 51.23）。重要的是，尽管模型预测发生显著变化，临床医生对 95.9% 的二元攻击图像和 73.8% 的多病种攻击图像仍保持原解释。这些发现揭示人与机器解释之间具有临床重要性的差异，并说明医疗 AI robustness evaluation 需要从传统 pixel-space attack 扩展到 generative threat model，以暴露视觉和临床上合理的图像变化所造成的失效。

### 71. OASIS: Optimizing Attacker Sequences for Hard-Label Black-Box Text Attacks

**内容概述：** OASIS 将硬标签黑盒文本攻击中的多个攻击器视为可优化序列，通过一次性双目标搜索兼顾攻击成功率与扰动幅度，并在不同数据集、受害模型和大语言模型上优于单一攻击器与人工组合链。

- 收录理由：直接研究硬标签黑盒文本对抗攻击，并把攻击器组合本身定义为新的攻击优化面，属于模型输入攻击与鲁棒性评估。
- 论文/arXiv：[OASIS: Optimizing Attacker Sequences for Hard-Label Black-Box Text Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29568)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29568`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30

- 作者及单位：
  - Qian Chen — 单位未核实
  - Shiliang Xiao — 单位未核实
  - Yuzhi Liang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Different attack methods follow different search trajectories, they succeed on different subsets of samples, whereas existing hard-label black-box text attacks mainly focus on improving individual attackers or manually combining them. We present {\OURS}, a method for optimizing attacker sequences in hard-label black-box text attacks. {\OURS} first performs a one-time bi-objective attack chain search over candidate sequences to balance attack success rate and perturbation, and then reuses the selected fixed global chain during attack chain execution. Experiments across multiple datasets, victim models, and large language models show that {\OURS} consistently outperforms strong standalone baselines and simple manually constructed chains. These results suggest that attacker composition is not merely an implementation choice, but a practical optimization target for improving hard-label black-box text attacks.
- 中文摘要：不同攻击方法遵循不同的搜索轨迹，并会在不同的样本子集上成功；然而，现有硬标签黑盒文本攻击主要着眼于改进单个攻击器或以人工方式组合攻击器。我们提出 OASIS，一种为硬标签黑盒文本攻击优化攻击器序列的方法。OASIS 首先在候选序列上执行一次性的双目标攻击链搜索，以平衡攻击成功率和扰动幅度，之后在攻击链执行期间复用所选的固定全局攻击链。跨多个数据集、受害模型和大语言模型的实验表明，OASIS 持续优于强单体基线和简单的人工构造攻击链。这些结果说明，攻击器组合并非仅仅是一项实现选择，而是提升硬标签黑盒文本攻击的一个实际优化目标。

### 72. JITterFlip: Uncovering Fault Attack Surfaces in JIT-Compiled LLM Serving

**内容概述：** JITterFlip 首次把位翻转攻击面从模型参数和计算代码转向 GPU LLM 推理的 CPU 侧 JIT 服务控制平面，可制造乱码输出或在保持输出完全正确时将延迟放大最高 124.97 倍，并以端到端 Rowhammer 实验验证攻击。

- 收录理由：揭示云端 LLM 推理栈中新的可用性与完整性攻击面，并实证绕过现有 LLM 位翻转防御，威胁模型和攻击后果均直接关联 AI 服务安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[JITterFlip: Uncovering Fault Attack Surfaces in JIT-Compiled LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29745)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29745`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Tairui Wang — 单位未核实
  - Zhi Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Yansong Gao — 单位未核实
  - Xin Zhang — 单位未核实
  - Qingni Shen — 单位未核实
  - Zhonghai Wu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：LLMs are widely deployed through cloud-hosted inference services, where Just-in-Time (JIT) compilation is used to reduce recurring framework and GPU-launch overhead. JIT serving introduces a host-side control plane that selects compiled artifacts and orchestrates their execution on the GPU. Meanwhile, the shared cloud setting has motivated a growing body of bit-flip attacks (BFAs) against LLM/DNN inference. Most existing BFAs target model parameters or weights and require model-specific knowledge. A smaller body of work reduces this dependency by faulting executable code, yet still corrupts code that directly implements model computation, limiting their attack effect to inference depletion. We present JITterFlip, the first BFA targeting the host-side JIT serving control plane of GPU-based LLM inference. By faulting CPU-resident serving decisions rather than model computation, JITterFlip enables both gibberish output generation and a correct-output sponge attack. To identify exploitable targets in a large JIT compiler stack, JITterFlip develops a decision-guided fault-vulnerable code analysis. Across four text and multimodal LLM workloads, the identified vulnerable code faults exhibit cross-model transferability, produce gibberish outputs with PPL ratios of $15.45\times$ to $2.48{\times}10^{6}\times$, and demonstrate correct-output sponge attacks with latency amplification of $2.03\times$ to $181.90\times$. JITterFlip also bypasses recent BFA defenses for LLMs while retaining both attack effects. Last, we demonstrate end-to-end Rowhammer attacks across four LLMs: a single bit flip in CPU-resident branch code propagates across the CPU-GPU boundary to disrupt GPU-executed inference without direct access to GPU memory, reaching up to $7.23{\times}10^{6}\times$ PPL amplification or $124.97\times$ latency amplification while preserving the exact generated output.
- 中文摘要：大语言模型广泛通过云托管推理服务部署，其中即时（JIT）编译被用于减少反复出现的框架和 GPU 启动开销。JIT 服务引入了一个主机侧控制平面，用于选择已编译产物并编排它们在 GPU 上的执行。与此同时，共享云环境催生了越来越多针对 LLM/DNN 推理的位翻转攻击（BFA）。大多数现有 BFA 以模型参数或权重为目标，并需要特定于模型的知识。少量工作通过破坏可执行代码降低了这种依赖，但其破坏的仍是直接实现模型计算的代码，因此攻击效果局限于推理能力耗损。我们提出 JITterFlip，这是首个以基于 GPU 的 LLM 推理之主机侧 JIT 服务控制平面为目标的 BFA。通过干扰 CPU 驻留的服务决策而非模型计算，JITterFlip 既能生成乱码输出，也能实施输出正确的海绵攻击。为从大型 JIT 编译器栈中识别可利用目标，JITterFlip 开发了决策引导的故障脆弱代码分析。在四种文本和多模态 LLM 工作负载上，所识别的脆弱代码故障表现出跨模型可迁移性，能够生成 PPL 比率为 15.45 倍至 2.48×10^6 倍的乱码输出，并实现延迟放大 2.03 倍至 181.90 倍的正确输出海绵攻击。JITterFlip 还绕过了近期面向 LLM 的 BFA 防御，同时保留两类攻击效果。最后，我们在四个 LLM 上展示了端到端 Rowhammer 攻击：CPU 驻留分支代码中的单个位翻转可跨越 CPU–GPU 边界，破坏在 GPU 上执行的推理，而无需直接访问 GPU 内存；其 PPL 放大最高达到 7.23×10^6 倍，或在保持生成输出完全一致的同时将延迟放大 124.97 倍。

### 73. Frontier vision-language models have overtaken young adults at detecting AI-generated portraits -- but not their calibration

**内容概述：** 该研究在同一组真实及身份匹配的生成肖像上比较 19 个前沿视觉语言模型与 1,667 名成年人，发现 2026 年 7 月的新模型已在检测准确率上超过年轻成年人，但判断阈值偏差和示例敏感性仍显示其校准不及人类。

- 收录理由：直接评估前沿多模态模型识别 AI 生成肖像的真实性检测能力，并揭示准确率之外的校准与信任偏差，属于生成内容检测安全评测。
- 论文/arXiv：[Frontier vision-language models have overtaken young adults at detecting AI-generated portraits -- but not their calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30210)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30210`

- 英文摘要：AI image generators now create face portraits that are hard to tell from real photographs. Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed to flag such images. We benchmarked 19 VLMs on the same 198 face portraits -- real photographs and identity-matched ChatGPT-4o and Imagen 3 versions -- under the same task as our earlier study of 1,667 adults (85% correct overall; accuracy fell steeply with age). The June-2026 cohort of 14 models only matched adults in their 20s-30s. Four weeks later the ceiling broke. Among five July-2026 releases under the identical protocol, gpt-5.6-sol reached 92.8% balanced accuracy (five-draw mean 92.1%), clearly above adults in their 20s (88.5%), and claude-fable-5 detected every AI image while averaging 91.9%. Model sensitivity now exceeds young adults decisively (d' up to 3.4 versus ~ 2.4). What has not been overtaken is human calibration. Model criteria spread from c = -1.10 to +1.45 while humans sit near zero at every age; both new leaders are biased (+0.44, -0.97), and only a few mid-ranked models approach the human balance. Changing the labelled examples still flipped about one answer in four. The best machines now out-see young adults here, without matching the human balance between suspicion and trust.
- 中文摘要：AI 图像生成器如今能够创建难以与真实照片区分的人脸肖像。视觉语言模型（VLM）正越来越多地被建议用于标记此类图像。我们使用与此前针对 1,667 名成年人研究相同的任务，在同一组 198 张人脸肖像上对 19 个 VLM 进行基准测试；这些肖像包括真实照片，以及与身份匹配的 ChatGPT-4o 和 Imagen 3 生成版本。人类总体正确率为 85%，且准确率随年龄增长急剧下降。由 14 个模型组成的 2026 年 6 月批次只能追平 20–30 多岁的成年人。四周后，这一上限被突破。在相同协议下测试的五个 2026 年 7 月发布模型中，gpt-5.6-sol 达到 92.8% 的平衡准确率（五次抽样均值为 92.1%），明显高于 20 多岁成年人（88.5%）；claude-fable-5 检出了所有 AI 图像，平均准确率为 91.9%。模型敏感度如今已明确超过年轻成年人（d' 最高 3.4，而人类约为 2.4）。尚未超过人类的是校准能力。模型判定标准从 c = -1.10 到 +1.45 不等，而各年龄段人类均接近零；两个新领先模型也都存在偏差（+0.44、-0.97），只有少数排名居中的模型接近人类的平衡水平。改变带标签示例仍会使约四分之一的回答发生翻转。就这项任务而言，最好的机器如今比年轻成年人看得更准，却仍未达到人类在怀疑与信任之间的平衡。

### 74. IndicDetect: Evaluating Cross-Lingual LLM-Generated Text Detection for Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil

**内容概述：** IndicDetect 为印地语、泰卢固语和泰米尔语构建跨领域、跨生成器并含对抗扰动的 AI 文本检测基准，显示现有检测器的主要短板是分布偏移下的鲁棒性而非同分布峰值准确率。

- 收录理由：系统测量低资源语言 AI 生成文本检测器在未知生成器、领域迁移和对抗攻击下的失效，直接服务于生成内容溯源与检测安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[IndicDetect: Evaluating Cross-Lingual LLM-Generated Text Detection for Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29919)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29919`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 提交日期：2026-08-30

  - Junchao Wu — 单位未核实
  - Nilesh Dokuparthi — 单位未核实
  - Greeshma Yaluru — 单位未核实
  - Tatiana Muniz Rodriguez — 单位未核实
  - Lidia S. Chao — 单位未核实
  - Derek F. Wong — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：The rapid proliferation of LLMs has further heightened the need to develop dependable AI-generated text detection, especially beyond English. Nevertheless, current benchmarks pay little attention to Indic languages and test detectors in idealized settings that do not represent the real world. We present a generalized benchmark for AI-generated text detection in Hindi, Telugu, and Tamil, which we call IndicDetect, designed to assess the robustness of detectors under realistic distribution shifts. IndicDetect comprises highly curated human-written texts matched with LLM-generated counterparts across various domains and generators, and systematically evaluates detectors in the presence of domain shift, generator shift, and adversarial perturbation. Using a single and repeatable evaluation scheme, we evaluate a wide range of statistical and neural detectors. We find substantial robustness failures: supervised neural detectors perform well in-distribution, while training-free methods degrade considerably under unseen generators and adversarial attacks. The severity of these failures varies across languages, with Hindi exhibiting the largest overall degradation under adversarial perturbations. These results highlight that the primary weakness of existing detectors in Indic settings lies in their robustness, not in their peak accuracy. IndicDetect provides standard data splits, an evaluation protocol, and baselines to establish a robust, language-aware foundation for AI-generated text detection in Indic scripts.
- 中文摘要：大语言模型的快速普及进一步提高了开发可靠 AI 生成文本检测技术的必要性，尤其是在英语之外的语言中。然而，当前基准很少关注印度语言，并且通常在无法代表现实世界的理想化环境中测试检测器。我们提出 IndicDetect，这是一个面向印地语、泰卢固语和泰米尔语的通用 AI 生成文本检测基准，旨在评估检测器在真实分布偏移下的鲁棒性。IndicDetect 包含经过高度筛选的人工文本，以及跨多个领域和生成器与其匹配的 LLM 生成文本，并系统评估检测器在领域偏移、生成器偏移和对抗扰动下的表现。我们采用统一且可复现的评估方案，测试了广泛的统计检测器与神经检测器。结果发现明显的鲁棒性失效：监督式神经检测器在同分布条件下表现良好，而无需训练的方法在面对未见生成器和对抗攻击时显著退化。失效严重程度因语言而异，其中印地语在对抗扰动下的总体退化最大。这些结果表明，现有检测器在印度语言环境中的首要弱点是鲁棒性，而非峰值准确率。IndicDetect 提供标准数据划分、评估协议和基线，为印度文字体系中的 AI 生成文本检测建立一个鲁棒且具语言意识的基础。

### 75. When Less is More: Understanding When Token Filtering Helps and Fails in AI-generated Text Detection

**内容概述：** 该研究用熵差分数和 top-k 累积概率过滤分析零样本 AI 文本检测，发现仅保留 40% token 有时最优，但收益取决于源模型强弱，并将失效归因于 token 级熵校准的双向权衡。

- 收录理由：解释 AI 生成文本检测中 token 证据何时会系统性损害判断，明确检测方法在不同生成模型下的机制边界，属于生成内容检测可靠性研究。
- 论文/arXiv：[When Less is More: Understanding When Token Filtering Helps and Fails in AI-generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29903)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29903`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：Accepted to EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
- 作者及单位：
  - Xiaoyang Han — 单位未核实
  - Lvxiaowei Xu — 单位未核实
  - Ming Cai — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has made AI-generated text detection increasingly critical. Existing zero-shot detectors assume that more token-level evidence leads to more reliable detection. However, our empirical study challenges this consensus: fewer tokens sometimes work better, retaining only 40% can yield optimal performance, yet this benefit is not universal. Using the Entropy Gap Score (EGS), we introduce top-$k$ cumulative probability filtering as a diagnostic probe. Across three representative settings, filtering exhibits strikingly different behaviors. We analyze EGS via typical set theory and quantify its dynamics through entropy calibration and distribution analysis. We find that filtering helps for weak source LMs, where low-entropy tokens are harmful, but fails for strong source LMs, where they are not notably harmful. Our work provides the first systematic analysis showing that some tokens are not merely uninformative but systematically harmful due to entropy miscalibration, revealing a two-sided trade-off in token-level detection.
- 中文摘要：大语言模型的快速发展使 AI 生成文本检测变得愈发关键。现有零样本检测器假设，更多 token 级证据会带来更可靠的检测。然而，我们的实证研究挑战了这一共识：更少的 token 有时反而更好，仅保留 40% 即可取得最优性能，但这种收益并非普遍存在。我们使用熵差分数（EGS），引入 top-k 累积概率过滤作为诊断探针。在三种代表性设置中，过滤呈现出显著不同的行为。我们利用典型集理论分析 EGS，并通过熵校准和分布分析量化其动态。我们发现，对于较弱的源语言模型，低熵 token 会造成损害，因此过滤有效；对于较强的源语言模型，低熵 token 并无显著危害，因此过滤失效。我们的工作首次系统表明，由于熵校准失配，某些 token 不仅信息量不足，还会系统性地产生负面作用，从而揭示 token 级检测中的双向权衡。

### 76. Beyond Speech: Dual-Domain SSL Fusion for Unified All-Type Audio Deepfake Detection

**内容概述：** 该方法融合 EAT-large 与 wav2vec 2.0 的事件级和波形级自监督特征，在未知音频类型下统一检测语音、环境声、歌声和音乐深度伪造，并在 AT-ADD Track 2 取得 95.58% Macro-F1。

- 收录理由：面向现实中音频类型未知的统一深度伪造检测威胁，覆盖多类合成音频而非单一语音场景，直接关联生成音频真实性防护。
- 论文/arXiv：[Beyond Speech: Dual-Domain SSL Fusion for Unified All-Type Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29021)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29021`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- 作者及单位：
  - Cunhang Fan — 单位未核实
  - Junqin Cao — 单位未核实

  - Jun Xue — 单位未核实
  - Zhao Lv — 单位未核实
  - Xin Fang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Unified all-type audio deepfake detection aims to determine whether an input clip is real or fake when its audio type may be speech, environmental sound, singing voice, or music. Existing speech-centric or type-dependent solutions are insufficient for this setting because the test-time audio type is unknown, while the required output is still a single binary decision. To address these issues, this paper proposes a dual-domain SSL fusion method that maps heterogeneous audio into a shared binary authenticity space. EAT-large and wav2vec 2.0 XLS-R-300M are used as complementary SSL feature sources, providing broad acoustic and event-level representations as well as waveform-level, vocal, and speech-sensitive representations. Layer-wise weighted fusion integrates multi-level artifacts from different transformer depths, while token-level fusion forms a unified feature pool without enforcing frame-level alignment between the two SSL streams. The fused tokens are summarized by multi-head attentive statistics pooling and classified with a binary MLP head. With conservative speech refinement applied on top of this unified core detector, the submitted system achieves 95.58% Macro-F1 on the AT-ADD Track 2 evaluation set and ranks second in the challenge.
- 中文摘要：统一的全类型音频深度伪造检测旨在判断输入片段是真实还是伪造，而其音频类型可能是语音、环境声、歌声或音乐。现有以语音为中心或依赖音频类型的方案无法满足这一设置，因为测试时音频类型未知，但所需输出仍是单一的二元判定。为解决这些问题，本文提出一种双域自监督学习（SSL）融合方法，将异构音频映射到共享的二元真实性空间。我们使用 EAT-large 和 wav2vec 2.0 XLS-R-300M 作为互补的 SSL 特征来源，前者提供广泛的声学与事件级表征，后者提供波形级、声音及语音敏感表征。逐层加权融合整合不同 Transformer 深度的多层次伪造痕迹，而 token 级融合在不强制两个 SSL 流进行帧级对齐的情况下形成统一特征池。融合后的 token 通过多头注意力统计池化汇总，并由二元 MLP 头分类。在这一统一核心检测器之上采用保守的语音细化后，提交系统在 AT-ADD Track 2 评估集上取得 95.58% 的 Macro-F1，并在挑战赛中排名第二。

### 77. WoE Wrote It? Watermarking Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Black-Box Text Provenance

**内容概述：** WoE 通过偏置稀疏混合专家模型中特定专家的词表，将水印嵌入模型参数，使其在权重失窃、检查点泄露、蒸馏、监督微调和输出改写后仍可通过黑盒文本归因。

- 收录理由：针对推理时水印在模型权重泄露后可被关闭的核心威胁，提出参数内生的模型归属机制并评估多种规避攻击，属于模型知识产权与生成文本溯源安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[WoE Wrote It? Watermarking Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Black-Box Text Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29151)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29151`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Jona te Lintelo — 单位未核实
  - Lichao Wu — 单位未核实
  - Stjepan Picek — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large Language Model (LLM) watermarks provide a mechanism for text provenance, enabling model owners to identify machine-generated content and attribute it to a specific watermarked model. However, current LLM watermarking approaches predominantly rely on inference-time sampler methods and focus their analysis on dense models. Inference-time methods are only effective when the text is explicitly generated via the model owner's controlled API; they fail in a post-compromise scenario. An adversary who steals or leaks the model weights gains complete control over inference and can simply run an unmodified sampler, bypassing the watermark and preventing post-theft attribution. In this work, we introduce Watermarking of Experts (WoE), a novel black-box text provenance method that leverages the unique structural properties of sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. WoE biases the vocabulary of specific experts and shifts the watermark signal embedding away from unenforceable inference wrappers. This approach ensures the watermark remains intrinsic to the model parameters, enabling defenders to attribute text generated by stolen weights, leaked checkpoints, and secondary dense models distilled from the stolen architecture without needing access to the adversary's deployment or weights. We evaluate WoE across eight MoE models, demonstrating successful watermark detection from suspect text, achieving an average true positive rate of 90.1% at a 1% false positive rate, reaching up to 94.9%, while largely preserving general model utility. Furthermore, WoE remains detectable under adversarial supervised fine-tuning, model extraction, and output-level paraphrasing, forcing malicious actors into a trade-off in which weakening the attribution signal requires additional model adaptation or text-rewriting operations, or compromises the utility of the resulting output.
- 中文摘要：大语言模型水印为文本溯源提供了一种机制，使模型所有者能够识别机器生成内容，并将其归因于特定的带水印模型。然而，当前 LLM 水印方法主要依赖推理时采样器方法，且分析集中于稠密模型。推理时方法只有在文本明确通过模型所有者控制的 API 生成时才有效；它们在入侵后的场景中会失效。窃取或泄露模型权重的攻击者可以完全控制推理，只需运行未经修改的采样器即可绕过水印，从而阻止失窃后的归因。在本工作中，我们提出专家水印（Watermarking of Experts，WoE），这是一种利用稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型独特结构属性的新型黑盒文本溯源方法。WoE 对特定专家的词表施加偏置，并将水印信号的嵌入位置从不可强制执行的推理封装器转移出去。该方法确保水印内生于模型参数，使防御者无需访问攻击者的部署或权重，便可归因由被盗权重、泄露检查点以及从被盗架构蒸馏出的次级稠密模型生成的文本。我们在八个 MoE 模型上评估 WoE，证明可以从可疑文本中成功检测水印；在 1% 假阳性率下，平均真阳性率达到 90.1%，最高为 94.9%，同时基本保持模型的通用效用。此外，在对抗性监督微调、模型提取和输出级改写之后，WoE 仍可被检测到，迫使恶意行为者面对权衡：削弱归因信号需要额外的模型适配或文本重写操作，或者会损害所得输出的效用。

### 78. Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation

**内容概述：** ICWBench 发现 14 个前沿模型均无法在三类上下文水印指令上同时保证可检测性与回答质量；作者通过 logits 扰动自蒸馏和验证器奖励强化学习，将两个开源模型的平均水印检出率提升至约 97%。

- 收录理由：系统评估并改进第三方仅靠上下文指令即可调用的 LLM 水印接口，直接关联生成文本溯源能力、可检测性和效用保持。
- 论文/arXiv：[Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29030`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Yepeng Liu — 单位未核实
  - Tianyi Chen — 单位未核实
  - Xuandong Zhao — 单位未核实
  - Dawn Song — 单位未核实
  - Yuheng Bu — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.
- 中文摘要：上下文水印（ICW）在查询前添加一条指令，要求模型在回答中嵌入统计上可检测的信号。因此，它为 LLM 提供了一个第三方无需访问模型内部即可调用的水印接口。其可靠性取决于 LLM 能否在不降低回答质量的情况下遵循指令，但当前 LLM 在这方面的表现尚未得到测量。我们提出 ICWBench，其中包含三个可验证的 ICW 指令家族，并分别从可检测性和回答质量两个维度评分。对 14 个前沿闭源和开源 LLM 的评估发现，没有任何被测模型能在全部三个家族上同时实现两项目标。为此，我们提出一种自包含的两阶段训练方法：它不需要从更强模型蒸馏，不需要人工标注，也不要求模型预先具备遵循 ICW 指令的能力。第一阶段是带 logits 扰动的自蒸馏（SDLP），使用同一个基础 LLM 同时充当教师和学生：与指令等价的解码时 logits 扰动使教师遵循 ICW 指令，学生则被训练为匹配教师的输出分布。第二阶段使用自动验证器作为奖励开展强化学习。将该方法应用于 Qwen3-14B 和 GPT-OSS-20B 后，三个 ICW 指令上的平均 TPR@1%FPR 分别从 0.100 提升至 0.974、从 0.337 提升至 0.968，同时在困惑度评估和 LLM-as-a-Judge 评估下均保持较高回答质量。

### 79. FairReL: Deepfake Detection using Fairness-Aware Representation Learning

**内容概述：** FairReL 分别约束深度伪造检测器的多尺度空间特征和微调残差表征，以组条件小波去相关与子空间均值对齐降低人口群体间误报差异，同时提升未见数据集上的检测 AUC。

- 收录理由：直接处理深度伪造检测对特定人口群体更易将真实人脸误判为伪造的安全公平问题，并同时评估跨数据集检测效果与群体误报差异。
- 论文/arXiv：[FairReL: Deepfake Detection using Fairness-Aware Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28777)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28777`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CV`
- 提交日期：2026-08-28
- arXiv Comments：26 pages, 7 figures. Accepted at BMVC 2026. Code: https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL
- 作者及单位：
  - Xiaoman Lu — 单位未核实
  - Jiaqi Li — 单位未核实
  - Shuntian Zheng — 单位未核实
  - Huiping Chen — 单位未核实
  - Yu Guan — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Although recent deepfake detectors achieve high overall accuracy, their errors remain unevenly distributed across demographic subgroups, with real faces from certain groups more often misclassified as fake. Existing fairness-aware detectors typically regularise the entire feature representation, without identifying or controlling the specific components that drive unfair predictions. Such coarse intervention can over-suppress useful forgery cues while leaving demographic structure in component-specific subspaces. To address this, we identify two subgroup-sensitive components: multi-scale spatial features, which encode local facial and forgery patterns, and fine-tuning-induced residual features, which adapt the backbone to the unfair training distribution. We propose FairReL, a fairness-aware representation-learning framework that targets both components with dedicated demographic supervision. FairReL uses an SVD-decomposed foundation-model backbone to isolate the fine-tuning-induced residual representation, and introduces two complementary losses. Group-Conditional Wavelet Decorrelation (GCWD) suppresses subgroup-imbalanced structure across spatial wavelet sub-bands, while Subspace-Localised Mean Alignment (SLMA) aligns subgroup means within each real/fake class in the residual representation. Experiments on FF++, Celeb-DF, DFD and DFDC show that, against the state-of-the-art fairness-aware detector, FairReL improves unseen-dataset AUC by 3.9% while reducing subgroup FPR disparity by 10.2%. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL .
- 中文摘要：尽管近期深度伪造检测器取得了很高的总体准确率，其错误在不同人口子群体之间仍分布不均，某些群体的真实人脸更常被误判为伪造。现有公平性感知检测器通常正则化整个特征表征，却不识别或控制驱动不公平预测的具体组成部分。这种粗粒度干预可能过度抑制有用的伪造线索，同时在特定组件的子空间中留下人口属性结构。为解决这一问题，我们识别出两个对子群体敏感的组件：编码局部人脸与伪造模式的多尺度空间特征，以及使骨干网络适应不公平训练分布的微调诱导残差特征。我们提出 FairReL，一个使用专门人口属性监督来针对这两个组件的公平性感知表征学习框架。FairReL 使用经 SVD 分解的基础模型骨干网络分离微调诱导的残差表征，并引入两项互补损失。组条件小波去相关（GCWD）抑制空间小波子带中子群体不均衡的结构，子空间局部化均值对齐（SLMA）则在残差表征中对每个真实/伪造类别内部的子群体均值进行对齐。在 FF++、Celeb-DF、DFD 和 DFDC 上的实验表明，相比当前最先进的公平性感知检测器，FairReL 将未见数据集 AUC 提升 3.9%，同时将子群体假阳性率差异降低 10.2%。代码已在 https://github.com/xiaoman89/FairReL 公开。

### 80. Guardrail-Agnostic Societal Bias Evaluation in Large Vision-Language Models

**内容概述：** 该方法通过让 LVLM 完成与图中人物无关的故事、术语解释和考试问答任务，把图像仅作为隐式人口线索，从而绕开拒答干扰；对 20 个模型的测试显示所有模型都会在无关任务中不当使用用户人口信息。

- 收录理由：揭示强安全护栏会使传统偏见基准因拒答而失真，并提出与护栏无关的多模态社会偏见测量方式，直接关联模型公平性与安全评测有效性。
- 论文/arXiv：[Guardrail-Agnostic Societal Bias Evaluation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29590)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29590`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CV`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：Accepted at ECCV 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Yusuke Hirota — 单位未核实
  - Michael Ross Boone — 单位未核实
  - Arun George Zachariah — 单位未核实
  - Jibin Rajan Varghese — 单位未核实
  - Yu-Chiang Frank Wang — 单位未核实
  - Boyi Li — 单位未核实
  - Ryo Hachiuma — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：We propose a societal bias evaluation method for large vision-language models (LVLMs) in the era of strong safety guardrails. Existing benchmarks rely on prompts that ask models to infer attributes of people in images (e.g., "Is this person a CEO or a secretary?"). However, we find that LVLMs with strong guardrails, such as GPT and Claude, often refuse these prompts, making evaluations unreliable. To address this, we change the prior evaluation paradigm by decoupling the task from the depicted person: instead of inferring person's attributes, we use prompts that do not ask about the person (e.g., "Write a fictional story about an imaginary person.") and attach the image as provisional user information to implicitly provide demographic cues, then compare outputs across user demographics. Instantiated across three tasks --- story generation, term explanation, and exam-style QA --- our method avoids refusals even in guardrailed LVLMs, enabling reliable bias measurement. Applying it to 20 recent LVLMs, both open-source and proprietary, we find that all models undesirably use user demographic information in person-irrelevant tasks; for instance, characters in stories are often portrayed as mechanic for male users and nurse for female users. Although still biased, proprietary models like GPT-5 show lower bias than open-source ones. We analyze potential factors behind this gap, discussing continuous model monitoring and improvement as a possible contributor for reducing bias.
- 中文摘要：我们提出一种适用于强安全护栏时代的大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）社会偏见评估方法。现有基准依赖要求模型推断图中人物属性的提示（例如，“这个人是 CEO 还是秘书？”）。然而，我们发现具有强护栏的 LVLM（如 GPT 和 Claude）经常拒绝此类提示，使评估变得不可靠。为此，我们通过将任务与图中人物解耦来改变以往的评估范式：我们不再推断人物属性，而是使用不询问该人物的提示（例如，“写一个关于虚构人物的故事。”），并把图像作为临时用户信息附加，以隐式提供人口属性线索，随后比较不同用户人口属性下的输出。该方法在故事生成、术语解释和考试式问答三项任务中实例化后，即使面对带护栏的 LVLM 也能避免拒答，从而实现可靠的偏见测量。我们将其应用于 20 个近期的开源和闭源 LVLM，发现所有模型都会在与人物无关的任务中不当地使用用户人口信息；例如，故事中的角色常被描述为男性用户对应机械师、女性用户对应护士。GPT-5 等闭源模型虽然仍存在偏见，但程度低于开源模型。我们分析了造成这一差距的潜在因素，并讨论了持续模型监控与改进可能对减少偏见所起的作用。

### 81. ContextBias: Controlled Evaluation of Bias Persistence Under Context Shift in Text-to-Image Models

**内容概述：** ContextBias 以 92 种职业角色、1,656 条语义受控提示和 66,240 张生成图像隔离上下文变化的影响，发现无关场景不仅未抑制角色刻板属性，跨角色属性集中度反而上升。

- 收录理由：通过受控上下文迁移揭示文本生成图像模型中常规无上下文评测无法观察的持续性职业与人口偏见，提供可审计的生成模型公平性基准。
- 论文/arXiv：[ContextBias: Controlled Evaluation of Bias Persistence Under Context Shift in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29847)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29847`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/Sina-Emami/ContextBias)；[Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaghayegh/ContextBias)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CV`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：Accepted to EMNLP 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Shaghayegh Kolli — 单位未核实
  - Sina Emami — 单位未核实
  - Moreno D'Incà — 单位未核实
  - Pouyan Nejadi — 单位未核实
  - Nicu Sebe — 单位未核实
  - Massimiliano Mancini — 单位未核实
  - Jana Diesner — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Text-to-image models learn associations between concepts - in the case of this paper, people's professions, which we refer to as roles - and visual attributes. These associations can underpin many observed forms of stereotypical bias. A key open question in this area is whether these associations are stable or change when visual representations of people in professional roles are placed in different prompted contexts. We introduce ContextBias, a controlled evaluation framework, and ContextBench, a benchmark spanning 92 roles and 1,656 semantically controlled prompts, designed to isolate the effect of contextual variation on role-linked visual representations. Evaluating four state-of-the-art models on 66,240 generated images, we find that placing a role in a semantically unrelated context does not suppress role-linked attributes; instead, cross-role attribute concentration increases (pooled BI $+0.047$). Demographic cues, characteristic garments, and role-specific tools remain highly prevalent across context-free, related, and unrelated conditions, and are robust to semantic prompt reformulation. Scene composition and camera framing show the greatest context-sensitivity. These findings reveal a form of stereotypical persistence that remains largely invisible to context-free evaluations, highlighting the need for controlled contextual variation in bias benchmarking. Code and dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaghayegh/ContextBias , https://github.com/Sina-Emami/ContextBias
- 中文摘要：文本生成图像模型会学习概念与视觉属性之间的关联；在本文中，这些概念是人们的职业，我们称其为角色。这些关联可能构成许多已观察到的刻板偏见形式的基础。该领域一个关键的开放问题是：当职业角色人物的视觉表征被置于不同提示上下文时，这些关联会保持稳定还是发生变化。我们提出受控评估框架 ContextBias，以及覆盖 92 种角色和 1,656 条语义受控提示的基准 ContextBench，旨在隔离上下文变化对角色关联视觉表征的影响。我们在 66,240 张生成图像上评估四个当前最先进模型，发现将角色置于语义无关的上下文并不会抑制角色关联属性；相反，跨角色属性集中度有所增加（合并 BI +0.047）。人口属性线索、标志性服装和角色特有工具在无上下文、相关上下文和无关上下文条件下均高度常见，并且对提示的语义改写具有鲁棒性。场景构图和相机取景表现出最高的上下文敏感性。这些发现揭示了一种在无上下文评估中大多不可见的刻板印象持续现象，凸显偏见基准测试需要引入受控的上下文变化。代码与数据集：https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaghayegh/ContextBias，https://github.com/Sina-Emami/ContextBias。

### 82. MMMMM: A Unified Taxonomy for Investigating the Mechanisms of Multilingual MultiModal Misinformation

**内容概述：** MMMMM 从七种语言的 Twitter/X 真实错误信息中归纳多语言多模态错误信息分类体系，并用 VLM 多阶段标注和人工验证将其操作化，揭示不同主题所采用的图文欺骗机制并不相同。

- 收录理由：以真实社交媒体数据刻画多语言多模态错误信息的组合与欺骗机制，为有针对性的检测和缓解提供分类基础，直接关联生成内容与信息完整性安全。
- 论文/arXiv：[MMMMM: A Unified Taxonomy for Investigating the Mechanisms of Multilingual MultiModal Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29681)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29681`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CY`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Nadav Borenstein — 单位未核实
  - Greta Warren — 单位未核实
  - Desmond Elliott — 单位未核实
  - Isabelle Augenstein — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Multimodal misinformation on social media is highly prevalent, potent, and harmful, yet difficult to detect and counter, and still poorly understood compared to its text-only counterpart. Research on the properties and deceptive strategies of multimodal misinformation is hindered by a lack of taxonomies grounded in real-world contexts and by the limitations of current multimodal machine learning models, which prevent the automation of annotation and analysis at scale. We address these shortcomings in three steps. First, we collect a large-scale, high-quality dataset of real-world misinformation instances from Twitter/X in seven languages. Second, we develop a novel, comprehensive taxonomy of multimodal misinformation grounded in an in-depth qualitative analysis of the data and prior theoretical work. Finally, we operationalise the taxonomy through an automated multi-step annotation pipeline using a Vision-Language Model (VLM), and perform human-validation. Our novel approach leads to previously undocumented insights about how social media users combine images with text to spread misinformation in the wild, e.g., that AI-generated content is particularly prevalent in technology and science, while vaccination misinformation disproportionately utilises images from news outlets to assert credibility. Our method and findings provide guidance for targeted approaches for detecting multimodal misinformation, and suggest that mitigation efforts should be developed and applied strategically rather than uniformly.
- 中文摘要：社交媒体上的多模态错误信息十分普遍、影响力强且危害严重，却难以检测和应对；与纯文本错误信息相比，人们对其理解仍然不足。由于缺少扎根于现实情境的分类体系，同时当前多模态机器学习模型存在局限，无法大规模自动完成标注与分析，因此对多模态错误信息属性和欺骗策略的研究受到阻碍。我们分三步弥补这些不足。首先，我们收集了一个大规模、高质量的现实错误信息数据集，其中包含 Twitter/X 上七种语言的实例。其次，我们基于对数据的深入定性分析和既有理论工作，建立了一个新颖而全面的多模态错误信息分类体系。最后，我们使用视觉语言模型（VLM）构建自动化多阶段标注流水线，使该分类体系可实际应用，并进行人工验证。我们的新方法带来了此前未被记录的见解，说明社交媒体用户在真实环境中如何结合图像与文本传播错误信息；例如，AI 生成内容在技术和科学主题中尤其常见，而疫苗接种错误信息则不成比例地使用新闻媒体图像来声称可信度。我们的方法和发现为有针对性地检测多模态错误信息提供指导，并表明缓解措施应以策略性的方式开发和应用，而非一概而论。

### 83. Memory-First Fact-Checking: A Knowledge-Graph-Grounded Multi-Agent System for Misinformation Detection

**内容概述：** 该事实核查框架先以知识图谱语义记忆检索和 NLI 判断声明，证据不足时再调用支持、反驳和裁决 Agent 检索可信网页，并把验证信息回写为结构化三元组以持续扩展记忆。

- 收录理由：围绕错误信息检测构建带证据门控、对抗式多 Agent 审议和可追溯记忆更新的完整机制，直接涉及 Agent 证据完整性与可解释事实核查。
- 论文/arXiv：[Memory-First Fact-Checking: A Knowledge-Graph-Grounded Multi-Agent System for Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29617)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29617`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`、`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Amelia Petrenciuc — 单位未核实
  - Alexandru Lecu — 单位未核实
  - Adrian Groza — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：This paper introduces a hybrid fact-checking framework that integrates Knowledge Graph-based semantic memory with adversarial multi-agent reasoning for explainable misinformation detection. The proposed system follows a memory-first, web-fallback architecture, in which input claims are initially evaluated against a dual-index Knowledge Graph through Sentence-BERT-based semantic retrieval and Natural Language Inference. When the evidence retrieved from the graph is insufficient to support a reliable decision, the framework collects information from trusted web sources and assesses it using an adversarial tribunal composed of support, contradiction, and judging agents. A graph-aware confidence mechanism combines semantic similarity, NLI confidence, and structural graph evidence to determine whether internal knowledge is sufficient, thereby reducing unnecessary web retrieval. Following verification, validated information is transformed into structured triples and incorporated into the Knowledge Graph, supporting the incremental expansion of the system's semantic memory. Experimental evaluation on a curated COVID-19 misinformation benchmark demonstrates that the proposed framework achieves an accuracy of 97.4\% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 92.6% on resolved claims, outperforming a Llama~3.3~70B baseline, which obtains an accuracy of 87.7% and a macro-averaged F1-score of 86.3%.
- 中文摘要：本文提出一个混合事实核查框架，将基于知识图谱的语义记忆与对抗式多 Agent 推理相结合，用于可解释的错误信息检测。该系统采用“记忆优先、网页回退”的架构：首先通过基于 Sentence-BERT 的语义检索和自然语言推断，在双索引知识图谱上评估输入声明。当从图中检索到的证据不足以支持可靠决策时，框架会从可信网页来源收集信息，并使用由支持、反驳和裁决 Agent 组成的对抗式审议庭进行评估。图感知置信机制综合语义相似度、NLI 置信度和图结构证据，以判断内部知识是否充足，从而减少不必要的网页检索。验证完成后，已验证信息会被转换为结构化三元组并纳入知识图谱，以支持系统语义记忆的增量扩展。在一个精选的 COVID-19 错误信息基准上进行实验评估，结果表明，该框架在已解决声明上取得 97.4% 的准确率和 92.6% 的宏平均 F1，优于准确率为 87.7%、宏平均 F1 为 86.3% 的 Llama 3.3 70B 基线。

### 84. An Explainable Coherence Score for Detecting Temporal Inconsistencies in Political News

**内容概述：** 时间一致性分数通过时间事实抽取、时间知识图谱、内外部层级验证和解释生成，检测文风正常但日期、任期或因果顺序错误的政治新闻，并在注入时间错误的基准上达到 0.909 精确率。

- 收录理由：针对绕过文体型假新闻检测器的时间性政治错误信息，提供来源可追溯、错误类型可解释且面向人工复核的检测机制。
- 论文/arXiv：[An Explainable Coherence Score for Detecting Temporal Inconsistencies in Political News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29175)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29175`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.AI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Marius Nicusor Pantea — 单位未核实
  - Adrian Groza — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Temporal inconsistencies, such as mandates attributed outside their real interval, events presented as past before they occurred, or inverted causal sequences, are a form of political disinformation that evades style-based fake news detectors: a well-written article with a single wrong date carries no lexical signal of falsehood. This paper introduces the Temporal Coherence Score (TCS), a continuous, intrinsically interpretable metric that quantifies the temporal coherence of a news article, computed by a four-stage pipeline: extraction of temporal facts, construction of a temporal knowledge graph, hierarchical verification against internal consistency rules and external reference sources, and score aggregation with automatically generated explanations. Verification combines eight internal checkers derived from Allen's interval algebra with a five-level external hierarchy ranging from a locally stored reference knowledge base of 1{,}256 curated political facts to live Wikidata SPARQL queries. On a benchmark of 100 political news articles with injected temporal errors, the system reaches a precision of 0.909 at the selected operating threshold, with a single residual false positive, a profile deliberately tuned for human-in-the-loop fact-checking assistance, where false alarms are costlier than missed detections. Unlike lexical baselines that output only a binary label, every flagged article is accompanied by the inconsistency type, the entities involved, and the reference source that contradicts the claim.
- 中文摘要：时间不一致包括把任期归于其真实时间区间之外、在事件发生前就将其描述为过去事件，或颠倒因果顺序；这是一种能够躲避基于文体的假新闻检测器的政治虚假信息，因为一篇写作良好但只有一个错误日期的文章不会在词汇层面显现虚假信号。本文提出时间一致性分数（TCS），这是一项连续且内在可解释的指标，用于量化新闻文章的时间一致性。它由四阶段流水线计算：抽取时间事实、构建时间知识图谱、依据内部一致性规则和外部参考来源开展分层验证，以及汇总分数并自动生成解释。验证过程将八个源自 Allen 区间代数的内部检查器，与五级外部层次结构相结合；后者从本地存储、包含 1,256 条精选政治事实的参考知识库一直延伸到实时 Wikidata SPARQL 查询。在一个由 100 篇注入时间错误的政治新闻文章组成的基准上，该系统在所选运行阈值下达到 0.909 的精确率，仅剩一个假阳性。该配置特意面向人在回路的事实核查辅助，因为在此场景中误报的代价高于漏检。与只输出二元标签的词汇基线不同，每篇被标记文章都会附带不一致类型、涉及的实体，以及与该声明矛盾的参考来源。

### 85. One AI Signal, Many Human Judgments: A Bayesian Cascade Analysis of AI-based Credibility Indicators in Online Information Spread

**内容概述：** 该研究把 AI 可信度指示器作为共享公共信号扩展贝叶斯级联模型，显示群体一致可能只是重复依赖同一 AI，过度信任弱 AI 会固化错误，而向不同用户提供多样化 AI 信号可保留群体信息量。

- 收录理由：明确建模 AI 错误信息提示在人际传播中的级联放大与纠错阻断风险，并以人类实验校准干预设计，属于有技术机制的风险沟通与 Human-AI oversight。
- 论文/arXiv：[One AI Signal, Many Human Judgments: A Bayesian Cascade Analysis of AI-based Credibility Indicators in Online Information Spread](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30311)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30311`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.HC`、`cs.AI`、`cs.SI`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：22 pages, 20 figures. Accepted at HCOMP 2026 (2026 ACM Conference on Human-AI Complementarity and Alignment). Supplementary material included as appendices
- 作者及单位：
  - Zhuoran Lu — 单位未核实
  - Weilong Wang — 单位未核实
  - Yangyang Yu — 单位未核实
  - Xinru Wang — 单位未核实
  - Zhuoyan Li — 单位未核实
  - Zhiwei Liu — 单位未核实
  - Sophia Ananiadou — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Social media platforms increasingly use AI-based credibility indicators to help users judge misinformation. Unlike individual human-AI decision-making, these indicators are embedded in information spread: users see both an AI prediction and earlier judgments shaped by the same AI, and their own judgments may then enter the public history. Yet how to analytically characterize this process remains under-explored. We therefore introduce a social-learning lens for this setting by extending the classical Bayesian cascade model with the AI indicator as a shared public signal. The resulting Gateway condition compares the evidence from the AI prediction with users' private impressions. Through this view, we show that AI changes what public history means. Crowd agreement may reflect accumulated independent human evidence, or repeated dependence on the same AI prediction. This creates a preservation-correction trade-off: stronger reliance on AI can preserve correct predictions, but can also lock in incorrect ones by blocking corrective private impressions. We calibrate the model using human-subject data on news veracity judgments. Although the AI outperforms human users, the average user weights it below her own impression but above several peer judgments, while individual users vary from discounting the AI to relying on it enough to cascade. Simulations show that over-reliance on a weak AI is especially harmful, and that diversifying AI signals across users can better keep the crowd informative. We conclude with implications for understanding human-AI interaction in information spread and designing misinformation interventions.
- 中文摘要：社交媒体平台越来越多地使用基于 AI 的可信度指示器帮助用户判断错误信息。与个体的人机决策不同，这些指示器嵌入信息传播过程：用户既会看到 AI 预测，也会看到受同一 AI 影响的先前判断，而他们自己的判断随后也可能进入公共历史记录。然而，如何从分析角度刻画这一过程仍缺乏研究。因此，我们通过将 AI 指示器作为共享公共信号扩展经典贝叶斯级联模型，为这一场景引入社会学习视角。由此得到的“网关条件”比较 AI 预测提供的证据与用户的私人印象。在这一视角下，我们表明 AI 改变了公共历史的含义。群体一致既可能反映累积的独立人类证据，也可能反映对同一 AI 预测的反复依赖。这产生一种“保持—纠正”权衡：更强地依赖 AI 可以保持正确预测，但也可能通过阻断具有纠正作用的私人印象而锁定错误预测。我们使用关于新闻真实性判断的人类受试者数据校准模型。尽管 AI 表现优于人类用户，平均用户赋予 AI 的权重仍低于自己的印象、但高于数名同伴的判断；个体用户则从低估 AI 到依赖程度足以触发级联不等。模拟表明，过度依赖较弱 AI 尤其有害，而在用户之间提供多样化 AI 信号能够更好地保持群体的信息量。最后，我们讨论这些结果对于理解信息传播中的人机交互和设计错误信息干预措施的意义。

### 86. Benchmark Contamination: A Taxonomy Organized by Defeated Mitigation

**内容概述：** 该研究按会击败何种缓解措施将基准污染划分为直接、衍生、时间、分布和评测中获得五类，并提供四字段披露协议与预注册可靠性测量，结果显示现有文档无一覆盖全部五类。

- 收录理由：直接处理模型能力分数与数据泄漏在排行榜中不可区分的评测有效性威胁，并把运行中获得的污染绑定到具体分数进行披露，属于 AI 安全评测完整性研究。
- 论文/arXiv：[Benchmark Contamination: A Taxonomy Organized by Defeated Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29463)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29463`
- 代码：[Code and artifacts](https://github.com/Jangulo7/contamination-disclosure-paper)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.AI`、`cs.CL`、`cs.LG`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：18 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables. Specification, audit instrument, pre-registration and analysis code: https://github.com/Jangulo7/contamination-disclosure-paper
- 作者及单位：
  - Johanna Angulo — 单位未核实
  - Víctor Yeste — 单位未核实
  - Hector Espinos-Morato — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：A benchmark score is a joint property of the model, the evaluation harness, the elicitation budget, the sampled population, and contamination status. Leaderboards publish the model and the score, so capability and leakage stay observationally equivalent. Existing taxonomies classify contamination for automated detection, not the question a reporter faces at publication: given the mitigations already applied, which validity threats remain open? We introduce a taxonomy organized by the mitigation each type defeats -- direct, derivative, temporal, distributional, and acquired -- spanning training-time and evaluation-time leakage. Holding out a private test set closes the first alone. The fifth is acquired during the evaluation itself; because it is a property of one run, it must be recorded with the reported score rather than with the benchmark release. We operationalize it as a four-field disclosure protocol in which "unknown" is a valid entry, released under CC BY 4.0 with a JSON Schema, a validator, and worked examples. Two coders external to the design team applied a pre-registered instrument to 41 documents. Per-variable linear-weighted $κ$ runs from 0.00 to 0.35 (median 0.21) over 29 main-pass documents against a single-coder test-retest ceiling of 0.84, collapsing under the class skew the registration anticipated; pooling raises it to 0.46 through chance correction rather than better agreement. Two variables fall below the prevalence-robust threshold registered in advance: strata reporting and the acquired type introduced here. Disagreement concentrates on when a variable applies rather than on what a document states. Elicitation budgets are reported in 13% of documents, and no document addresses all five types. The contribution is the taxonomy, the score-side artifact that follows from it, and a pre-registered measurement of instrument reliability and current disclosure.
- 中文摘要：一项基准分数是模型、评估工具链、引导预算、抽样总体和污染状态共同作用的结果。排行榜只发布模型和分数，因此能力与泄漏在观察上保持等价。现有分类体系为自动化检测而划分污染，却没有回答报告者在发表时面临的问题：在已经采取现有缓解措施之后，哪些有效性威胁仍未消除？我们提出一个按每类污染能够击败的缓解措施组织的分类体系，包括直接型、衍生型、时间型、分布型和获得型，覆盖训练时与评估时泄漏。保留私有测试集只能消除第一类。第五类是在评估本身进行期间获得的；由于它是某一次运行的属性，必须与所报告分数一同记录，而不是只随基准发布记录。我们将其操作化为一个四字段披露协议，其中“未知”是合法取值；该协议以 CC BY 4.0 发布，并附有 JSON Schema、验证器和完整示例。两名不属于设计团队的编码员使用一项预注册工具对 41 份文档进行了编码。在 29 份主要分析文档上，各变量的线性加权 κ 值从 0.00 到 0.35 不等，中位数为 0.21，而单编码员重测上限为 0.84；正如预注册所预期的那样，该指标在类别偏斜下失效。合并变量后，κ 提升至 0.46，但这是机会校正所致，而非一致性真正改善。两个变量低于预先注册的稳健于流行率的阈值：分层报告，以及本文引入的获得型污染。分歧主要集中在变量何时适用，而不是文档实际陈述了什么。只有 13% 的文档报告引导预算，没有任何文档处理全部五类污染。本文贡献包括该分类体系、由此产生且绑定分数的产物，以及对工具可靠性和当前披露状况的预注册测量。

### 87. Understanding before verifying: Claim normalization for automated citation verification

**内容概述：** CNCV 在证据检索和引文分类前增加声明规范化，以重写方式分别解决范围不匹配、视角不匹配和命题纠缠，使编码器与生成式 LLM 的宏 F1 平均提升 12% 和 10%。

- 收录理由：针对自动引文核验中的证据完整性瓶颈提出可测技术干预，并验证自动规范化声明检索到的证据可达到人工证据的下游效果，直接关联科研 AI 输出可信度。
- 论文/arXiv：[Understanding before verifying: Claim normalization for automated citation verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30145)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30145`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.IR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Yifan He — 单位未核实
  - Mengjia Wu — 单位未核实
  - Siming Deng — 单位未核实
  - Yi Zhang — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Citation accuracy has been studied for decades because of its importance to research reliability. Content-level citation verification assesses the reliability of scholarly claims. Recent work adopts a two-stage retrieval-classification framework inherited from fact-checking. However, this design overlooks the complexity of the raw citing claim and introduces three issues into the verification system, namely scope mismatch, perspective mismatch, and proposition entanglement. These issues increase the difficulty of retrieval and classification, thereby limiting model performance. Motivated by this gap, we propose claim normalization, which applies three rewriting strategies to the raw citing claim before retrieval and classification, allowing each downstream model to perform a single, well-defined task. Building on this method, we develop Claim-Normalized Citation Verification (CNCV), a new three-stage framework consisting of claim normalization, evidence retrieval with grounding, and citation classification. We evaluate CNCV across 18 classifiers using a factorial experiment on human-annotated citation instances. Compared with the prior two-stage framework, CNCV improves macro F1 by an average of 12% for encoders and 10% for generative LLMs, driven by improved evidence quality, the dominant factor identified in our experiments. Evidence retrieved from automatically normalized claims yields downstream classification performance statistically equivalent to that obtained with manually annotated evidence.
- 中文摘要：由于引文准确性对研究可靠性十分重要，人们已经对其研究数十年。内容层面的引文核验用于评估学术声明的可靠性。近期工作采用了继承自事实核查的两阶段“检索—分类”框架。然而，这种设计忽略原始引用声明的复杂性，并给核验系统带来三个问题：范围不匹配、视角不匹配和命题纠缠。这些问题增加了检索与分类难度，进而限制模型性能。受这一缺口启发，我们提出声明规范化，在检索和分类之前对原始引用声明应用三种重写策略，使每个下游模型都只执行一项定义明确的任务。在该方法基础上，我们开发了声明规范化引文核验（CNCV），这是一个由声明规范化、带依据的证据检索和引文分类组成的新型三阶段框架。我们通过对人工标注引文实例开展析因实验，使用 18 个分类器评估 CNCV。与此前的两阶段框架相比，CNCV 使编码器的宏 F1 平均提高 12%，使生成式 LLM 的宏 F1 平均提高 10%；实验识别出的主导因素是证据质量改善。由自动规范化声明检索到的证据，其下游分类性能与使用人工标注证据所取得的性能在统计上等价。

### 88. Drishti: AI-Led Human-Directed Vulnerability Auditing for 5G Cores

**内容概述：** Drishti 以反模式目录、关键路径分诊、同心式验证和补丁审查组织 AI 主导、人工定向的 5G 核心网漏洞审计，并在两个真实项目中发现预认证崩溃、内存放大及无效补丁问题。

- 收录理由：以真实开源关键基础设施、可达性验证、影响复现和补丁完整性证据评估 AI 辅助源码审计能力，符合 Agent 驱动高后果漏洞发现与验证方向。
- 论文/arXiv：[Drishti: AI-Led Human-Directed Vulnerability Auditing for 5G Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30112)
- arXiv ID：`2608.30112`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`
- 提交日期：2026-08-31
- arXiv Comments：9 pages, 2 figures, 1 table, accepted for publication at ACM CCS workshop on CPSIoTSec 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Sriram Ramachandran — 单位未核实
  - Levente Csikor — 单位未核实
  - Dinil Mon Divakaran — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Candidate generation for open-source vulnerabilities is no longer scarce. AI-assisted code review now produces defect candidates cheaply, and industry programs pair them with expert human triage. The remaining scarcity is validation and impact assessment, and the gap is largest in critical-infrastructure software like 5G cores. Here, validation has four costs: verification, reachability, impact, and fix-completeness. We present Drishti, an AI-led human-directed vulnerability audit framework with four components, one per cost: (i) an anti-pattern catalog for verification, (ii) critical-path triage for reachability, (iii) concentric validation for impact, and (iv) patch-review for fix-completeness. Across audits of Open5GS and free5GC, Drishti produced three findings. The first is a pre-authentication NULL-dereference in the Open5GS NRF multipart parser, fixed upstream with a CVE requested. The second is an ASN.1-PER memory amplification in the free5GC NGAP decoder. A 2-byte input from a rogue gNodeB OOM-kills the AMF in 6.2 seconds. The third is a defective patch on CVE-2025-69248 whose defense-in-depth check is dead code before authentication.
- 中文摘要：开源软件漏洞候选的生成已不再稀缺。AI 辅助代码审查如今能够低成本地产生缺陷候选，业界项目则配合专家人工分诊。当前真正稀缺的是验证与影响评估，这一缺口在 5G 核心网等关键基础设施软件中最为突出。在这里，验证包含四类成本：确认、可达性、影响和修复完整性。我们提出 Drishti，一个 AI 主导、人工定向的漏洞审计框架，其四个组件分别对应一类成本：（i）用于确认的反模式目录；（ii）用于可达性分析的关键路径分诊；（iii）用于影响评估的同心式验证；（iv）用于修复完整性的补丁审查。在对 Open5GS 和 free5GC 的审计中，Drishti 产生了三项发现。第一项是 Open5GS NRF 多部分解析器中的预认证 NULL 解引用问题，上游已修复并已申请 CVE。第二项是 free5GC NGAP 解码器中的 ASN.1-PER 内存放大问题：来自恶意 gNodeB 的 2 字节输入可在 6.2 秒内使 AMF 因内存耗尽而终止。第三项是 CVE-2025-69248 的缺陷补丁，其中用于纵深防御的检查在认证前是死代码。

### 89. Agent-Driven Verification of Memory Safety for liblzma Decoder Components with VST

**内容概述：** 该项目让 AI Agent 为生产级 liblzma 解码器构造 VST/Rocq 证明脚本、由人类建模与审核且由内核检查证明项，完成 27 个函数体证明并在原有 C 代码中发现零输入处理的未定义行为。

- 收录理由：以机器可检查证明、生产级代码规模和真实缺陷发现评估 Agent 驱动的软件安全验证能力，而非仅报告代码生成或静态分类准确率。
- 论文/arXiv：[Agent-Driven Verification of Memory Safety for liblzma Decoder Components with VST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29716)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29716`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码

- arXiv Comments：未提供

  - Prokhor Shlyakhtun — 单位未核实
  - Alexander Gryzlov — 单位未核实
  - Vladimir Kukharenko — 单位未核实

- 英文摘要：We report on the verification of memory safety for decoder components of liblzma, the compression library underlying xz-utils: the LZMA2 state machine, the LZMA1 decoder it controls, the outer decoding path, and the shared sliding-window dictionary. Built with the Verified Software Toolchain (VST), machine-checked body theorems establish memory safety and partial functional correctness. Across 27 completed body proofs, the largest covers lzma decode, whose 338 source lines expand to 1,934 lines of C after preprocessing; its proof comprises 183,268 lines of proof script over 775,768 lines of mechanically extracted goal statements. The verification exposed undefined behavior in raw LZMA1 zero-input handling, where range-decoder macros add zero to a null pointer and subtract two null pointers. Unlike similar work that synthesizes verified code, we verify pre-existing, production-scale C. AI agents complete proof goals and propose refinements; humans write and review models and specifications, and approve semantic changes; the Rocq kernel checks the proof terms. With agents constructing the proof scripts, the main engineering problems lay in translating and modeling production C, building a robust harness for driving Rocq, and providing feedback for proving agents. VST's assertion logic expressed every contract required by the development. We describe the pipeline, coordination mechanisms, and proof-engineering techniques that resolved these frictions.
- 中文摘要：我们报告了对 liblzma 解码器组件进行内存安全验证的工作。liblzma 是 xz-utils 所依赖的压缩库；所验证组件包括 LZMA2 状态机、由其控制的 LZMA1 解码器、外层解码路径，以及共享滑动窗口字典。该验证使用 Verified Software Toolchain（VST）构建，机器检查的函数体定理建立了内存安全性和部分功能正确性。在已完成的 27 个函数体证明中，规模最大的是 lzma_decode：其 338 行源代码经预处理后扩展为 1,934 行 C 代码；相应证明包含 183,268 行证明脚本，面对 775,768 行机械提取的目标陈述。验证暴露了原始 LZMA1 零输入处理中的未定义行为，其中范围解码器宏会给空指针加零，并对两个空指针做减法。不同于合成已验证代码的类似工作，我们验证的是既有的、生产规模的 C 代码。AI Agent 完成证明目标并提出改进建议；人类编写和审核模型与规格，并批准语义变更；Rocq 内核检查证明项。由 Agent 构造证明脚本后，主要工程问题集中在翻译和建模生产级 C 代码、构建驱动 Rocq 的稳健工具链，以及为证明 Agent 提供反馈。VST 的断言逻辑表达了该开发所需的每一项契约。我们描述了克服这些阻碍的流水线、协调机制和证明工程技术。

### 90. POLYFLOW: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Static Cross-Language Information Flow Analysis

**内容概述：** POLYFLOW 以静态控制流限定 LLM 的分析范围，让模型识别跨语言特性造成的隐式信息流，再由符号传播和多模型协商验证抑制上下文限制与幻觉，并发现基线遗漏的真实跨语言漏洞。

- 收录理由：把 LLM 置于静态分析约束和事实核验闭环中执行真实系统的跨语言漏洞发现，明确评估 AI 安全分析能力及其幻觉控制，符合高后果 AI 驱动网络安全方向。
- 论文/arXiv：[POLYFLOW: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Static Cross-Language Information Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29808)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29808`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.PL`、`cs.SE`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：未提供
- 作者及单位：
  - Haoran Yang — 单位未核实
  - Zhixuan Zhong — 单位未核实
  - Jiawei Guo — 单位未核实
  - Haipeng Cai — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Modern software systems are commonly constructed in multiple, interacting programming languages. This construction leads to additional, often stealthy vulnerabilities buried in complex information flow due to language interactions. Existing static analyzers are impeded by the heterogeneous semantics of different languages, whereas dynamic approaches suffer from the limited coverage of (available and/or generated) test inputs. In this paper, we develop PolyFlow, a neural-symbolic framework for statically reasoning about information flow across language boundaries, combining large language models (LLMs) and static analysis synergistically. Governed by the control-flow representation of a given multi-language system, PolyFlow leverages LLMs to identify implicit flow facts due to challenging language features, hence augmenting the base representation and then propagating data flow through the system. It tackles inherent barriers (e.g., token limit and hallucination) of LLMs by putting them under careful guidance (e.g., static-analysis-guided scoping, context management, and fact checking), along with a multi-LLM expert panel for negotiated validation. Our experiments on real-world Python-C and Java-C systems show that PolyFlow is cost-effective and superior to various kinds of state-of-the-art baselines, revealing previously unknown cross-language vulnerabilities that are missed by all the baselines.
- 中文摘要：现代软件系统通常由多种相互交互的编程语言构建。这种构造会因语言交互而在复杂信息流中埋藏额外且往往隐蔽的漏洞。现有静态分析器受到不同语言异构语义的阻碍，而动态方法则受限于现有和/或生成测试输入的覆盖范围。本文开发 PolyFlow，一个用于静态推理跨语言边界信息流的神经符号框架，它协同结合大语言模型（LLM）与静态分析。在给定多语言系统的控制流表示约束下，PolyFlow 利用 LLM 识别由棘手语言特性造成的隐式信息流事实，从而增强基础表示，随后在整个系统中传播数据流。它通过细致引导来应对 LLM 的内在障碍（例如 token 限制和幻觉），包括静态分析引导的范围限定、上下文管理和事实核验，并使用多 LLM 专家组进行协商验证。我们在真实 Python-C 和 Java-C 系统上的实验表明，PolyFlow 具有成本效益且优于多类当前最先进基线，并揭示出所有基线均遗漏的、此前未知的跨语言漏洞。

### 91. A Comprehensive Survey on Linguistic Steganography: Methods, Countermeasures, Evaluation, and Challenges

**内容概述：** 该综述系统整理 148 种语言隐写方法、60 种隐写分析对策、23 项评估指标和 9 个开放挑战，并归纳 LLM 时代从仅提示生成、可证明安全到黑盒访问等五项范式变化。

- 收录理由：系统覆盖 LLM 驱动的自然语言隐蔽信道、检测与对抗措施，可用于评估模型输出承载隐藏载荷和绕过内容审查的双重用途风险。
- 论文/arXiv：[A Comprehensive Survey on Linguistic Steganography: Methods, Countermeasures, Evaluation, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29077)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29077`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CR`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-29
- arXiv Comments：Accepted by EMNLP 2026
- 作者及单位：
  - Ruiyi Yan — 单位未核实
  - Chenhui Chu — 单位未核实
  - Zhongliang Yang — 单位未核实
  - Yugo Murawaki — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Linguistic steganography hides secret messages in natural language text. Large language models (LLMs) have reshaped the field, but a systematic account of how these scattered advances collectively reshape the field in this new era is still missing. We provide one along four axes: 148 steganographic methods, 60 linguistic steganalysis countermeasures, 23 evaluation metrics, and 9 open challenges, each with taxonomies, reviews, and adoption analyses. Cutting across these axes, we identify five specific paradigm shifts in the LLM era: (1) from covertext modification to prompt-only generation, (2) from heuristic to provable security, (3) from white-box symmetric LMs to black-box or asymmetric access, (4) from security-centric designs to joint optimization, and (5) from text-quality concerns to engineering issues. The survey aims to serve as both a reference and a roadmap for practical and responsible linguistic steganography in the LLM era.
- 中文摘要：语言隐写术把秘密消息隐藏在自然语言文本中。大语言模型（LLM）重塑了这一领域，但仍缺少系统性说明，解释这些分散进展如何在新时代共同改变该领域。我们从四个维度提供这样的梳理：148 种隐写方法、60 种语言隐写分析对策、23 项评估指标和 9 个开放挑战，并为每个维度提供分类体系、综述和采用情况分析。贯穿这些维度，我们识别出 LLM 时代的五项具体范式转变：（1）从修改载体文本转向仅使用提示生成；（2）从启发式方法转向可证明安全；（3）从白盒对称语言模型转向黑盒或非对称访问；（4）从以安全为中心的设计转向联合优化；（5）从文本质量问题转向工程问题。本综述旨在同时作为 LLM 时代实用且负责任的语言隐写术参考资料与路线图。

### 92. Evaluating the Capabilities of LLMs for Persuasive Dialogue

**内容概述：** Persuasio 以形式论证理论裁决自由文本辩论的逻辑胜者；在人类与 LLM 的 192 场政治辩论中，模型主导主观说服力排名却在形式裁决中明显更弱，而多 Agent 和 RAG 进一步扩大这一差距。

- 收录理由：对 LLM 说服能力进行主观与形式双轨评测，揭示修辞流畅度会夸大实际论证能力，为操纵风险和高影响能力评估提供关键校准证据。
- 论文/arXiv：[Evaluating the Capabilities of LLMs for Persuasive Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29738)
- arXiv ID：`2608.29738`
- 代码：暂未找到公开代码
- arXiv 分类：`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-30
- arXiv Comments：19 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables
- 作者及单位：
  - Jordan Robinson — 单位未核实
  - Angus R. Williams — 单位未核实
  - Katie Atkinson — 单位未核实
  - Anthony G. Cohn — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Large language models (LLMs) can generate apparently highly persuasive text, but does sounding persuasive mean arguing well? We introduce \textsc{Persuasio}, a multi-agent dialogue platform grounded in a formal argumentation-based theory of persuasion dialogues that adjudicates logical winners during free-text debates. Using this system, we generated 192 debates on a UK political topic between humans and LLMs, and evaluated 22 interlocutors through both automated adjudication and 9,702 crowdsourced pairwise judgements across 1{,}386 annotation instances. We observed a consistent decoupling between subjective and formal persuasiveness: LLMs dominated the subjective ranking yet performed substantially worse under argumentation-theoretic adjudication, where humans remained competitive. Multi-agent and retrieval-augmented variants further widened this divergence. These findings reveal a systematic gap between rhetorical fluency and formal argumentative strength in LLM-based persuasive dialogues.
- 中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）能够生成看似极具说服力的文本，但听起来有说服力是否意味着论证得好？我们提出 Persuasio，一个以形式化的论证型说服对话理论为基础的多 Agent 对话平台，可在自由文本辩论中裁定逻辑胜者。我们使用该系统生成了 192 场围绕英国政治议题、在人类与 LLM 之间开展的辩论，并通过自动裁决以及 1,386 个标注实例中的 9,702 次众包成对判断，对 22 名对话参与者进行了评估。我们观察到主观说服力与形式说服力持续解耦：LLM 主导主观排名，却在基于论证理论的裁决中表现明显更差，而人类仍具有竞争力。多 Agent 和检索增强变体进一步扩大了这种分歧。这些发现揭示 LLM 说服性对话中的修辞流畅度与形式论证强度之间存在系统性差距。

### 93. The Halt Vector: Internalizing a Causal Steering Intervention for Efficient Reasoning

**内容概述：** 该研究识别 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 第 18 层控制思考时长的 halt vector，并以保持非目标激活维度的权重内化方法，在五个未见基准上以准确率不变为条件减少约四分之一推理，同时修复随难度加剧的非终止问题。

- 收录理由：给出推理模型过度生成与非终止失效的因果表示机制和权重级干预，直接关联模型 DoS、成本与可用性安全，并明确不声称优于调优后的长度惩罚或提前退出。
- 论文/arXiv：[The Halt Vector: Internalizing a Causal Steering Intervention for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28859)
- arXiv ID：`2608.28859`
- 代码：[Code](https://github.com/dylanjayabahu/halt-vector)
- arXiv 分类：`cs.LG`、`cs.AI`、`cs.CL`
- 提交日期：2026-08-28
- arXiv Comments：23 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at the 2nd Workshop on Efficient Reasoning, COLM 2026 (non-archival). Code: https://github.com/dylanjayabahu/halt-vector
- 作者及单位：
  - Dylan Jayabahu — 单位未核实
  - Tinuade Adeleke — 单位未核实
- 英文摘要：Reasoning models do not stop when they know the answer. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B the chain of thought runs about twice as long as the model's own answer probability takes to settle, and how much of that excess is removable varies from problem to problem, so a global length penalty cannot take it out. We take it out by internalizing a causal interpretability finding into the weights. The mechanism is a halt vector: a difference-of-means direction at layer 18 of this model whose steering strength controls how long it thinks, while a replicated value axis does nothing. Installing that intervention in the weights is harder than it looks. Maximizing the scalar projection onto the direction corrupts the off-axis dimensions a frozen downstream reader depends on, and generation gets longer instead of shorter; what works is reconstructing the whole steered activation with those dimensions pinned to their natural values. Fit from 24 problems and no reinforcement learning, the halt removes about a quarter of the thinking at held accuracy across five unseen benchmarks, and the cut tracks each problem's own removable slack at 0.70. It also closes a non-termination pathology that grows with difficulty and that a decoding-time confidence hook makes worse. We do not claim to beat a well-tuned length penalty or decoding-time early exit on the raw trade-off; the contribution is how the halt is obtained.
- 中文摘要：推理模型即使知道答案也不会停止。在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 上，思维链持续时间约为模型自身答案概率趋于稳定所需时间的两倍，而且可移除的冗余量因问题而异，因此全局长度惩罚无法将其去除。我们通过把一个因果可解释性发现内化到权重中来消除这部分冗余。其机制是 halt vector：这是该模型第 18 层的一个均值差方向，施加在该方向上的引导强度可以控制模型思考时长，而一个复现得到的价值轴则不起作用。把该干预安装到权重中比看起来更难。最大化该方向上的标量投影会破坏冻结的下游读取器所依赖的轴外维度，导致生成反而变长；有效的做法是重构完整的引导后激活，同时将这些维度固定在其自然值上。该 halt 仅使用 24 个问题拟合，且不使用强化学习；在五个未见基准上，它能在保持准确率的情况下减少约四分之一思考量，并且减少幅度与每个问题自身可移除冗余之间的相关系数达到 0.70。它还消除了一个随难度增加而加剧的非终止病理，而解码时置信度钩子会使该问题恶化。我们不声称在原始权衡上优于经过良好调优的长度惩罚或解码时提前退出；本文贡献在于 halt 的获得方式。
